================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================

# 观尘 GuanChen v2

> 传统文化智能推演与知识服务 AI Agent Application  
> 建设基线：**GuanChen v2 冻结规划 v1.0**  
> 当前状态：**Stage 8 — Memory + Persistence 已通过验收（Stage 0–8 完成）**

## 1. Overview

观尘 GuanChen v2 是一个面向中文传统文化场景的垂直 AI Agent 应用。

项目通过：

- 确定性领域计算；
- 可追溯知识检索；
- 多轮上下文维护；
- 可恢复工作流；
- 严格的 Agent / Tool / Retrieval / Memory 边界；

为用户提供传统文化知识解释、结构化推演与现实反思服务。

项目同时服务于第二个目标：系统实践 AI Agent / LLM Application Engineering，并形成一个能够用于中国大陆 AI Agent / LLM 应用开发岗位求职展示的旗舰项目。

本项目不是“AI 算命大全”，也不以堆叠更多玄学 Agent 为目标。

---

## 2. Product Scope

GuanChen v2.0 只包含四个核心领域 Agent：

| Agent | 核心职责 | 主要工程模式 |
|---|---|---|
| Bazi Analysis Agent | 基于确定性排盘事实与知识依据解释个人八字问题 | Tool-centric |
| Dream Interpretation Agent | 在多轮对话中维护 DreamRecord，并提供传统文化 + 非诊断性情境解释 | Context-centric |
| Liuyao Decision Reflection Agent | 澄清问题、用户确认后起卦，并生成现实决策反思 | Workflow-centric / HITL |
| Traditional Culture Knowledge Agent | 基于受控知识库回答传统文化知识问题并提供来源 | Retrieval-centric |

v2.0 不增加第五业务 Agent。

---

## 3. Engineering Principles

1. **确定性计算不交给 LLM。**  
   八字排盘、六爻卦象等由代码 / 成熟库完成；LLM 负责理解、选择、解释和整合。

2. **优先复用成熟能力。**  
   能稳定调用成熟库 / 官方 API 的能力，不重复造轮子。

3. **Agent 只用于真正需要决策、语言理解和编排的地方。**  
   确定性能力优先设计为 Tool / Service。

4. **Retrieval 是共享基础设施。**  
   不为每个 Agent 单独建设一套 RAG。

5. **Memory 按领域选择，而不是全量注入。**  
   不把用户全部历史无脑塞给所有 Agent。

6. **LangGraph 在真实 Workflow 需求出现后再引入。**  
   不为了统一技术栈而提前 Graph 化。

7. **评价和可观测性是最终产品能力的一部分。**  
   项目不以“感觉能用”作为质量标准。

8. **阶段内不扩项。**  
   新想法进入 v2.1 Backlog；除非阻塞正确性、安全性或可运行性。

---

## 4. Target Architecture

> 下图描述的是 **Stage 12 完成后的目标架构**。  
> 它不代表当前已经实现这些组件。

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    U[Web Frontend] -->|HTTP / SSE| API[FastAPI Gateway + Safety]

    API --> CG[ConversationGraph / Router]

    CG --> B[Bazi Agent]
    CG --> D[Dream Agent]
    CG --> L[Liuyao Agent]
    CG --> K[Knowledge Agent]

    B --> BT[calculate_bazi Tool]
    BT --> LP[lunar_python]

    D --> DC[Dream Context / Memory]

    L --> LG[LangGraph HITL]
    LG --> HT[cast_hexagram Tool]
    HT --> IP[ichingpy]

    B --> R[Shared Retrieval Service]
    D --> R
    L --> R
    K --> R

    R --> DR[Dense: BGE]
    R --> SR[Sparse: BM25S]
    DR --> F[RRF + Optional Reranker]
    SR --> F
    F --> PG[(PostgreSQL + pgvector)]

    CG --> MEM[(PostgreSQL: profiles / conversations / memory)]
    CG --> OBS[Evaluation / Observability]

    MCP[MCP Server] --> BT
    MCP --> R
    MCP --> HT

    REDIS[(Redis: rate limit / idempotency / short TTL cache)]
    API --> REDIS
```

详细说明见 [`docs/architecture.md`](docs/architecture.md)。

---

## 5. Unified Request Lifecycle

目标请求生命周期：

```text
User Request
→ Request Context Initialization
→ Safety Pre-check
→ Load Minimal Routing Context
→ Route
→ Requirement Check
→ Load Selected Domain Context
→ Execute Selected Agent(s)
→ Tool / Retrieval As Needed
→ Response + Citation
→ Explicit / Confirmed Memory Update
→ Complete
```

以下能力属于横切关注点，而不是单一业务节点：

- Trace / Observability
- Persistence / Checkpoint
- Error Handling
- Streaming / SSE

详细说明见 [`docs/request_lifecycle.md`](docs/request_lifecycle.md)。

---

## 6. Roadmap

| Stage | Theme |
|---:|---|
| 0 | Product / Domain / Architecture Freeze |
| 1 | Engineering Skeleton + LLM Foundation |
| 2 | Bazi Deterministic Tool |
| 3 | Bazi Agent Vertical Slice |
| 4 | Shared RAG + Knowledge Agent |
| 5 | Dream Agent + Context Engineering |
| 6 | Liuyao Agent + LangGraph HITL |
| 7 | Router + Multi-Agent Orchestration |
| 8 | Memory + Persistence |
| 9 | MCP Standardization |
| 10 | Evaluation + Observability |
| 11 | Safety + FastAPI + SSE + Frontend Integration |
| 12 | Production Deployment + Interview Delivery |

详细路线见 [`docs/roadmap.md`](docs/roadmap.md)。

---

## 7. Current Implementation Status

### 已完成：Stage 0 – Stage 8

| Stage | 主题 | 状态 |
|---:|---|---|
| 0 | Product / Domain / Architecture Freeze | 完成 |
| 1 | Engineering Skeleton + LLM Foundation | 完成 |
| 2 | Bazi Deterministic Tool | 完成 |
| 3 | Bazi Agent Vertical Slice | 完成 |
| 4 | Shared RAG + Knowledge Agent | 完成 |
| 5 | Dream Agent + Context Engineering | 完成 |
| 6 | Liuyao Agent + LangGraph HITL | 完成 |
| 7 | Router + Multi-Agent Orchestration | 完成 |
| 8 | Memory + Persistence | 完成（本阶段） |

### Stage 8 交付摘要

- **Postgres 域表**：`user_profile` / `birth_profile` / `conversation` / `message` / `dream_record` / `decision_record` + `conversation_states`（SQLAlchemy async、FK 级联、索引、`created_at` / `updated_at`）。
- **SQLAlchemy async repository 层**：UserProfile / BirthProfile（upsert = 用户更正覆盖语义）/ Conversation（幂等 append）/ DreamRecord / DecisionRecord。
- **LangGraph PostgreSQL 持久 checkpointer**：官方 `AsyncPostgresSaver` + `AsyncConnectionPool` 封装（`memory/checkpointer.py`），六爻 HITL 线程跨进程可恢复。
- **Memory Selector 领域隔离**：BAZI 只注入 `BIRTH_PROFILE`；LIUYAO 在待确认状态注入决策问题种子；DREAM / KNOWLEDGE / CHITCHAT 不注入任何历史记忆——不把全历史无脑塞给模型。
- **长期记忆写回**：八字执行成功后持久化出生资料（重启免重问）；六爻每次决策持久化 `DecisionRecord`（含 thread_id，支持 `resume` 接力）。
- **三类必测全绿**：持久化 / 恢复 / 隔离（见 `tests/` 与 `docs/stage_status.md`）。

### 验证状态

```text
默认套件：358 passed, 43 deselected
集成套件（-m integration，含 Stage 8 Memory）：42 passed
静态检查：ruff check src tests scripts 通过
Stage 7 路由 Eval（full-chain）：黄金集 65 条，primary / secondary accuracy 均 100%
Stage 4 Retrieval Eval：45 条，RRF Recall@5=0.9111；reranker 未通过 Gate，生产保持关闭
Stage 4 Knowledge Agent：10/10 真实回答 citation lineage 通过
Stage 8 应用验收：32 个真实入口场景通过
```

### 尚未实现（目标架构，后续 Stage）

- MCP Server（Stage 9）
- Evaluation + Observability runtime（Stage 10）
- FastAPI / SSE / 前端集成（Stage 11）
- Redis、生产部署（Stage 12）

## 9. Stage Discipline

每一个 Stage：

1. 开始前确认目标、固定任务、新知识、调用 / 自研边界、测试范围、非目标和完成标准；
2. Stage 内只完成当前 Stage 的冻结任务；
3. Gate 未通过，不进入下一 Stage；
4. 新想法进入 v2.1 Backlog；
5. 只有正确性、安全性或不可运行的阻塞问题可以触发最小方案变更，并记录 ADR。

当前 Stage 9（MCP Standardization）尚未开始；Stage 8 Gate 已通过，可进入 Stage 9。


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\bazi_cli.py
================================================================================

import asyncio

from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.exceptions import BaziAgentError
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziBirthDataPatch,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    MissingField,
    NeedUserInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import BaziAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.session import BaziTaskSession
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings

FIELD_PROMPTS = {
    MissingField.YEAR: "请输入出生年份 (例 1998) > ",
    MissingField.MONTH: "请输入出生月份 (1-12) > ",
    MissingField.DAY: "请输入出生日期 (1-31) > ",
    MissingField.HOUR: "请输入出生小时 (0-23) > ",
    MissingField.MINUTE: "请输入出生分钟 (0-59) > ",
    MissingField.GENDER: "请输入性别 (male/female) > ",
}


async def run_cli():
    print("========================================")
    print("        GuanChen Bazi CLI")
    print("========================================")

    question = input("\n请描述你想咨询的问题。出生年月日等资料稍后会单独收集。\n>")
    try:
        initial_input = BaziAgentInput(question=question)
    except ValidationError:
        print("初始输入无效")
        return

    print("\n 系统初始化与排盘引擎加载中...")
    settings = Settings()
    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(settings=settings)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial_input=initial_input, runtime=runtime)

    result = await session.evaluate()

    while True:
        if isinstance(result, BaziAgentResult):
            print("\n========================================")
            print(" 排盘与解读已完成")
            print("========================================")
            print(f"【原问题】: {result.question}")
            print(f"【日主等权威事实】: {result.chart.day_master.value}")
            print(f"\n【大模型结构化命理解释】\n{result.answer}\n")
            break

        elif isinstance(result, NeedUserInput):
            print(f"\n [系统提示] 出生资料不完整，请继续补充 (针对问题: '{result.original_question}')")
            patch_kwargs = {}
            for field in result.missing_fields:
                prompt_str = FIELD_PROMPTS.get(field, f"请输入 {field}: ")
                val = input(prompt_str).strip()
                if val:
                    patch_kwargs[field] = val

            try:
                patch = BaziBirthDataPatch(**patch_kwargs)
                result = await session.supplement(patch=patch)
            except ValidationError:
                print("字段输入校验失败，请检查并重新输入")
                continue

        elif isinstance(result, InvalidBaziInput):
            print(f"\n [系统提示] {result.reason}")
            print("请修正出生资料（输入新值覆盖，回车跳过）。")

            patch_kwargs = {}
            patch_kwargs["year"] = input("修改年份 > ").strip() or None
            patch_kwargs["month"] = input("修改月份 > ").strip() or None
            patch_kwargs["day"] = input("修改日期 > ").strip() or None

            patch_kwargs = {k: v for k, v in patch_kwargs.items() if v is not None}

            try:
                patch = BaziBirthDataPatch(**patch_kwargs)
                result = await session.supplement(patch=patch)
            except ValidationError:
                print("字段输入校验失败")
                continue


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        asyncio.run(run_cli())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n\n 会话已终止。")
    except BaziAgentError:
        print("\n\n Agent错误")


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\chat_cli.py
================================================================================

"""Unified local conversation entry. /birth JSON supplies explicit fields; /new starts a new task."""

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import sys

from guanchen_v2.application.factory import open_application
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings


async def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--session")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    session_id = args.session
    async with open_application(Settings()) as app:
        print('输入 /birth {"year":1992,...} 补充资料，/new 问题切换任务，/quit 退出。')
        while True:
            query = input("> ").strip()
            if query == "/quit":
                return
            if not query:
                continue
            kwargs = {}
            if query.startswith("/birth "):
                kwargs["birth_patch"] = json.loads(query[7:])
                query = "补充出生资料"
            elif query.startswith("/new "):
                kwargs["new_task"] = True
                query = query[5:]
            result = await app.execute(session_id, query, **kwargs)
            session_id = result.session_id
            print(f"会话 {session_id} / {result.status}\n{result.content}")
            for citation in result.citations:
                print(f"来源：{citation.source_title} / {citation.section} / {citation.chunk_id}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.platform == "win32":
        asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())
    asyncio.run(main())

import asyncio

from guanchen_v2.memory.database import engine
from guanchen_v2.memory.models import MemoryBase


async def main() -> None:
    """创建 Stage 8 全部 Memory 表（会话状态 + 长期用户资料域表）。"""
    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(MemoryBase.metadata.create_all)
    print("Memory tables created.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

import os

# 必须在 numpy / torch / sentence-transformers 等依赖加载之前设置。
os.environ["OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["OMP_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["MKL_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import time
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, TypeAlias

from sqlalchemy import select

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.eval_metrics import (
    calculate_mrr_at_k,
    calculate_percentile,
    calculate_recall_at_k,
    calculate_relative_mrr_gain,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.eval_models import (
    RetrievalEvalCase,
    RetrievalEvalMetrics,
    RetrievalEvalReport,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import (
    KnowledgeChunkRow,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import (
    DenseRetriever,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.rrf import (
    ReciprocalRankFusion,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import (
    SparseRetriever,
)

# ============================================================
# Frozen Stage 4 Eval Contract
# ============================================================

MIN_EVAL_CASES = 45

REQUIRED_DOMAIN_CASE_COUNTS = {
    KnowledgeDomain.BAZI: 30,
    KnowledgeDomain.DREAM: 10,
    KnowledgeDomain.YIJING: 5,
}

OVERALL_RRF_RECALL_GATE = 0.85
DOMAIN_RRF_RECALL_GATE = 0.80

RECALL_K = 5
MRR_K = 10
CANDIDATE_LIMIT = 20

EVAL_CASES_FILE = Path("evals/retrieval/stage4_retrieval_golden.json")

FAILURE_REPORT_FILE = Path("evals/retrieval/stage4_retrieval_failures.jsonl")

FINAL_REPORT_FILE = Path("evals/retrieval/stage4_retrieval_report.json")


# 正式 Stage 4 relevance unit。
SourceSectionKey: TypeAlias = tuple[str, str]


# ============================================================
# CLI
# ============================================================


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="GuanChen Stage 4 Retrieval Evaluation")

    parser.add_argument(
        "--skip-reranker",
        action="store_true",
        help=("只运行 Dense / Sparse / RRF baseline；完全不加载 BGE Reranker。"),
    )

    return parser.parse_args()


# ============================================================
# Eval case loader
# ============================================================


def _parse_filter(
    raw_filter: Any,
) -> RetrievalFilter | None:
    if raw_filter is None:
        return None

    if not isinstance(raw_filter, dict):
        raise ValueError("filters 必须是 object 或 null")

    allowed_fields = {
        "domains",
        "source_types",
        "source_titles",
        "sections",
    }

    unknown_fields = set(raw_filter) - allowed_fields

    if unknown_fields:
        raise ValueError(f"filters 出现未知字段：{sorted(unknown_fields)}")

    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}

    domains = raw_filter.get("domains")
    if domains is not None:
        kwargs["domains"] = frozenset(KnowledgeDomain(value) for value in domains)

    source_types = raw_filter.get("source_types")
    if source_types is not None:
        kwargs["source_types"] = frozenset(SourceType(value) for value in source_types)

    source_titles = raw_filter.get("source_titles")
    if source_titles is not None:
        kwargs["source_titles"] = frozenset(source_titles)

    sections = raw_filter.get("sections")
    if sections is not None:
        kwargs["sections"] = frozenset(sections)

    if not kwargs:
        return None

    return RetrievalFilter(**kwargs)


def load_eval_cases(
    file_path: Path,
) -> list[RetrievalEvalCase]:
    with file_path.open(
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as file:
        raw_data = json.load(file)

    if not isinstance(raw_data, list):
        raise ValueError("Eval 文件顶层必须是 JSON array")

    cases: list[RetrievalEvalCase] = []
    seen_case_ids: set[str] = set()

    for raw_item in raw_data:
        if not isinstance(raw_item, dict):
            raise ValueError("每条 Eval case 必须是 JSON object")

        payload = dict(raw_item)

        payload["filters"] = _parse_filter(payload.get("filters"))

        case = RetrievalEvalCase.model_validate(payload)

        if case.case_id in seen_case_ids:
            raise ValueError(f"发现重复 case_id：{case.case_id}")

        seen_case_ids.add(case.case_id)
        cases.append(case)

    return cases


# ============================================================
# Metadata helpers
# ============================================================


def _enum_or_raw_value(
    value: Any,
) -> Any:
    """
    ORM 字段有时是 str，有时可能是 StrEnum。
    Eval 只需要稳定比较其真实值。
    """
    if hasattr(value, "value"):
        return value.value

    return value


def _source_section_key(
    source_title: str,
    section: str,
) -> SourceSectionKey:
    return (
        source_title,
        section,
    )


def _hit_source_section_key(
    hit: Any,
) -> SourceSectionKey:
    return _source_section_key(
        hit.source_title,
        hit.section,
    )


def _row_source_section_key(
    row: KnowledgeChunkRow,
) -> SourceSectionKey:
    return _source_section_key(
        row.source_title,
        row.section,
    )


def _row_matches_filter(
    row: KnowledgeChunkRow,
    filters: RetrievalFilter,
) -> bool:
    """
    Eval Preflight 自己判断 GT 是否满足 filter。

    不依赖 SparseRetriever 私有字段或私有方法。
    """
    if filters.domains:
        allowed_domains = {domain.value for domain in filters.domains}

        if _enum_or_raw_value(row.domain) not in allowed_domains:
            return False

    if filters.source_types:
        allowed_source_types = {source_type.value for source_type in filters.source_types}

        if _enum_or_raw_value(row.source_type) not in allowed_source_types:
            return False

    if filters.source_titles:
        if row.source_title not in filters.source_titles:
            return False

    if filters.sections:
        if row.section not in filters.sections:
            return False

    return True


# ============================================================
# Preflight
# ============================================================


async def check_preflight(
    cases: list[RetrievalEvalCase],
) -> tuple[
    dict[str, frozenset[SourceSectionKey]],
    dict[str, KnowledgeDomain],
]:
    """
    正式 Benchmark 前检查：

    1. 至少达到当前多领域 Golden Set 的最小题量；
    2. 所有 anchor GT chunk 必须真实存在；
    3. GT 必须满足该 case 的 metadata filter；
    4. 每个 case 的所有 GT anchor 必须属于同一 KnowledgeDomain；
    5. Bazi / Dream / Yijing 必须达到最小覆盖题量；
    6. 从 anchor chunk 派生正式 expected source_title + section。

    返回：
        - case_id -> expected source/section relevance set
        - case_id -> ground-truth domain
    """
    if len(cases) < MIN_EVAL_CASES:
        raise ValueError(f"[BLOCKER] Retrieval Eval 至少需要 {MIN_EVAL_CASES} 条有效人工考题，当前只有 {len(cases)} 条。")

    all_gt_ids = {chunk_id for case in cases for chunk_id in case.relevant_chunk_ids}

    async with async_session() as session:
        statement = select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.chunk_id.in_(sorted(all_gt_ids)))
        rows = (await session.execute(statement)).scalars().all()

    row_map = {row.chunk_id: row for row in rows}
    missing_ids = all_gt_ids - set(row_map)

    if missing_ids:
        raise ValueError(f"Preflight 失败：以下 Ground Truth anchor chunk 不存在于当前数据库：{sorted(missing_ids)}")

    expected_source_sections: dict[
        str,
        frozenset[SourceSectionKey],
    ] = {}
    case_domains: dict[str, KnowledgeDomain] = {}
    coverage_counts = {domain: 0 for domain in REQUIRED_DOMAIN_CASE_COUNTS}

    for case in cases:
        case_expected: set[SourceSectionKey] = set()
        gt_domains: set[KnowledgeDomain] = set()

        for chunk_id in case.relevant_chunk_ids:
            row = row_map[chunk_id]

            if case.filters is not None and not _row_matches_filter(
                row,
                case.filters,
            ):
                raise ValueError(f"Preflight 失败：case [{case.case_id}] 的 GT [{chunk_id}] 不满足自身 RetrievalFilter。")

            try:
                gt_domain = KnowledgeDomain(_enum_or_raw_value(row.domain))
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"Preflight 失败：case [{case.case_id}] 的 GT [{chunk_id}] 包含未知 domain [{row.domain}]。") from exc

            gt_domains.add(gt_domain)
            case_expected.add(_row_source_section_key(row))

        if not case_expected:
            raise ValueError(f"case [{case.case_id}] 无法派生 expected source/section")

        if len(gt_domains) != 1:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Preflight 失败：case [{case.case_id}] 的 Ground Truth 跨越多个 domain：{sorted(domain.value for domain in gt_domains)}"
            )

        case_domain = next(iter(gt_domains))
        expected_source_sections[case.case_id] = frozenset(case_expected)
        case_domains[case.case_id] = case_domain

        if case_domain in coverage_counts:
            coverage_counts[case_domain] += 1

    coverage_errors: list[str] = []

    for domain, minimum in REQUIRED_DOMAIN_CASE_COUNTS.items():
        actual = coverage_counts[domain]
        if actual < minimum:
            coverage_errors.append(f"{domain.value}: {actual}/{minimum}")

    if coverage_errors:
        raise ValueError("[BLOCKER] Retrieval Eval 多领域覆盖不足：" + ", ".join(coverage_errors))

    coverage_text = ", ".join(f"{domain.value}={coverage_counts[domain]}" for domain in REQUIRED_DOMAIN_CASE_COUNTS)

    print(f"✓ Preflight PASS：{len(cases)} cases、GT lineage、metadata filters、domain isolation、expected source/section 全部有效。")
    print(f"✓ Multi-domain coverage：{coverage_text}")

    return expected_source_sections, case_domains


# ============================================================
# Metric helpers
# ============================================================


def _mean(
    values: list[float],
) -> float:
    if not values:
        raise ValueError("没有 metric observation，无法计算均值")

    return sum(values) / len(values)


def _first_relevant_rank(
    relevant_items: set[Any] | frozenset[Any],
    ranked_items: list[Any],
) -> int | None:
    for rank, item in enumerate(
        ranked_items,
        start=1,
    ):
        if item in relevant_items:
            return rank

    return None


def _serialize_filter(
    filters: RetrievalFilter | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if filters is None:
        return None

    return {
        "domains": (sorted(domain.value for domain in filters.domains) if filters.domains else None),
        "source_types": (sorted(source_type.value for source_type in filters.source_types) if filters.source_types else None),
        "source_titles": (sorted(filters.source_titles) if filters.source_titles else None),
        "sections": (sorted(filters.sections) if filters.sections else None),
    }


def _serialize_source_sections(
    values: (set[SourceSectionKey] | frozenset[SourceSectionKey]),
) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    return [
        {
            "source_title": source_title,
            "section": section,
        }
        for source_title, section in sorted(values)
    ]


def _hit_view(
    hit: Any,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Failure Report 用的人类可读 hit。

    不把算法 score 放进正式业务输出；
    Eval failure report 属于离线诊断，因此这里主要展示 lineage。
    """
    return {
        "chunk_id": hit.chunk_id,
        "source_title": hit.source_title,
        "section": hit.section,
    }


# ============================================================
# Main evaluation
# ============================================================


async def run_evaluation(
    *,
    skip_reranker: bool,
) -> None:
    print("=" * 60)
    print("GuanChen Stage 4 Retrieval Benchmark")
    print("=" * 60)

    if skip_reranker:
        print("运行模式：Baseline only (Dense + Sparse + RRF)")
        print("Reranker Gate：DEFERRED")
    else:
        print("运行模式：Full Retrieval Eval (Dense + Sparse + RRF + Reranker)")

    if not EVAL_CASES_FILE.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"[BLOCKER] 评测文件不存在：{EVAL_CASES_FILE}")

    cases = load_eval_cases(EVAL_CASES_FILE)

    print(f"考卷已加载，共计 {len(cases)} 道考题。")

    print("正在初始化 PostgreSQL / pgvector...")

    await init_db()

    expected_source_sections, case_domains = await check_preflight(cases)

    print("正在加载 Dense / Sparse Retrieval...")

    embedding_adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()

    dense_retriever = DenseRetriever(embedding_adapter)

    sparse_retriever = SparseRetriever()
    await sparse_retriever.rebuild_index()

    rrf = ReciprocalRankFusion()

    # ========================================================
    # Optional Reranker
    #
    # skip 模式下连模块都不 import，
    # 避免当前本地 CUDA / CrossEncoder 问题。
    # ========================================================

    rerank_adapter = None
    reranker = None

    if not skip_reranker:
        from guanchen_v2.knowledge.rerank_adapter import (
            BgeRerankerAdapter,
        )
        from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.rerank import (
            KnowledgeReranker,
        )

        rerank_adapter = BgeRerankerAdapter()

        reranker = KnowledgeReranker(rerank_adapter)

    # ========================================================
    # Warm-up
    # ========================================================

    print("正在进行 Embedding Warm-up...")

    embedding_adapter.embed_query("八字预热")

    if not skip_reranker:
        assert rerank_adapter is not None

        print("正在进行 Reranker Warm-up...")

        rerank_adapter.score_pairs(
            [
                (
                    "预热",
                    "这是一段预热文字",
                )
            ]
        )

    print("\n开始执行 Retrieval Eval...\n")

    # ========================================================
    # Official source/section metrics
    # ========================================================

    dense_recalls: list[float] = []
    dense_mrrs: list[float] = []

    sparse_recalls: list[float] = []
    sparse_mrrs: list[float] = []

    rrf_recalls: list[float] = []
    rrf_mrrs: list[float] = []

    rerank_recalls: list[float] = []
    rerank_mrrs: list[float] = []

    # ========================================================
    # Exact chunk diagnostic metrics
    # ========================================================

    dense_chunk_recalls: list[float] = []
    dense_chunk_mrrs: list[float] = []

    sparse_chunk_recalls: list[float] = []
    sparse_chunk_mrrs: list[float] = []

    rrf_chunk_recalls: list[float] = []
    rrf_chunk_mrrs: list[float] = []

    rerank_chunk_recalls: list[float] = []
    rerank_chunk_mrrs: list[float] = []

    rerank_latencies: list[float] = []

    # ========================================================
    # Per-domain official metrics
    # ========================================================

    domain_observations: dict[str, dict[str, list[float]]] = {}

    # ========================================================
    # Failure report
    # ========================================================

    FAILURE_REPORT_FILE.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    if FAILURE_REPORT_FILE.exists():
        FAILURE_REPORT_FILE.unlink()

    for index, case in enumerate(
        cases,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(f"[{index:02d}/{len(cases)}] {case.case_id}: {case.query}")

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # Ground Truth
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        expected_sections = expected_source_sections[case.case_id]

        exact_chunk_gt = case.relevant_chunk_ids

        # ====================================================
        # 1. Dense
        # ====================================================

        dense_hits = await dense_retriever.search(
            case.query,
            limit=CANDIDATE_LIMIT,
            filters=case.filters,
        )

        dense_chunk_ids = [hit.chunk_id for hit in dense_hits]

        dense_section_keys = [_hit_source_section_key(hit) for hit in dense_hits]

        # Official
        dense_recalls.append(
            calculate_recall_at_k(
                expected_sections,
                dense_section_keys,
                RECALL_K,
            )
        )

        dense_mrrs.append(
            calculate_mrr_at_k(
                expected_sections,
                dense_section_keys,
                MRR_K,
            )
        )

        # Diagnostic
        dense_chunk_recalls.append(
            calculate_recall_at_k(
                exact_chunk_gt,
                dense_chunk_ids,
                RECALL_K,
            )
        )

        dense_chunk_mrrs.append(
            calculate_mrr_at_k(
                exact_chunk_gt,
                dense_chunk_ids,
                MRR_K,
            )
        )

        # ====================================================
        # 2. Sparse
        # ====================================================

        sparse_hits = sparse_retriever.search(
            case.query,
            limit=CANDIDATE_LIMIT,
            filters=case.filters,
        )

        sparse_chunk_ids = [hit.chunk_id for hit in sparse_hits]

        sparse_section_keys = [_hit_source_section_key(hit) for hit in sparse_hits]

        # Official
        sparse_recalls.append(
            calculate_recall_at_k(
                expected_sections,
                sparse_section_keys,
                RECALL_K,
            )
        )

        sparse_mrrs.append(
            calculate_mrr_at_k(
                expected_sections,
                sparse_section_keys,
                MRR_K,
            )
        )

        # Diagnostic
        sparse_chunk_recalls.append(
            calculate_recall_at_k(
                exact_chunk_gt,
                sparse_chunk_ids,
                RECALL_K,
            )
        )

        sparse_chunk_mrrs.append(
            calculate_mrr_at_k(
                exact_chunk_gt,
                sparse_chunk_ids,
                MRR_K,
            )
        )

        # ====================================================
        # 3. RRF
        # ====================================================

        rrf_hits = rrf.fuse(
            dense_hits,
            sparse_hits,
            limit=CANDIDATE_LIMIT,
        )

        rrf_chunk_ids = [hit.chunk_id for hit in rrf_hits]

        rrf_section_keys = [_hit_source_section_key(hit) for hit in rrf_hits]

        # Official source/section relevance
        current_rrf_recall = calculate_recall_at_k(
            expected_sections,
            rrf_section_keys,
            RECALL_K,
        )

        rrf_recalls.append(current_rrf_recall)

        rrf_mrrs.append(
            calculate_mrr_at_k(
                expected_sections,
                rrf_section_keys,
                MRR_K,
            )
        )

        # Exact chunk diagnostic
        current_rrf_chunk_recall = calculate_recall_at_k(
            exact_chunk_gt,
            rrf_chunk_ids,
            RECALL_K,
        )

        rrf_chunk_recalls.append(current_rrf_chunk_recall)

        rrf_chunk_mrrs.append(
            calculate_mrr_at_k(
                exact_chunk_gt,
                rrf_chunk_ids,
                MRR_K,
            )
        )

        # ====================================================
        # 4. Optional Reranker
        # ====================================================

        reranked_hits = None
        reranked_chunk_ids = None
        reranked_section_keys = None

        if not skip_reranker:
            assert reranker is not None

            start = time.perf_counter()

            reranked_hits = reranker.rerank(
                case.query,
                rrf_hits,
                limit=CANDIDATE_LIMIT,
            )

            elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000.0

            if reranked_hits:
                rerank_latencies.append(elapsed_ms)

            reranked_chunk_ids = [hit.chunk_id for hit in reranked_hits]

            reranked_section_keys = [_hit_source_section_key(hit) for hit in reranked_hits]

            # Official
            rerank_recalls.append(
                calculate_recall_at_k(
                    expected_sections,
                    reranked_section_keys,
                    RECALL_K,
                )
            )

            rerank_mrrs.append(
                calculate_mrr_at_k(
                    expected_sections,
                    reranked_section_keys,
                    MRR_K,
                )
            )

            # Diagnostic
            rerank_chunk_recalls.append(
                calculate_recall_at_k(
                    exact_chunk_gt,
                    reranked_chunk_ids,
                    RECALL_K,
                )
            )

            rerank_chunk_mrrs.append(
                calculate_mrr_at_k(
                    exact_chunk_gt,
                    reranked_chunk_ids,
                    MRR_K,
                )
            )

        # ====================================================
        # Per-domain official observations
        # ====================================================

        domain_name = case_domains[case.case_id].value
        bucket = domain_observations.setdefault(
            domain_name,
            {
                "dense_recall": [],
                "dense_mrr": [],
                "sparse_recall": [],
                "sparse_mrr": [],
                "rrf_recall": [],
                "rrf_mrr": [],
                "rerank_recall": [],
                "rerank_mrr": [],
            },
        )

        bucket["dense_recall"].append(dense_recalls[-1])
        bucket["dense_mrr"].append(dense_mrrs[-1])
        bucket["sparse_recall"].append(sparse_recalls[-1])
        bucket["sparse_mrr"].append(sparse_mrrs[-1])
        bucket["rrf_recall"].append(rrf_recalls[-1])
        bucket["rrf_mrr"].append(rrf_mrrs[-1])

        if not skip_reranker:
            bucket["rerank_recall"].append(rerank_recalls[-1])
            bucket["rerank_mrr"].append(rerank_mrrs[-1])

        # ====================================================
        # Failure Analysis
        #
        # 只按正式 source/section Gate 判断 failure。
        # Exact chunk miss 会作为 diagnostic 一并记录。
        # ====================================================

        if current_rrf_recall < 1.0:
            failure_record = {
                "case_id": case.case_id,
                "query": case.query,
                "note": case.note,
                "filters": _serialize_filter(case.filters),
                # --------------------------------------------
                # Official GT
                # --------------------------------------------
                "expected_source_sections": (_serialize_source_sections(expected_sections)),
                "source_section_rrf_recall_at_5": (current_rrf_recall),
                "dense_first_relevant_source_section_rank": (
                    _first_relevant_rank(
                        expected_sections,
                        dense_section_keys,
                    )
                ),
                "sparse_first_relevant_source_section_rank": (
                    _first_relevant_rank(
                        expected_sections,
                        sparse_section_keys,
                    )
                ),
                "rrf_first_relevant_source_section_rank": (
                    _first_relevant_rank(
                        expected_sections,
                        rrf_section_keys,
                    )
                ),
                # --------------------------------------------
                # Exact chunk diagnostics
                # --------------------------------------------
                "anchor_chunk_ids": sorted(exact_chunk_gt),
                "exact_chunk_rrf_recall_at_5": (current_rrf_chunk_recall),
                "dense_first_exact_chunk_rank": (
                    _first_relevant_rank(
                        exact_chunk_gt,
                        dense_chunk_ids,
                    )
                ),
                "sparse_first_exact_chunk_rank": (
                    _first_relevant_rank(
                        exact_chunk_gt,
                        sparse_chunk_ids,
                    )
                ),
                "rrf_first_exact_chunk_rank": (
                    _first_relevant_rank(
                        exact_chunk_gt,
                        rrf_chunk_ids,
                    )
                ),
                # --------------------------------------------
                # Human-readable result lineage
                # --------------------------------------------
                "dense_top_10": [_hit_view(hit) for hit in dense_hits[:10]],
                "sparse_top_10": [_hit_view(hit) for hit in sparse_hits[:10]],
                "rrf_top_10": [_hit_view(hit) for hit in rrf_hits[:10]],
                "reranked_top_10": ([_hit_view(hit) for hit in reranked_hits[:10]] if reranked_hits is not None else None),
            }

            with FAILURE_REPORT_FILE.open(
                "a",
                encoding="utf-8",
            ) as file:
                file.write(
                    json.dumps(
                        failure_record,
                        ensure_ascii=False,
                    )
                    + "\n"
                )

    # ========================================================
    # Aggregate official metrics
    # ========================================================

    dense_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
        recall_at_5=_mean(dense_recalls),
        mrr_at_10=_mean(dense_mrrs),
        query_count=len(cases),
    )

    sparse_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
        recall_at_5=_mean(sparse_recalls),
        mrr_at_10=_mean(sparse_mrrs),
        query_count=len(cases),
    )

    rrf_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
        recall_at_5=_mean(rrf_recalls),
        mrr_at_10=_mean(rrf_mrrs),
        query_count=len(cases),
    )

    # ========================================================
    # Aggregate exact chunk diagnostics
    # ========================================================

    dense_exact_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
        recall_at_5=_mean(dense_chunk_recalls),
        mrr_at_10=_mean(dense_chunk_mrrs),
        query_count=len(cases),
    )

    sparse_exact_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
        recall_at_5=_mean(sparse_chunk_recalls),
        mrr_at_10=_mean(sparse_chunk_mrrs),
        query_count=len(cases),
    )

    rrf_exact_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
        recall_at_5=_mean(rrf_chunk_recalls),
        mrr_at_10=_mean(rrf_chunk_mrrs),
        query_count=len(cases),
    )

    # ========================================================
    # Aggregate per-domain official metrics
    # ========================================================

    domain_metrics: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}

    for domain_name, bucket in sorted(domain_observations.items()):
        domain_report: dict[str, Any] = {
            "query_count": len(bucket["rrf_recall"]),
            "dense": {
                "recall_at_5": _mean(bucket["dense_recall"]),
                "mrr_at_10": _mean(bucket["dense_mrr"]),
            },
            "sparse": {
                "recall_at_5": _mean(bucket["sparse_recall"]),
                "mrr_at_10": _mean(bucket["sparse_mrr"]),
            },
            "rrf": {
                "recall_at_5": _mean(bucket["rrf_recall"]),
                "mrr_at_10": _mean(bucket["rrf_mrr"]),
            },
        }

        if bucket["rerank_recall"]:
            domain_report["reranked"] = {
                "recall_at_5": _mean(bucket["rerank_recall"]),
                "mrr_at_10": _mean(bucket["rerank_mrr"]),
            }
        else:
            domain_report["reranked"] = None

        domain_metrics[domain_name] = domain_report

    # ========================================================
    # Report construction
    # ========================================================

    if skip_reranker:
        report = RetrievalEvalReport(
            query_count=len(cases),
            dense=dense_metrics,
            sparse=sparse_metrics,
            rrf=rrf_metrics,
            dense_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(dense_exact_metrics),
            sparse_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(sparse_exact_metrics),
            rrf_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(rrf_exact_metrics),
        )

    else:
        if not rerank_latencies:
            raise ValueError("Reranker 已启用，但没有任何有效 latency observation。")

        reranked_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
            recall_at_5=_mean(rerank_recalls),
            mrr_at_10=_mean(rerank_mrrs),
            query_count=len(cases),
        )

        reranked_exact_metrics = RetrievalEvalMetrics(
            recall_at_5=_mean(rerank_chunk_recalls),
            mrr_at_10=_mean(rerank_chunk_mrrs),
            query_count=len(cases),
        )

        relative_gain = calculate_relative_mrr_gain(
            rrf_metrics.mrr_at_10,
            reranked_metrics.mrr_at_10,
        )

        report = RetrievalEvalReport(
            query_count=len(cases),
            dense=dense_metrics,
            sparse=sparse_metrics,
            rrf=rrf_metrics,
            dense_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(dense_exact_metrics),
            sparse_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(sparse_exact_metrics),
            rrf_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(rrf_exact_metrics),
            reranked=reranked_metrics,
            reranked_exact_chunk_diagnostic=(reranked_exact_metrics),
            reranker_relative_mrr_gain=(relative_gain),
            reranker_added_p50_ms=(
                calculate_percentile(
                    rerank_latencies,
                    50,
                )
            ),
            reranker_added_p95_ms=(
                calculate_percentile(
                    rerank_latencies,
                    95,
                )
            ),
        )

    # ========================================================
    # Save report
    # ========================================================

    report_payload = asdict(report)
    report_payload["domain_metrics"] = domain_metrics

    FINAL_REPORT_FILE.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    with FINAL_REPORT_FILE.open(
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as file:
        json.dump(
            report_payload,
            file,
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )

    # ========================================================
    # Console report
    # ========================================================

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("Retrieval Eval Report")
    print("=" * 60)

    print(
        json.dumps(
            report_payload,
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
    )

    imperfect_count = sum(1 for recall in rrf_recalls if recall < 1.0)

    print(f"\nOfficial source/section failures: {imperfect_count}/{len(cases)} cases have RRF Recall@5 < 1.0")

    print(f"Failure report: {FAILURE_REPORT_FILE}")

    print(f"Final report: {FINAL_REPORT_FILE}")

    # ========================================================
    # Gate 1:
    # Official Stage 4 Core Retrieval Quality
    # ========================================================

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("Verdict 1: Stage 4 Core Retrieval Quality Gate")
    print("=" * 60)

    recall_pass = report.rrf.recall_at_5 >= OVERALL_RRF_RECALL_GATE
    domain_recall_pass = all(metrics["rrf"]["recall_at_5"] >= DOMAIN_RRF_RECALL_GATE for metrics in domain_metrics.values())

    print("Official relevance unit: source_title + section")

    print(f"Dense  Recall@5={report.dense.recall_at_5:.4f}  MRR@10={report.dense.mrr_at_10:.4f}")

    print(f"Sparse Recall@5={report.sparse.recall_at_5:.4f}  MRR@10={report.sparse.mrr_at_10:.4f}")

    print(f"RRF    Recall@5={report.rrf.recall_at_5:.4f}  MRR@10={report.rrf.mrr_at_10:.4f}")

    print("\nPer-domain official metrics:")
    for domain_name, metrics in sorted(domain_metrics.items()):
        print(
            f"  {domain_name:<20} "
            f"n={metrics['query_count']:<3} "
            f"RRF Recall@5={metrics['rrf']['recall_at_5']:.4f}  "
            f"MRR@10={metrics['rrf']['mrr_at_10']:.4f}"
        )

    if recall_pass and domain_recall_pass:
        print(
            "\n[PASS] Core Retrieval Gate："
            f"overall RRF Recall@5 >= {OVERALL_RRF_RECALL_GATE:.2f}，"
            f"且每个要求领域 RRF Recall@5 >= {DOMAIN_RRF_RECALL_GATE:.2f}"
        )
    else:
        print("\n[FAIL] Core Retrieval Gate：overall 或至少一个领域未达到 Recall Gate。")

    # ========================================================
    # Diagnostic exact chunk results
    # ========================================================

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("Diagnostic: Exact Chunk Metrics")
    print("=" * 60)

    print(f"Dense  exact Recall@5={report.dense_exact_chunk_diagnostic.recall_at_5:.4f}  MRR@10={report.dense_exact_chunk_diagnostic.mrr_at_10:.4f}")

    print(
        f"Sparse exact Recall@5={report.sparse_exact_chunk_diagnostic.recall_at_5:.4f}  MRR@10={report.sparse_exact_chunk_diagnostic.mrr_at_10:.4f}"
    )

    print(f"RRF    exact Recall@5={report.rrf_exact_chunk_diagnostic.recall_at_5:.4f}  MRR@10={report.rrf_exact_chunk_diagnostic.mrr_at_10:.4f}")

    print("\n注意：Exact Chunk Metrics 仅用于诊断，不参与 Stage 4 PASS/FAIL。")

    # ========================================================
    # Gate 2:
    # Optional Production Reranker
    # ========================================================

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("Verdict 2: Production Reranker Gate")
    print("=" * 60)

    if skip_reranker:
        print("[DEFERRED] 本次使用 --skip-reranker。")

        print("未执行 Reranker MRR Gain / latency Gate。")

        print("Production policy: reranker_enabled=False")

    else:
        assert report.reranker_relative_mrr_gain is not None

        assert report.reranker_added_p95_ms is not None

        assert report.reranker_added_p50_ms is not None

        mrr_pass = report.reranker_relative_mrr_gain >= 0.05

        latency_pass = report.reranker_added_p95_ms <= 800.0

        reranker_enabled = mrr_pass and latency_pass

        print(f"Relative MRR Gain: {report.reranker_relative_mrr_gain * 100:.2f}%")

        print(f"Reranker p50: {report.reranker_added_p50_ms:.2f} ms")

        print(f"Reranker p95: {report.reranker_added_p95_ms:.2f} ms")

        if reranker_enabled:
            print("\n[ENABLED] Reranker 通过效果与延迟双 Gate。")

            print("Production policy: reranker_enabled=True")
        else:
            print("\n[DISABLED] Reranker 未通过效果/延迟双 Gate。")

            print("Production policy: reranker_enabled=False")


async def main() -> None:
    args = parse_args()

    try:
        await run_evaluation(
            skip_reranker=args.skip_reranker,
        )
    finally:
        await dispose_db()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\eval_router.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 7 full-chain Router gate. Rules-only is diagnostic and never passes the gate."""

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import HighPrecisionRuleRouter, TwoLayerIntentRouter, build_routing_result

GOLDEN_FILE = Path("evals/router/stage7_router_golden.json")
REPORT_FILE = Path("evals/router/stage7_router_report.json")
FAILURES_FILE = Path("evals/router/stage7_router_failures.jsonl")
ACCURACY_GATE = 0.92
MIN_GOLDEN_CASES = 60
MIN_DUAL_OR_AMBIGUOUS = 10


def load_golden():
    cases = json.loads(GOLDEN_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for case in cases:
        IntentType(case["primary_intent"])
        for intent in case.get("secondary_intents", []):
            IntentType(intent)
    return cases


async def evaluate(cases, *, rules_only=False, router=None):
    settings = None
    if not rules_only and router is None:
        settings = Settings()  # same env-file/prefix as the application; no silent fallback
        router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), LLMClient.from_settings(settings))
    rules = HighPrecisionRuleRouter()
    predictions, failures, violations = [], [], []
    primary_correct = secondary_correct = covered = 0
    rule_hits = 0
    for case in cases:
        rule = rules.match(case["query"])
        rule_hits += int(rule is not None)
        if rules_only and rule is None:
            predictions.append({"id": case["id"], "status": "not_evaluated"})
            continue
        try:
            result = build_routing_result(rule) if rules_only else await router.route(case["query"])
            primary = result.primary_intent.value
            secondary = [intent.value for intent in result.secondary_intents]
            primary_ok = primary == case["primary_intent"]
            secondary_ok = secondary == case.get("secondary_intents", [])
            primary_correct += int(primary_ok)
            secondary_correct += int(secondary_ok)
            covered += 1
            row = {
                "id": case["id"],
                "status": "evaluated",
                "primary_intent": primary,
                "secondary_intents": secondary,
                "required_data": [x.value for x in result.required_data],
                "primary_correct": primary_ok,
                "secondary_correct": secondary_ok,
            }
            predictions.append(row)
            if not primary_ok or not secondary_ok:
                failures.append({**row, "expected_primary": case["primary_intent"], "expected_secondary": case.get("secondary_intents", [])})
            if (
                case["primary_intent"] in {"knowledge", "dream"}
                and not case.get("secondary_intents")
                and RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE in result.required_data
            ):
                violations.append(row)
        except Exception as exc:
            row = {"id": case["id"], "status": "error", "error_type": type(exc).__name__}
            predictions.append(row)
            failures.append(row)
    total = len(cases)
    checks = {
        "full_chain": not rules_only,
        "golden_size_ok": total >= MIN_GOLDEN_CASES,
        "six_intents_ok": {c["primary_intent"] for c in cases} == {i.value for i in IntentType},
        "dual_or_ambiguous_ok": sum(c.get("category") in {"explicit_dual", "ambiguous"} for c in cases) >= MIN_DUAL_OR_AMBIGUOUS,
        "all_evaluated": covered == total,
        "accuracy_ok": primary_correct / total >= ACCURACY_GATE,
        "no_birth_profile_violation": not violations,
    }
    return {
        "frozen_contract": "Stage 7 Router Eval",
        "mode": "rules_only" if rules_only else "full_chain",
        "accuracy_gate": ACCURACY_GATE,
        "total": total,
        "evaluated": covered,
        "correct": primary_correct,
        "accuracy": None if rules_only else primary_correct / total,
        "secondary_accuracy": None if rules_only else secondary_correct / total,
        "rule_covered": rule_hits,
        "rule_coverage": rule_hits / total,
        "rule_layer_accuracy": primary_correct / covered if rules_only and covered else None,
        "llm_fallback_needed": total - rule_hits,
        "checks": checks,
        "passed": all(checks.values()),
        "birth_profile_check": {"checked": not rules_only, "violations": violations},
        "failures": failures,
        "predictions": predictions,
        "model": settings.deepseek_model if settings else None,
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "base_commit": subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip(),
    }


async def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--rules-only", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    report = await evaluate(load_golden(), rules_only=args.rules_only)
    path = REPORT_FILE.with_name("stage7_router_rules_report.json") if args.rules_only else REPORT_FILE
    path.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    if not args.rules_only:
        FAILURES_FILE.write_text("".join(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for x in report["failures"]), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k: report[k] for k in ("mode", "total", "evaluated", "accuracy", "secondary_accuracy", "passed")}, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0 if report["passed"] else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(asyncio.run(main()))


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\ingest_corpus.py
================================================================================

import asyncio
import time
from pathlib import Path

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.loader import KnowledgeSourceLoader
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.normalizer import KnowledgeSourceNormalizer
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.parser import MarkdownKnowledgeParser
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.service import KnowledgeIngestionService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import init_db


async def main():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("GuanChen Corpus Ingestion (Dev/Eval)")
    print("=" * 60)

    # 1. 启动并初始化数据库表结构与 Vector 扩展
    print("正在初始化数据库表结构...")
    await init_db()

    # 2. 组装流水线组件
    print("正在加载 BGE Embedding 模型 (可能需要几秒)...")
    embedding_adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter(batch_size=4)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    ingestion_service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, embedding_adapter)

    raw_root = Path("data/knowledge/raw_verified").resolve()
    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(raw_root)
    normalizer = KnowledgeSourceNormalizer()
    parser = MarkdownKnowledgeParser()

    # 3. 解析本地语料
    print(f"正在扫描并解析目录: {raw_root} ...")
    sources = loader.load_all()

    total_docs = []
    for raw_source in sources:
        norm_source = normalizer.normalize(raw_source)
        docs = parser.parse(norm_source)
        total_docs.extend(docs)

    print(f"解析完毕。共提取出 {len(total_docs)} 个逻辑文档 (KnowledgeDocument)。")
    print("开始全量入库 (切分 -> 向量化 -> PostgreSQL UPSERT)...")

    # 4. 执行写入
    start_time = time.perf_counter()
    success_count = 0

    for i, doc in enumerate(total_docs, 1):
        try:
            await ingestion_service.ingest_document(doc)
            success_count += 1
            print(f"[{i}/{len(total_docs)}] 成功入库: {doc.source_title} - {doc.section}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{i}/{len(total_docs)}] 入库失败: {doc.source_title} - {doc.section} | 错误: {e}")

    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"入库作业完成！成功: {success_count}/{len(total_docs)}，耗时: {elapsed:.2f} 秒。")
    print("你现在可以执行 scripts/eval_retrieval.py 开启检索评测了。")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\stage4_answer_acceptance.py
================================================================================

"""Real Stage4 answers and source lineage checks against stored knowledge chunks."""
import asyncio
import json
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import select

from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.application.factory import build_retrieval_service
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import create_database_runtime
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import KnowledgeChunkRow
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient

QUERIES = ['什么是八字', '正官是什么意思', '正印是什么意思', '五行相生的顺序是什么', '天干和地支有什么区别',
           '六爻中的世应是什么', '动爻是什么意思', '阴阳是什么', '十神是什么意思', '周易和六爻有什么区别']
OUT = Path('evals/acceptance/stage4_answer_report.json')


async def main():
    settings = Settings()
    db = create_database_runtime(database_url=settings.database_url)
    report = {'timestamp': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), 'model': settings.deepseek_model, 'cases': []}
    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        retrieval = await build_retrieval_service(db.session_factory)
        agent = KnowledgeAgent(retrieval, LLMClient.from_settings(settings))
        for index, query in enumerate(QUERIES):
            response = await agent.answer(query)
            sources = []
            async with db.session_factory() as session:
                for citation in response.citations:
                    row = await session.scalar(select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.chunk_id == citation.chunk_id))
                    assert row is not None
                    assert row.source_title == citation.source_title and row.section == citation.section
                    sources.append({'chunk_id': row.chunk_id, 'source_title': row.source_title, 'section': row.section, 'text': row.text})
            report['cases'].append({'query': query, 'response': asdict(response), 'sources': sources, 'lineage_passed': True})
            OUT.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
            print(index+1, 'lineage_passed', len(sources), flush=True)
        report['lineage_passed'] = len(report['cases']) == 10 and all(c['lineage_passed'] for c in report['cases'])
        OUT.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    finally:
        await db.dispose()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    asyncio.run(main())


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\stage5_acceptance.py
================================================================================

import asyncio
import json

from rich.console import Console
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.dream.context import DreamContextService
from guanchen_v2.dream.extractor import DreamExtractor
from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import DreamRetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import (
    DreamAgentRuntime,
    DreamAgentStatus,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    get_database_runtime,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import KnowledgeChunkRow
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.rerank_adapter import BgeRerankerAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.assembler import RetrievalResultAssembler
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.rerank import KnowledgeReranker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.rrf import ReciprocalRankFusion
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import SparseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient

console = Console()


async def verify_dream_corpus() -> int:
    """
    验收前确认当前连接的真实数据库中确实存在《周公解梦》 Dream chunks。

    不依赖固定 chunk 数量，只要求至少存在一条真实入库数据，
    避免误连空数据库 / 测试数据库时仍然继续执行验收。
    """
    async with async_session() as session:
        stmt = (
            select(func.count(KnowledgeChunkRow.chunk_id))
            .where(KnowledgeChunkRow.domain == "dream")
            .where(KnowledgeChunkRow.source_title == "《周公解梦》")
        )

        result = await session.execute(stmt)
        count = int(result.scalar_one())

    if count <= 0:
        raise RuntimeError("当前数据库中未找到《周公解梦》Dream chunks。请确认 GUANCHEN_DATABASE_URL 指向已经完成正式语料入库的 GuanChen 数据库。")

    return count


async def build_real_retrieval_service(
    settings: Settings,
) -> RetrievalService:
    """
    构建 Stage 4 真实共享检索链路：

        PostgreSQL / pgvector
        + BGE Dense Retrieval
        + BM25S Sparse Retrieval
        + RRF Fusion
        + RetrievalResultAssembler

    当前生产策略保持 EVAL-02 的正式决定：
        reranker_enabled=False

    因此实际返回链路为：
        Dense + Sparse -> RRF -> Top-K
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. 绑定当前应用 Settings 对应的真实 DatabaseRuntime
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    get_database_runtime(settings=settings)

    # 幂等保证 pgvector extension / ORM schema 存在。
    # create_all 不会删除已经入库的知识数据。
    await init_db()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. 验证 Dream corpus
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    dream_chunk_count = await verify_dream_corpus()

    console.print(f"[bold green]✓ 已连接真实知识库，《周公解梦》Dream chunks: {dream_chunk_count}[/bold green]")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Dense Retrieval
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("[bold yellow]正在加载 BGE Embedding 与 Dense Retriever...[/bold yellow]")

    embedding_adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()

    dense_retriever = DenseRetriever(
        embedding_adapter=embedding_adapter,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Sparse Retrieval
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("[bold yellow]正在从 PostgreSQL 构建 BM25S Sparse Index...[/bold yellow]")

    sparse_retriever = SparseRetriever()

    indexed_chunk_count = await sparse_retriever.rebuild_index()

    if indexed_chunk_count <= 0:
        raise RuntimeError("SparseRetriever 未从数据库加载到任何 KnowledgeChunk，拒绝继续 Stage 5 真实检索验收。")

    console.print(f"[bold green]✓ Sparse Index 构建完成，共索引 {indexed_chunk_count} chunks[/bold green]")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. RRF / Reranker / Assembler
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    rrf = ReciprocalRankFusion()

    # RetrievalService 当前构造契约仍然要求提供 reranker。
    # BgeRerankerAdapter 本身采用 lazy model loading；
    # 由于 reranker_enabled=False，本次不会执行 CrossEncoder rerank。
    reranker_adapter = BgeRerankerAdapter()
    reranker = KnowledgeReranker(
        adapter=reranker_adapter,
    )

    assembler = RetrievalResultAssembler()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Shared Retrieval Service
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    retrieval_service = RetrievalService(
        dense_retriever=dense_retriever,
        sparse_retriever=sparse_retriever,
        rrf=rrf,
        reranker=reranker,
        assembler=assembler,
        candidate_limit=20,
        result_limit=5,
        reranker_enabled=False,
    )

    console.print("[bold green]✓ Stage 4 RetrievalService 初始化完成：Dense + Sparse + RRF，Reranker=OFF[/bold green]")

    return retrieval_service


async def print_turn_result(
    turn_num: int,
    user_text: str,
    result,
) -> None:
    console.print(f"\n[bold magenta]================ TURN {turn_num} ================[/bold magenta]")

    console.print(f"[bold]user_text:[/bold] {user_text}\n")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Agent Result
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("[bold cyan]=== AGENT RESULT ===[/bold cyan]")

    console.print(f"status: {result.status.value}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # State
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("\n[bold cyan]=== STATE ===[/bold cyan]")

    record = result.record

    console.print(f"dream_id: {record.dream_id}")

    console.print(f"revision: {record.revision}")

    console.print(f"interpretation_status: {record.interpretation_state.status.value}")

    console.print(f"last_interpreted_revision: {record.interpretation_state.last_interpreted_revision}")

    console.print(
        "elements:\n"
        + json.dumps(
            record.elements.model_dump(
                exclude_defaults=True,
            ),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        )
    )

    console.print(
        "reality_background:\n"
        + json.dumps(
            record.reality_background.model_dump(
                exclude_defaults=True,
            ),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Clarification
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    if result.status == DreamAgentStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
        console.print(f"\n[bold yellow]>>> NEEDS CLARIFICATION: {result.clarification_question}[/bold yellow]")
        return

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Answer contract
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    if result.response is None:
        raise RuntimeError("DreamAgentStatus.ANSWERED 但 response 为空，违反 DreamAgentResult contract。")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Answer
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("\n[bold green]=== ANSWER ===[/bold green]")

    console.print(f"[bold]traditional_view:[/bold]\n{result.response.traditional_view}\n")

    console.print(f"[bold]psychological_context_view:[/bold]\n{result.response.psychological_context_view}\n")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Citations
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("[bold green]=== CITATIONS ===[/bold green]")

    if not result.citations:
        console.print("[] (No citations)")
        return

    for index, citation in enumerate(
        result.citations,
        start=1,
    ):
        console.print(f"[{index}] {citation.source_title} - {citation.section} (Chunk: {citation.chunk_id})")


async def run_acceptance() -> None:
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Application Settings / LLM
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("[bold yellow]正在初始化真实 DeepSeek LLM...[/bold yellow]")

    settings = Settings()

    llm_client = LLMClient.from_settings(
        settings,
    )

    extractor = DreamExtractor(
        llm_client=llm_client,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Stage 4 Real Retrieval
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    console.print("[bold yellow]正在连接向量数据库与 Stage 4 真实检索服务...[/bold yellow]")

    retrieval_service_base = await build_real_retrieval_service(
        settings=settings,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Dream Services
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    context_service = DreamContextService(
        extractor=extractor,
    )

    dream_retrieval_service = DreamRetrievalService(
        retrieval_service=retrieval_service_base,
    )

    runtime = DreamAgentRuntime(
        context_service=context_service,
        retrieval_service=dream_retrieval_service,
        llm_client=llm_client,
    )

    console.print("[bold green]✓ Dream Agent 已挂载真实 Stage 4 RetrievalService[/bold green]")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Acceptance Scenarios
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    scenarios = [
        (
            "Scenario A (首次完整解释)",
            "我梦到自己回到小学，在一条很长的走廊里一直找不到出口，后来看到一扇红色的门。我在梦里特别害怕，醒来以后还觉得很真实。",
        ),
        (
            "Scenario B1 (补充人物)",
            "后来想起来，我以前的班主任也在走廊尽头。",
        ),
        (
            "Scenario B2 (连续补充)",
            "还有一扇一直打不开的门。梦里我妈妈站在门边。",
        ),
        (
            "Scenario C (明确 Correction)",
            "我刚才记错了，不是妈妈，是以前的班主任。",
        ),
        (
            "Scenario D (现实背景补充)",
            "最近工作项目快到截止日期了，我现在确实压力很大。",
        ),
        (
            "CONTINUE Smoke",
            "然后呢？",
        ),
        (
            "Scenario E (模糊新梦边界)",
            "昨晚我又梦见自己在树林里。",
        ),
        (
            "Scenario F (明确切换新梦)",
            "换一个梦。我梦见自己在海边，看到很大的浪。",
        ),
    ]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Run multi-turn acceptance
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    for turn_num, (name, user_text) in enumerate(
        scenarios,
        start=1,
    ):
        console.rule(f"[bold red]{name}[/bold red]")

        result = await runtime.handle_turn(
            user_text,
        )

        await print_turn_result(
            turn_num=turn_num,
            user_text=user_text,
            result=result,
        )

        # 避免连续真实 LLM 请求过快。
        await asyncio.sleep(1)


async def main() -> None:
    try:
        await run_acceptance()
    finally:
        # DatabaseRuntime 使用进程级 lazy engine。
        # CLI / acceptance script 结束时显式释放连接池。
        await dispose_db()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\stage8_acceptance.py

"""Stage 1-8 live acceptance through the production assembly (synthetic inputs only)."""

import asyncio
import json
import sys
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from guanchen_v2.application.factory import open_application
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.memory.selector import MemorySelector

PROFILE = dict(year=1992, month=8, day=18, hour=10, minute=0, gender="male")
OUT = Path("evals/acceptance/stage8_live_report.json")
KNOWLEDGE = [
    "什么是八字",
    "正官是什么意思",
    "正印是什么意思",
    "五行相生的顺序是什么",
    "天干和地支有什么区别",
    "六爻中的世应是什么",
    "动爻是什么意思",
    "阴阳是什么",
    "十神是什么意思",
    "周易和六爻有什么区别",
]
DREAM = [
    "我昨晚梦见在河边和爸爸散步，水很清，感觉轻松，请解释这个梦。",
    "补充现实背景：最近工作压力很大，常常加班。",
    "补充梦中人物：妈妈后来也来了，和我们一起散步。",
    "更正一下，不是爸爸，是哥哥和我散步。",
    "然后呢",
    "换一个梦：我在山上独自飞翔，感觉非常自由。",
]
DUAL = [
    "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看，我梦见在海边散步，感觉平静，想了解事业方向。",
    "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起分析，我梦见在山上走路，感觉轻松，想了解工作状态。",
    "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起解读，我梦见在家里看书，感觉温暖，想了解关系相处。",
    "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦综合看，我梦见朋友来家里吃饭，感觉开心，想了解人际关系。",
    "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起参考，我梦见在田野散步，感觉平静，想了解近期状态。",
]


async def main():
    prefix = "acceptance-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    report = {"timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), "synthetic_inputs": True, "cases": [], "checks": {}}
    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def save():
        OUT.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    async def turn(app, label, session, query, **kwargs):
        try:
            result = await app.execute(prefix + session, query, **kwargs)
            report["cases"].append({"id": label, "query": query, "response": result.model_dump(mode="json")})
            print(label, result.status, len(result.citations), flush=True)
            save()
            return result
        except Exception as exc:
            report["cases"].append({"id": label, "error_type": type(exc).__name__})
            save()
            raise

    async with open_application(Settings()) as app:
        for index, query in enumerate(KNOWLEDGE):
            await turn(app, f"knowledge-{index + 1}", "-knowledge", query, new_task=True)
        first = await turn(app, "bazi-missing", "-bazi", "我的八字事业怎么样")
        partial = {key: value for key, value in PROFILE.items() if key not in {"hour", "gender"}}
        await turn(app, "bazi-partial", "-bazi", "补充部分出生资料", birth_patch=partial)
        await turn(app, "bazi-hour", "-bazi", "补充出生时间", birth_patch={"hour": 10})
        await turn(app, "bazi-complete", "-bazi", "补充性别", birth_patch={"gender": "male"})
        await turn(app, "bazi-correction", "-bazi", "我的八字感情怎么样", birth_patch={"hour": 16, "minute": 30})
        ids = []
        for index, query in enumerate(DREAM):
            await turn(app, f"dream-{index + 1}", "-dream", query)
            record = await MemorySelector(app.session_factory).load_dream_memory(prefix + "-dream")
            ids.append(str(record.dream_id))
            report["cases"][-1]["dream_state"] = record.model_dump(mode="json")
            save()
        report["checks"]["same_dream_first_five"] = len(set(ids[:5])) == 1
        report["checks"]["new_dream_sixth"] = ids[5] != ids[4]
        start = await turn(app, "liuyao-start", "-liuyao", "帮我起一卦，未来三个月是否接受新公司的工程师职位？收入增加但通勤更远。")
        report["checks"]["interrupt_before_cast"] = start.status == "interrupted"
        await turn(app, "liuyao-negative", "-liuyao", "我不确认，取消")
        decision = await MemorySelector(app.session_factory).load_decision_memory(prefix + "-liuyao")
        report["checks"]["negative_no_cast"] = decision.hexagram is None and decision.status.value == "cancelled"
        await turn(app, "liuyao-restart-start", "-resume", "帮我起一卦，未来三个月是否接受新公司的工程师职位？收入增加但通勤更远。")
        for index, query in enumerate(DUAL):
            result = await turn(app, f"dual-{index + 1}", f"-dual-{index}", query, birth_patch=PROFILE)
            report["checks"][f"dual-{index + 1}-two-intents"] = len(result.used_intents) == 2
        report["checks"]["birth_requires_input"] = first.status == "needs_input"
    # Destroy engine / pool / runtimes and build the real application again.
    async with open_application(Settings()) as rebuilt:
        resumed = await turn(rebuilt, "liuyao-after-rebuild", "-resume", "确认")
        report["checks"]["resume_completed"] = resumed.status == "answered"
        await turn(rebuilt, "bazi-after-rebuild", "-bazi", "我的八字事业怎么样")
        record = await MemorySelector(rebuilt.session_factory).load_birth_profile(prefix + "-bazi")
        report["checks"]["corrected_profile_recovered"] = record.hour == 16 and record.minute == 30
        await turn(rebuilt, "dream-after-rebuild", "-dream", "然后呢")
        record = await MemorySelector(rebuilt.session_factory).load_dream_memory(prefix + "-dream")
        report["checks"]["dream_recovered"] = str(record.dream_id) == ids[-1]
    report["passed"] = all(report["checks"].values())
    save()
    print("passed=", report["passed"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.platform == "win32":
        asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())
    asyncio.run(main())


================================================================================
FILE: tests\conftest.py

"""全局测试基础设施。

约定（与 tests/knowledge 保持一致）：
- 所有依赖 PostgreSQL 的测试统一指向独立测试数据库 guanchen_test；
- DB 测试通过 `pytest -m integration` 运行；
- 每个 DB 测试使用独立 AsyncEngine（绑定到当前 event loop），
  并在 teardown 时 dispose，避免 asyncpg 连接池跨 loop 复用。
"""
import asyncio
import sys
from collections.abc import AsyncGenerator

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import (
    AsyncEngine,
    async_sessionmaker,
)

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.memory.database import create_async_engine_and_factory
from guanchen_v2.memory.models import MemoryBase

# psycopg（langgraph-checkpoint-postgres 依赖）不支持 Windows ProactorEventLoop，
# 全局切换到 SelectorEventLoopPolicy，保证 checkpointer 集成测试可用。
if sys.platform == "win32":
    asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())

TEST_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/guanchen_test"


@pytest.fixture
async def db_env() -> AsyncGenerator[tuple[AsyncEngine, async_sessionmaker], None]:
    """
    每个测试独立的数据库环境：全新表 + teardown 清空。

    覆盖 MemoryBase 全部表以及 langgraph-checkpoint-postgres 官方表。
    """
    engine, factory = create_async_engine_and_factory(TEST_DATABASE_URL)

    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(MemoryBase.metadata.create_all)

    try:
        yield engine, factory
    finally:
        async with engine.begin() as conn:
            await conn.run_sync(MemoryBase.metadata.drop_all)
            # langgraph-checkpoint-postgres 官方表：必须连同迁移追踪表一并清理，
            # 否则 setup() 会因 checkpoint_migrations 记录版本号而跳过真实建表。
            await conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS checkpoints CASCADE"))
            await conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS checkpoint_blobs CASCADE"))
            await conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS checkpoint_writes CASCADE"))
            await conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS checkpoint_migrations CASCADE"))
        await engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
async def postgres_session_factory(
    db_env: tuple[AsyncEngine, async_sessionmaker],
) -> async_sessionmaker:
    return db_env[1]


@pytest.fixture
def sample_state() -> ConversationState:
    return ConversationState(session_id="sample-session")

from unittest.mock import Mock

import httpx
import pytest
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import (
    BaseChatModel,
)
from openai import APIStatusError
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    InvalidBaziInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.requirements import (
    check_bazi_requirements,
)
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMProviderError,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BirthProfile,
    Gender,
)


def test_unknown_provider_exception_message_is_stable_and_raw_cause_is_preserved():
    model = Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)

    raw_error = RuntimeError("SECRET_PROVIDER_BODY_123")

    model.invoke.side_effect = raw_error

    client = LLMClient(
        model=model,
        max_retries=0,
        retry_wait_exponential_jitter=False,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        LLMProviderError,
    ) as exc_info:
        client.text("hello")

    assert str(exc_info.value) == "LLM 调用失败"

    assert "SECRET_PROVIDER_BODY_123" not in str(exc_info.value)

    assert exc_info.value.__cause__ is raw_error


def test_api_status_error_does_not_leak_provider_message():
    model = Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)

    request = httpx.Request(
        "POST",
        "https://api.example.test/chat",
    )

    raw_error = APIStatusError(
        message=("Bad Request: SECRET_PROVIDER_RESPONSE"),
        response=httpx.Response(
            400,
            request=request,
        ),
        body={"detail": ("SECRET_PROVIDER_RESPONSE")},
    )

    model.invoke.side_effect = raw_error

    client = LLMClient(
        model=model,
        max_retries=0,
        retry_wait_exponential_jitter=False,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        LLMProviderError,
    ) as exc_info:
        client.text("hello")

    assert str(exc_info.value) == "LLM 服务请求失败"

    assert "SECRET_PROVIDER_RESPONSE" not in str(exc_info.value)

    assert exc_info.value.__cause__ is raw_error


def test_invalid_bazi_date_returns_stable_business_reason():
    input_data = BaziAgentInput(
        question="测事业",
        year=2023,
        month=2,
        day=29,
    )

    result = check_bazi_requirements(input_data)

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        InvalidBaziInput,
    )

    assert result.reason == ("非法的日期组合，请检查年月日后重新提供。")

    assert "day is out of range" not in result.reason


def test_birth_profile_validation_does_not_leak_datetime_exception():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
    ) as exc_info:
        BirthProfile(
            year=2023,
            month=2,
            day=29,
            hour=12,
            minute=0,
            gender=Gender.FEMALE,
        )

    message = str(exc_info.value)

    assert "非法的日期时间组合" in message

    assert "day is out of range" not in message
