import json

from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamExtractionError
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import LLMError
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamElements,
    DreamPatch,
    DreamRecord,
    RealityBackground,
)
from guanchen_v2.prompts.dream_agent import (
    DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    DREAM_EXTRACTION_USER_TEMPLATE,
)


class DreamExtractor:
    """
    Dream Agent 的结构化增量信息抽取器。

    职责：
    - 只抽取用户本轮明确提供的 DreamPatch；
    - 只向 LLM 暴露当前有效 Dream 状态；
    - 不负责状态 merge；
    - 不负责梦境解释；
    - 所有 LLM 调用统一通过 LLMClient。
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        llm_client: LLMClient,
    ) -> None:
        self._llm_client = llm_client

    def _build_context(
        self,
        current_record: DreamRecord | None,
    ) -> str:
        """
        构建抽取任务所需的最小当前状态。

        不传递：
        - raw_descriptions
        - interpretation_state
        - revision
        - 其他历史运行时信息
        """
        if current_record is None:
            elements = DreamElements()
            reality_background = RealityBackground()
        else:
            elements = current_record.elements
            reality_background = current_record.reality_background

        context = {
            "elements": elements.model_dump(),
            "reality_background": (reality_background.model_dump()),
        }

        return json.dumps(
            context,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )

    async def extract(
        self,
        user_text: str,
        current_record: DreamRecord | None = None,
    ) -> DreamPatch:
        if not user_text.strip():
            raise DreamExtractionError("梦境信息抽取输入不能为空")

        current_context = self._build_context(current_record=current_record)

        user_prompt = DREAM_EXTRACTION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            current_context=current_context,
            user_text=user_text,
        )

        try:
            result = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
                system_prompt=(DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT),
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
                schema=DreamPatch,
            )
        except LLMError as exc:
            raise DreamExtractionError("梦境信息结构化抽取失败") from exc

        if not isinstance(
            result,
            DreamPatch,
        ):
            raise DreamExtractionError("模型未返回合法的 DreamPatch 结构化输出")

        return result


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\dream\retrieval.py
================================================================================

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    RetrievalResult,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamRecord,
    DreamRetrievalAspect,
    DreamRetrievalQuery,
    NonBlankText,
)

DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER = RetrievalFilter(domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.DREAM}))


class DreamRetrievalEvidence(BaseModel):
    """一个 Dream retrieval aspect 对应的检索结果集合。"""

    aspect: DreamRetrievalAspect
    query: NonBlankText
    results: list[RetrievalResult] = Field(default_factory=list)


def _join_values(values: list[str]) -> str:
    return "、".join(values)


def build_dream_retrieval_queries(
    record: DreamRecord,
) -> list[DreamRetrievalQuery]:
    """
    将当前 DreamRecord 转换为确定性的 Dream retrieval queries。

    只使用已经结构化确认的梦境状态，
    不使用 raw_descriptions、revision 或模型自行推断的信息。
    """
    queries: list[DreamRetrievalQuery] = []

    elements = record.elements
    background = record.reality_background

    symbol_parts: list[str] = []

    if elements.people:
        symbol_parts.append(f"人物：{_join_values(elements.people)}")

    if elements.scenes:
        symbol_parts.append(f"场景：{_join_values(elements.scenes)}")

    if elements.objects:
        symbol_parts.append(f"对象：{_join_values(elements.objects)}")

    if elements.actions:
        symbol_parts.append(f"行为：{_join_values(elements.actions)}")

    if elements.anomalies:
        symbol_parts.append(f"异常现象：{_join_values(elements.anomalies)}")

    if symbol_parts:
        queries.append(
            DreamRetrievalQuery(
                aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL,
                query=(f"梦境传统象征 {'；'.join(symbol_parts)}"),
            )
        )

    emotion_parts: list[str] = []

    if elements.emotions:
        emotion_parts.append(f"梦中情绪：{_join_values(elements.emotions)}")

    if elements.subjective_feelings:
        emotion_parts.append(f"醒后感受：{_join_values(elements.subjective_feelings)}")

    if emotion_parts:
        queries.append(
            DreamRetrievalQuery(
                aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.EMOTION,
                query=(f"梦境情绪传统解释 {'；'.join(emotion_parts)}"),
            )
        )

    reality_parts: list[str] = []

    if background.recent_events:
        reality_parts.append(f"近期事件：{_join_values(background.recent_events)}")

    if background.ongoing_contexts:
        reality_parts.append(f"当前情境：{_join_values(background.ongoing_contexts)}")

    if background.stressors:
        reality_parts.append(f"压力：{_join_values(background.stressors)}")

    if background.user_associations:
        reality_parts.append(f"主观联想：{_join_values(background.user_associations)}")

    if reality_parts:
        queries.append(
            DreamRetrievalQuery(
                aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.REALITY_CONTEXT,
                query=(f"梦境与现实情境传统解释 {'；'.join(reality_parts)}"),
            )
        )

    return queries


def dedupe_retrieval_results(
    evidence_list: list[DreamRetrievalEvidence],
) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
    """
    跨 Dream retrieval aspect 对 Chunk 做稳定去重。

    保留同一 chunk 第一次出现的位置，
    不根据 score 再次排序。
    """
    deduped: list[RetrievalResult] = []
    seen_chunk_ids: set[str] = set()

    for evidence in evidence_list:
        for result in evidence.results:
            if result.chunk_id in seen_chunk_ids:
                continue

            seen_chunk_ids.add(result.chunk_id)
            deduped.append(result)

    return deduped


class DreamRetrievalService:
    """
    Dream Agent 的领域检索适配层。

    领域边界：
    - 所有 Dream query 必须限定在 KnowledgeDomain.DREAM；
    - 调用方不能覆盖或关闭该过滤条件；
    - Shared RetrievalService 继续负责 Dense/Sparse/RRF/Reranker。
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        retrieval_service: RetrievalService,
    ) -> None:
        self._retrieval_service = retrieval_service

    async def retrieve(
        self,
        record: DreamRecord,
    ) -> list[DreamRetrievalEvidence]:
        queries = build_dream_retrieval_queries(record)

        evidence_groups: list[DreamRetrievalEvidence] = []

        for retrieval_query in queries:
            results = await self._retrieval_service.retrieve(
                query=retrieval_query.query,
                filters=DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER,
            )

            evidence_groups.append(
                DreamRetrievalEvidence(
                    aspect=retrieval_query.aspect,
                    query=retrieval_query.query,
                    results=results,
                )
            )

        return evidence_groups


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\dream\runtime.py
================================================================================

import json
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import (
    BaseModel,
    ConfigDict,
    Field,
    model_validator,
)

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError
from guanchen_v2.dream.context import (
    DreamContextService,
    DreamContextStatus,
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import mark_interpreted
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import (
    DreamInterpretationError,
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import (
    DreamRetrievalService,
    dedupe_retrieval_results,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    Citation,
    RetrievalResult,
)
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamRecord,
    InterpretationStatus,
    NonBlankText,
)
from guanchen_v2.prompts.dream_agent import (
    DREAM_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    DREAM_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE,
)


class DreamResponseMode(StrEnum):
    INITIAL = "initial"
    UPDATE = "update"
    CONTINUE = "continue"


class DreamInterpretationDraft(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    traditional_view: NonBlankText | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=("仅基于提供的传统知识 evidence 形成的文化象征解释；证据不足或未使用传统证据时为 null"),
    )

    psychological_context_view: NonBlankText = Field(
        description=("非诊断性的心理 / 现实情境反思，只基于用户明确提供的梦境、情绪和现实背景"),
    )

    used_chunk_ids: list[str] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description=("traditional_view 实际使用的 TRADITIONAL EVIDENCE chunk_id，按首次使用顺序返回。"),
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_evidence_contract(
        self,
    ) -> "DreamInterpretationDraft":
        for chunk_id in self.used_chunk_ids:
            if not chunk_id.strip():
                raise ValueError("used_chunk_ids 不能包含空值。")

        if self.traditional_view is None and self.used_chunk_ids:
            raise ValueError("traditional_view 为空时，used_chunk_ids 必须为空。")

        if self.traditional_view is not None and not self.used_chunk_ids:
            raise ValueError("traditional_view 非空时，必须至少引用一个实际使用的 chunk_id。")

        return self


class DreamInterpretationResponse(BaseModel):
    traditional_view: NonBlankText
    psychological_context_view: NonBlankText


class DreamAgentStatus(StrEnum):
    NEEDS_CLARIFICATION = "needs_clarification"
    ANSWERED = "answered"


class DreamAgentResult(BaseModel):
    status: DreamAgentStatus
    record: DreamRecord
    clarification_question: str | None = None
    response: DreamInterpretationResponse | None = None
    citations: list[Citation] = Field(default_factory=list)


def select_response_mode(
    record: DreamRecord,
) -> DreamResponseMode:
    """确定性决定本轮 Dream 回复模式。"""
    if record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.NOT_STARTED:
        return DreamResponseMode.INITIAL

    if record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.STALE:
        return DreamResponseMode.UPDATE

    return DreamResponseMode.CONTINUE


def _format_evidence(
    results: list[RetrievalResult],
) -> str:
    """
    将 RetrievalResult 转换为 LLM 可使用的 evidence。

    只暴露 citation 所需 identity 与正文，
    不暴露 Dense/Sparse/RRF/Reranker 分数。
    """
    if not results:
        return "[]"

    formatted: list[str] = []

    for index, result in enumerate(
        results,
        start=1,
    ):
        formatted.append(
            f"[Evidence {index}]\nchunk_id: {result.chunk_id}\nsource_title: {result.source_title}\nsection: {result.section}\ntext: {result.text}\n"
        )

    return "\n".join(formatted)


def _resolve_used_evidence(
    results: list[RetrievalResult],
    used_chunk_ids: list[str],
) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
    """
    将 LLM 声明实际使用的 chunk_id 映射回本轮合法 evidence。

    - 禁止引用未检索到的 chunk；
    - 按 LLM 第一次使用顺序返回；
    - 重复 chunk_id 只保留一次。
    """
    result_map = {result.chunk_id: result for result in results}

    resolved: list[RetrievalResult] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()

    for raw_chunk_id in used_chunk_ids:
        chunk_id = raw_chunk_id.strip()

        if chunk_id in seen:
            continue

        if chunk_id not in result_map:
            raise DreamInterpretationError("模型返回了未授权的传统知识证据编号。")

        seen.add(chunk_id)
        resolved.append(result_map[chunk_id])

    return resolved


def _map_citations(
    results: list[RetrievalResult],
) -> list[Citation]:
    """只为已经确认实际使用的 evidence 生成 Citation。"""
    return [Citation.from_retrieval_result(result) for result in results]


class DreamAgentRuntime:
    def __init__(
        self,
        context_service: DreamContextService,
        retrieval_service: DreamRetrievalService,
        llm_client: LLMClient,
        record_store: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._context_service = context_service
        self._retrieval_service = retrieval_service
        self._llm_client = llm_client

        self._current_record: DreamRecord | None = None
        self._record_store = record_store

    async def _restore_record(self) -> None:
        """跨进程恢复：从持久层加载当前 DreamRecord（Stage 8）。"""
        if self._current_record is None and self._record_store is not None:
            self._current_record = await self._record_store.load_latest()

    async def _persist_record(self) -> None:
        """每个 turn 结束后持久化当前 DreamRecord。"""
        if self._record_store is not None and self._current_record is not None:
            await self._record_store.save(self._current_record)

    async def handle_turn(
        self,
        user_text: str,
    ) -> DreamAgentResult:
        await self._restore_record()

        # 1. 更新 Dream 状态
        context_result = await self._context_service.apply_turn(
            user_text,
            current_record=self._current_record,
        )

        # Merge 成功后，新状态立即成为当前状态。
        self._current_record = context_result.record
        await self._persist_record()

        # 2. 需要澄清时短路
        if context_result.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return DreamAgentResult(
                status=DreamAgentStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=self._current_record,
                clarification_question=(context_result.clarification_question),
            )

        # 3. 回复模式由状态机决定
        mode = select_response_mode(self._current_record)

        # 4. DreamRetrievalService 内部强制 DREAM domain
        evidence_groups = await self._retrieval_service.retrieve(self._current_record)

        deduped_results = dedupe_retrieval_results(evidence_groups)

        # 5. 构建最小化解释上下文
        state_str = json.dumps(
            {
                "elements": (self._current_record.elements.model_dump()),
                "reality_background": (self._current_record.reality_background.model_dump()),
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )

        if context_result.patch is None:
            delta_str = "{}"
        else:
            delta_str = context_result.patch.model_dump_json(exclude_unset=True)

        evidence_str = _format_evidence(deduped_results)

        user_prompt = DREAM_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            response_mode=mode.value,
            current_state=state_str,
            current_delta=delta_str,
            traditional_evidence=evidence_str,
        )

        # 6. LLM 结构化解释
        try:
            draft = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
                system_prompt=(DREAM_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT),
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
                schema=DreamInterpretationDraft,
            )
        except ProjectError as exc:
            raise DreamInterpretationError("梦境解释生成失败") from exc

        # 7. 验证 LLM 声明使用的 evidence
        used_results = _resolve_used_evidence(
            results=deduped_results,
            used_chunk_ids=draft.used_chunk_ids,
        )

        fallback_traditional_view = "当前共享知识库没有检索到足够可追溯的传统资料，因此本轮暂不作具体的传统象征判断。"

        # 8. traditional_view 必须与真实 evidence lineage 对齐
        if draft.traditional_view is None or not used_results:
            final_traditional_view = fallback_traditional_view
            citations: list[Citation] = []
        else:
            final_traditional_view = draft.traditional_view
            citations = _map_citations(used_results)

        response = DreamInterpretationResponse(
            traditional_view=final_traditional_view,
            psychological_context_view=(draft.psychological_context_view),
        )

        # 9. 解释成功后才将 interpretation 标为 CURRENT
        self._current_record = mark_interpreted(self._current_record)
        await self._persist_record()

        return DreamAgentResult(
            status=DreamAgentStatus.ANSWERED,
            record=self._current_record,
            response=response,
            citations=citations,
        )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\embeddings.py
================================================================================

from typing import Sequence

from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeEmbeddingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeChunk


class BgeEmbeddingAdapter:
    """
    GuanChen BGE Embedding Adapter。

    职责：
    1. 使用固定 Embedding 模型生成 Dense Vector。
    2. 保证输出维度为 512。
    3. 保证向量执行 L2 normalization。
    4. 管理模型生命周期与 device / batch 策略。
    5. 向持久化与检索层暴露稳定的模型身份。
    6. 隔离 sentence-transformers / PyTorch 异常。

    不负责：
    - PostgreSQL / pgvector
    - Retrieval
    - Reranker
    - Citation
    """

    MODEL_NAME = "BAAI/bge-small-zh-v1.5"
    DIMENSION = 512

    def __init__(
        self,
        device: str = "cpu",
        batch_size: int = 32,
    ) -> None:
        self._device = device
        self._batch_size = batch_size

        try:
            self._model = SentenceTransformer(
                self.MODEL_NAME,
                device=self._device,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("Embedding 模型加载失败。") from exc

    @property
    def model_name(self) -> str:
        """
        当前 Adapter 生成向量所使用的稳定模型身份。

        Persistence 与 Dense Retrieval 必须使用该值，
        防止不同 Embedding 模型的向量被错误混用。
        """
        return self.MODEL_NAME

    def embed_documents(
        self,
        chunks: Sequence[KnowledgeChunk],
    ) -> list[list[float]]:
        if not chunks:
            return []

        texts_to_embed: list[str] = []

        for chunk in chunks:
            if not chunk.text.strip():
                raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError(f"尝试对空文本进行 Embedding。Chunk ID: {chunk.chunk_id}")

            # stored chunk text != embedding representation
            #
            # 数据库存储的 chunk.text 保持原文可读性；
            # 模型输入额外加入 source_title + section，
            # 但绝不修改 KnowledgeChunk 本身。
            embed_input = f"{chunk.source_title}\n{chunk.section}\n{chunk.text}"

            texts_to_embed.append(embed_input)

        try:
            embeddings = self._model.encode(
                texts_to_embed,
                batch_size=self._batch_size,
                show_progress_bar=False,
                normalize_embeddings=True,
                convert_to_numpy=True,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("文档批量 Embedding 推理失败。") from exc

        expected_shape = (
            len(chunks),
            self.DIMENSION,
        )

        if not hasattr(embeddings, "shape") or embeddings.shape != expected_shape:
            actual_shape = getattr(
                embeddings,
                "shape",
                "Unknown",
            )

            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError(f"模型输出的形状异常，预期为 {expected_shape}，实际为 {actual_shape}。")

        try:
            return embeddings.tolist()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("向量格式转换失败。") from exc

    def embed_query(
        self,
        query: str,
    ) -> list[float]:
        if not query.strip():
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("检索查询 (Query) 不能为空。")

        try:
            embedding = self._model.encode(
                query,
                show_progress_bar=False,
                normalize_embeddings=True,
                convert_to_numpy=True,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("查询 Embedding 推理失败。") from exc

        expected_shape = (self.DIMENSION,)

        if not hasattr(embedding, "shape") or embedding.shape != expected_shape:
            actual_shape = getattr(
                embedding,
                "shape",
                "Unknown",
            )

            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError(f"模型输出了错误的形状 {actual_shape}，预期应为 {expected_shape}。")

        try:
            return embedding.tolist()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("向量格式转换失败。") from exc


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\errors.py
================================================================================

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class KnowledgeError(ProjectError):
    """GuanChen Knowledge 子系统异常根类型。"""

    pass


class KnowledgeEmbeddingError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Embedding 模型加载、推理或向量契约异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgePersistenceError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Knowledge persistence / database 基础异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeTransactionError(KnowledgePersistenceError):
    """
    Knowledge document transaction 执行失败。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeRetrievalError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Knowledge retrieval 执行或输入契约异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeRerankerError(KnowledgeRetrievalError):
    """
    Reranker 模型加载、推理或结果契约异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeAgentError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Knowledge Agent 业务契约异常。

    例如：
    - 非法 query
    - 非法 evidence lineage
    - 模型返回未授权 chunk_id
    """

    pass

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\eval_metrics.py

from collections.abc import Collection, Hashable, Sequence
from typing import TypeVar

T = TypeVar("T", bound=Hashable)


def calculate_recall_at_k(
    relevant_items: Collection[T],
    ranked_items: Sequence[T],
    k: int,
) -> float:
    """
    Recall@K

        | relevant ∩ top_k | / | relevant |

    relevance unit 由调用方决定：
    - 正式 Stage 4 Eval：source_title + section
    - Diagnostic：exact chunk_id
    """
    if k <= 0:
        raise ValueError("k 必须大于 0")

    if not relevant_items:
        raise ValueError("Ground Truth 不能为空")

    top_k = ranked_items[:k]
    hits = set(top_k).intersection(relevant_items)

    return len(hits) / len(relevant_items)


def calculate_mrr_at_k(
    relevant_items: Collection[T],
    ranked_items: Sequence[T],
    k: int,
) -> float:
    """
    单个 query 的 Reciprocal Rank@K。

    整个数据集对结果取平均后得到 MRR@K。
    """
    if k <= 0:
        raise ValueError("k 必须大于 0")

    if not relevant_items:
        raise ValueError("Ground Truth 不能为空")

    for rank, item in enumerate(
        ranked_items[:k],
        start=1,
    ):
        if item in relevant_items:
            return 1.0 / rank

    return 0.0


def calculate_percentile(
    latencies_ms: Sequence[float],
    percentile: float,
) -> float:
    """使用线性插值计算百分位数。"""
    if not latencies_ms:
        raise ValueError("没有 latency observation，无法计算 percentile")

    if not 0.0 <= percentile <= 100.0:
        raise ValueError("percentile 必须位于 [0, 100]")

    sorted_latencies = sorted(latencies_ms)

    position = (len(sorted_latencies) - 1) * (percentile / 100.0)

    lower = int(position)
    upper = lower + 1

    if upper >= len(sorted_latencies):
        return sorted_latencies[lower]

    lower_weight = upper - position
    upper_weight = position - lower

    return sorted_latencies[lower] * lower_weight + sorted_latencies[upper] * upper_weight


def calculate_relative_mrr_gain(
    baseline_mrr: float,
    candidate_mrr: float,
) -> float:
    """
    Reranker 相对于 RRF baseline 的 MRR 相对增益：

        (candidate - baseline) / baseline
    """
    if baseline_mrr <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("baseline MRR 必须大于 0，才能计算 relative MRR gain")

    return (candidate_mrr - baseline_mrr) / baseline_mrr

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import FrozenSet, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import RetrievalFilter


class RetrievalEvalCase(BaseModel):
    """
    单条 Retrieval Ground Truth。

    relevant_chunk_ids 的职责：
    1. 作为人工标注 anchor，保证标注能追溯到真实 corpus chunk；
    2. Preflight 时从数据库反查 source_title + section；
    3. source_title + section 才是 Stage 4 正式 Retrieval Gate 的 relevance unit；
    4. exact chunk_id 仅保留为诊断指标。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    case_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    query: str = Field(min_length=1)
    relevant_chunk_ids: FrozenSet[str] = Field(min_length=1)
    filters: Optional[RetrievalFilter] = None
    note: Optional[str] = None

    @field_validator("case_id", "query")
    @classmethod
    def validate_required_text(cls, value: str) -> str:
        normalized = value.strip()
        if not normalized:
            raise ValueError("字段不能为空或纯空格")
        return normalized

    @field_validator("relevant_chunk_ids")
    @classmethod
    def validate_chunk_ids(
        cls,
        value: FrozenSet[str],
    ) -> FrozenSet[str]:
        normalized = frozenset(chunk_id.strip() for chunk_id in value)

        if not normalized:
            raise ValueError("relevant_chunk_ids 不能为空")

        if any(not chunk_id for chunk_id in normalized):
            raise ValueError("relevant_chunk_ids 不能包含纯空格 ID")

        return normalized


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalEvalMetrics:
    """单条 Retrieval Pipeline 的汇总指标。"""

    recall_at_5: float
    mrr_at_10: float
    query_count: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalEvalReport:
    """
    Stage 4 Retrieval Eval 总报告。

    dense / sparse / rrf / reranked：
        正式 Stage 4 指标，relevance unit 为 source_title + section。

    *_exact_chunk_diagnostic：
        精确 chunk_id 诊断指标，不参与 Stage 4 Core Retrieval Gate。

    reranker 字段允许为 None：
        None 表示该 benchmark 没有执行 reranker，
        不能与实际测得的 0.0 混淆。
    """

    query_count: int

    dense: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    sparse: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    rrf: RetrievalEvalMetrics

    dense_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    sparse_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    rrf_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics

    reranked: RetrievalEvalMetrics | None = None
    reranked_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics | None = None

    reranker_relative_mrr_gain: float | None = None
    reranker_added_p50_ms: float | None = None
    reranker_added_p95_ms: float | None = None

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import FrozenSet, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator


class KnowledgeDomain(StrEnum):
    """共享知识库支持的业务知识领域白名单。"""

    BAZI = "bazi"
    YIJING = "yijing"
    LIUYAO = "liuyao"
    DREAM = "dream"
    TRADITIONAL_CULTURE = "traditional_culture"


class SourceType(StrEnum):
    """知识库来源类型边界。"""

    PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC = "public_domain_classic"
    PROJECT_NOTE = "project_note"


def normalize_required_text(value: str) -> str:
    """
    通用基础非空文本校验。

    仅剥离首尾空白，保留内部原有格式，
    包括换行、段落和有意义的空格。
    """
    normalized = value.strip()

    if not normalized:
        raise ValueError("字段不能为空或纯空格")

    return normalized


def canonicalize_text_for_id(value: str) -> str:
    """
    专用于确定性 ID 的文本规范化。

    将连续空白规范为单空格，
    不用于修改实际持久化的 KnowledgeDocument.text
    或 KnowledgeChunk.text。
    """
    canonicalized = " ".join(value.split())

    if not canonicalized:
        raise ValueError("文本规范化后不能为空")

    return canonicalized


def generate_deterministic_document_id(
    domain: str,
    source_title: str,
    section: str,
) -> str:
    """基于领域、书名和章节路径生成稳定逻辑文档 ID。"""
    normalized_domain = normalize_required_text(domain)
    normalized_title = normalize_required_text(source_title)
    normalized_section = normalize_required_text(section)

    content = (f"DOC||{normalized_domain}||{normalized_title}||{normalized_section}").encode("utf-8")

    return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()[:32]


def generate_deterministic_chunk_id(
    document_id: str,
    section: str,
    text: str,
) -> str:
    """
    基于 Document identity 与规范化 Chunk 内容生成确定性 ID。

    whitespace-only 差异经过 canonicalization 后不会改变 ID；
    规范化后的实际内容变化会改变 ID。
    """
    normalized_document_id = normalize_required_text(document_id)
    normalized_section = normalize_required_text(section)
    canonicalized_text = canonicalize_text_for_id(text)

    content = (f"{normalized_document_id}||{normalized_section}||{canonicalized_text}").encode("utf-8")

    return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()[:32]


class KnowledgeDocument(BaseModel):
    """具有稳定来源身份的逻辑知识文档单元。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    document_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str = Field(min_length=1)
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str = Field(min_length=1)
    text: str = Field(min_length=1)

    @field_validator(
        "document_id",
        "source_title",
        "section",
        "text",
        mode="before",
    )
    @classmethod
    def validate_required_fields(cls, value: str) -> str:
        return normalize_required_text(value)

    @classmethod
    def from_parser(
        cls,
        *,
        domain: KnowledgeDomain,
        source_title: str,
        source_type: SourceType,
        section: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> "KnowledgeDocument":
        """Parser 创建 KnowledgeDocument 的标准构造入口。"""
        document_id = generate_deterministic_document_id(
            domain=domain.value,
            source_title=source_title,
            section=section,
        )

        return cls(
            document_id=document_id,
            domain=domain,
            source_title=source_title,
            source_type=source_type,
            section=section,
            text=text,
        )


class KnowledgeChunk(BaseModel):
    """
    Embedding 与 Retrieval 使用的最小知识搜索单元。

    不持有向量、检索分数或 query-time ranking 信息。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    chunk_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    document_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str = Field(min_length=1)
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str = Field(min_length=1)
    text: str = Field(min_length=1)

    @field_validator(
        "chunk_id",
        "document_id",
        "source_title",
        "section",
        "text",
        mode="before",
    )
    @classmethod
    def validate_required_fields(cls, value: str) -> str:
        return normalize_required_text(value)

    @classmethod
    def from_document_part(
        cls,
        *,
        document_id: str,
        domain: KnowledgeDomain,
        source_title: str,
        source_type: SourceType,
        section: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> "KnowledgeChunk":
        """由 KnowledgeDocument 派生 Chunk 的标准构造入口。"""
        normalized_document_id = normalize_required_text(document_id)
        normalized_section = normalize_required_text(section)
        validated_text = normalize_required_text(text)

        chunk_id = generate_deterministic_chunk_id(
            document_id=normalized_document_id,
            section=normalized_section,
            text=validated_text,
        )

        return cls(
            chunk_id=chunk_id,
            document_id=normalized_document_id,
            domain=domain,
            source_title=source_title,
            source_type=source_type,
            section=normalized_section,
            text=validated_text,
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DenseSearchHit:
    """
    Dense Retrieval 返回的内部候选结果。

    score 越大表示相关性越强。
    Embedding 本身不进入该对象。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SparseSearchHit:
    """
    Sparse Retrieval 返回的内部候选结果。

    score 为 BM25 原始分值，不进行跨检索器直接比较。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SparseIndexEntry:
    """SparseRetriever 使用的只读知识快照。"""

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalFilter:
    """
    统一检索过滤器。

    同一字段中的多个值使用 OR 语义；
    不同字段之间使用 AND 语义；
    None 表示该字段不设限制。
    """

    domains: Optional[FrozenSet[KnowledgeDomain]] = None
    source_types: Optional[FrozenSet[SourceType]] = None
    source_titles: Optional[FrozenSet[str]] = None
    sections: Optional[FrozenSet[str]] = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RrfSearchHit:
    """
    RRF 融合后的内部候选结果。

    score 为 RRF 融合分。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float
    dense_rank: Optional[int]
    sparse_rank: Optional[int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RerankedSearchHit:
    """
    Reranker 重排后的内部候选结果。

    score 为 reranker 原始 relevance score。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float
    rrf_score: float
    rrf_rank: int
    dense_rank: Optional[int]
    sparse_rank: Optional[int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalResult:
    """RAG 对上层暴露的稳定最终检索结果。"""

    rank: int
    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    rrf_score: float
    rrf_rank: int
    reranker_score: Optional[float]
    dense_rank: Optional[int]
    sparse_rank: Optional[int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Citation:
    """
    对最终回答暴露的稳定知识来源凭证。

    不包含正文、检索分数或动态引用序号。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    source_title: str
    section: str

    @classmethod
    def from_retrieval_result(
        cls,
        result: RetrievalResult,
    ) -> "Citation":
        return cls(
            chunk_id=result.chunk_id,
            document_id=result.document_id,
            source_title=result.source_title,
            section=result.section,
        )

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\rerank_adapter.py

import math

import torch.nn as nn
from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeRerankerError


class BgeRerankerAdapter:
    """
    BAAI/bge-reranker-base 交叉编码器 (Cross-Encoder) 适配层。
    职责：
    1. 冻结模型语义契约，仅接受 SentenceTransformers 方案。
    2. 抵挡大模型环境所有的第三方报错，转译为安全的业务异常。
    3. 拦截数量不对齐、NaN、无穷大 (Inf) 等大模型幻觉/异常。
    """

    MODEL_NAME = "BAAI/bge-reranker-base"

    def __init__(self, batch_size: int = 4):
        if batch_size < 1:
            raise ValueError("batch_size 必须大于零")
        self._batch_size = batch_size
        self._model = None

    def _get_model(self):
        if self._model is not None:
            return self._model

        try:
            self._model = CrossEncoder(self.MODEL_NAME, activation_fn=nn.Identity())
            return self._model
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError(f"无法加载重排模型 {self.MODEL_NAME}。请检查网络或本地缓存。") from exc

    def score_pairs(self, pairs: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[float]:
        """
        向交叉编码器发送成对的 (query, passage)，获取未归一化的原生打分。
        分数越大代表相关性越高。
        """
        if not pairs:
            return []

        model = self._get_model()

        try:
            raw_scores = model.predict(pairs, batch_size=self._batch_size, show_progress_bar=False)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError("重排模型推理崩溃。") from exc

        try:
            if hasattr(raw_scores, "tolist"):
                scores_list = raw_scores.tolist()
            elif isinstance(raw_scores, list):
                scores_list = raw_scores
            else:
                scores_list = [float(raw_scores)]
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError("无法将重排模型的输出解析为浮点数列表。") from exc

        if len(scores_list) != len(pairs):
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError("重排模型返回的评分数量与输入对数量不匹配！")

        final_scores = []
        for i, s in enumerate(scores_list):
            try:
                score = float(s)
            except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise KnowledgeRerankerError(f"重排模型返回了非标量结构或无法转为浮点数：{s}") from exc

            if not math.isfinite(score):
                raise KnowledgeRerankerError(f"重排模型返回了非法的无穷数值打分 (NaN/Inf)：{score}")

            final_scores.append(score)

        return final_scores

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\casting.py

import secrets
from typing import Literal, Protocol, cast

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.iching_adapter import IChingPyAdapter
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinFace,
    CoinToss,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
)

SixLineValues = tuple[LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue]


class ManualCastingInput(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    method: Literal[CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES] = CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    lines: SixLineValues


class CoinRandomSource(Protocol):
    def toss(self) -> CoinFace: ...


class SystemRandomCoinSource:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._rng = secrets.SystemRandom()

    def toss(self) -> CoinFace:
        return CoinFace.HEAD if self._rng.choice([True, False]) else CoinFace.TAIL


class HexagramCastingService:
    def __init__(self, adapter: IChingPyAdapter, random_source: CoinRandomSource) -> None:
        self._adapter = adapter
        self._random = random_source

    def cast_manual(self, input_data: ManualCastingInput) -> HexagramResult:
        facts = self._adapter.resolve_facts(input_data.lines)
        return HexagramResult(
            casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
            lines=facts.lines,
            primary_hexagram=facts.primary_hexagram,
            changed_hexagram=facts.changed_hexagram,
            moving_line_positions=facts.moving_line_positions,
            coin_tosses=[],
        )

    def cast_random(self) -> HexagramResult:
        tosses = []
        line_values = []

        for pos in range(1, 7):
            coins = (self._random.toss(), self._random.toss(), self._random.toss())
            val_int = sum(3 if c == CoinFace.HEAD else 2 for c in coins)
            lv = LineValue(val_int)

            tosses.append(CoinToss(position=pos, coins=coins, line_value=lv))
            line_values.append(lv)

        canonical_lines = cast(SixLineValues, tuple(line_values))
        facts = self._adapter.resolve_facts(canonical_lines)

        return HexagramResult(
            casting_method=CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
            lines=facts.lines,
            primary_hexagram=facts.primary_hexagram,
            changed_hexagram=facts.changed_hexagram,
            moving_line_positions=facts.moving_line_positions,
            coin_tosses=tosses,
        )

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\checkpoint.py

import uuid
from typing import Any

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoCheckpointError


class LiuyaoCheckpointManager:
    """负责将 LangGraph 的 Thread Reference 同步到 Application Session State"""

    @staticmethod
    def get_or_create_thread(agent_state: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """从状态中获取当前 Thread ID，如果不存在则自动创建一个新的以隔离流程"""
        if not isinstance(agent_state, dict):
            raise LiuyaoCheckpointError("非法的 agent_state 容器类型。")
            
        if "thread_id" not in agent_state:
            agent_state["thread_id"] = f"liuyao_thread_{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
            agent_state.pop("status", None)
            agent_state.pop("pending_node", None)
        return agent_state["thread_id"]

    @staticmethod
    def save_checkpoint_reference(
        agent_state: dict[str, Any],
        thread_id: str,
        status: str,
        pending_node: str | None = None
    ) -> None:
        """更新工作流恢复所需的核心 metadata"""
        agent_state["thread_id"] = thread_id
        agent_state["status"] = status
        
        if pending_node:
            agent_state["pending_node"] = pending_node
        elif "pending_node" in agent_state:
            del agent_state["pending_node"]

    @staticmethod
    def get_thread_id(agent_state: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
        return agent_state.get("thread_id")

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class LiuyaoError(ProjectError):
    """
    GuanChen 六爻子系统异常根类型。
    """

    pass


class HexagramCalculationError(LiuyaoError):
    """
    六爻卦象确定性计算失败。
    当第三方排盘库崩溃或返回非法格式时抛出。
    """

    pass


class LiuyaoPolicyError(LiuyaoError):
    """六爻策略与状态机校验不一致异常"""

    pass

class LiuyaoCheckpointError(LiuyaoError):
    """
    LangGraph thread 创建、恢复或引用无效等持久化异常。
    """
    pass

from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, HexagramResult


def format_hexagram_facts_for_prompt(hexagram: HexagramResult) -> str:
    parts = []

    method_str = "手工指定" if hexagram.casting_method == CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES else "系统三枚硬币"
    parts.append(f"起卦方式：{method_str}")

    p_hex = hexagram.primary_hexagram
    p_name = f" ({p_hex.name}卦)" if p_hex.name else ""
    parts.append(f"\n本卦：上{p_hex.upper_trigram} 下{p_hex.lower_trigram}{p_name}")

    if hexagram.changed_hexagram:
        c_hex = hexagram.changed_hexagram
        c_name = f" ({c_hex.name}卦)" if c_hex.name else ""
        parts.append(f"变卦：上{c_hex.upper_trigram} 下{c_hex.lower_trigram}{c_name}")

    if hexagram.moving_line_positions:
        moves = "、".join([f"第{pos}爻" for pos in hexagram.moving_line_positions])
        parts.append(f"动爻位置：{moves}")
    else:
        parts.append("动爻位置：无动爻 (静卦)")

    parts.append("\n六爻确定性事实 (初爻 -> 上爻)：")
    for line in hexagram.lines:
        line_parts = [f"第{line.position}爻: {line.polarity.value}", "动" if line.moving else "静"]
        if line.heavenly_stem and line.earthly_branch:
            line_parts.append(f"干支={line.heavenly_stem}{line.earthly_branch}")
        if line.element:
            line_parts.append(f"五行={line.element}")
        if line.six_relative:
            line_parts.append(f"六亲={line.six_relative}")
        if line.is_shi:
            line_parts.append("[世爻]")
        if line.is_ying:
            line_parts.append("[应爻]")

        parts.append(" | ".join(line_parts))

    return "\n".join(parts)


def format_evidence_for_prompt(results: list) -> str:
    if not results:
        return "当前未检索到相关传统证据支持。"

    parts = []
    for idx, res in enumerate(results, 1):
        parts.append(f"[Evidence {idx}]\nchunk_id: {res.chunk_id}\nsource_title: {res.source_title}\ntext: {res.text}\n")
    return "\n".join(parts)


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\iching_adapter.py
================================================================================

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import HexagramCalculationError
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)


class VendorLineFacts(BaseModel):
    """
    从 ichingpy SixLinesDivinationEngine 标准化出的单爻事实。

    six_spirit 当前 ichingpy 0.1.10 未提供，因此保持 None。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    heavenly_stem: str
    earthly_branch: str
    element: str
    six_relative: str

    six_spirit: str | None = None

    is_shi: bool = False
    is_ying: bool = False


class HexagramFacts(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    lines: tuple[HexagramLine, ...]
    primary_hexagram: HexagramIdentity
    changed_hexagram: HexagramIdentity | None
    moving_line_positions: list[int]


class IChingPyAdapter:
    """
    GuanChen 与 ichingpy 之间的唯一适配边界。

    项目内部只使用：
        LineValue(6/7/8/9)
        HexagramResult / HexagramLine

    ichingpy 的对象结构不会泄漏到业务层。
    """

    def _resolve_vendor_hexagram(
        self,
        line_values: tuple[LineValue, ...],
    ) -> tuple[HexagramIdentity, tuple[VendorLineFacts, ...]]:
        """
        唯一正式 vendor path：

        LineValue
        -> ichingpy.LineStatus
        -> Line / Trigram / Hexagram
        -> SixLinesDivinationEngine.execute()
        -> hexagram.interpretation.lines
        -> GuanChen normalized facts
        """
        import ichingpy
        from ichingpy.enum.role import HexagramRole

        if len(line_values) != 6:
            raise HexagramCalculationError("ichingpy 六爻构造输入必须正好包含 6 爻")

        status_map = {
            LineValue.OLD_YIN: ichingpy.LineStatus.CHANGING_YIN,
            LineValue.YOUNG_YANG: ichingpy.LineStatus.STATIC_YANG,
            LineValue.YOUNG_YIN: ichingpy.LineStatus.STATIC_YIN,
            LineValue.OLD_YANG: ichingpy.LineStatus.CHANGING_YANG,
        }

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 1. 构造真实 ichingpy Hexagram
        # 顺序保持 bottom -> top：
        # lines[:3] = 初、二、三爻（内卦）
        # lines[3:] = 四、五、上爻（外卦）
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        vendor_lines = [ichingpy.Line(status=status_map[value]) for value in line_values]

        hexagram = ichingpy.Hexagram(
            inner=ichingpy.Trigram(lines=vendor_lines[:3]),
            outer=ichingpy.Trigram(lines=vendor_lines[3:]),
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 2. 六爻装卦 / 纳甲 / 六亲 / 世应
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        engine = ichingpy.SixLinesDivinationEngine()
        engine.execute(hexagram)

        interpretation = hexagram.interpretation
        if interpretation is None:
            raise HexagramCalculationError("ichingpy 未生成六爻装卦结果")

        interpreted_lines = list(interpretation.lines)

        if len(interpreted_lines) != 6:
            raise HexagramCalculationError(f"ichingpy 装卦结果异常：预期 6 爻，实际返回 {len(interpreted_lines)} 爻")

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 3. 卦象 Identity
        #
        # ichingpy Trigram 有正式 name，例如：
        # 乾 / 兑 / 离 / 震 / 巽 / 坎 / 艮 / 坤
        #
        # SixLinesDivinationEngine 本身不提供稳定的完整六十四卦名，
        # 所以 name 保持 None，不制造伪卦名。
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        identity = HexagramIdentity(
            name=None,
            upper_trigram=hexagram.outer.name,
            lower_trigram=hexagram.inner.name,
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 4. 从 SixLineLineInterp 提取真实装卦字段
        #
        # 注意：
        # 数据在 hexagram.interpretation.lines，
        # 不在原始 hexagram.inner.lines / outer.lines 上。
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        normalized_lines: list[VendorLineFacts] = []

        for line_interp in interpreted_lines:
            stem = line_interp.stem
            branch = line_interp.branch
            relative = line_interp.relative
            role = line_interp.role

            normalized_lines.append(
                VendorLineFacts(
                    heavenly_stem=stem.label,
                    earthly_branch=branch.label,
                    element=branch.phase.label,
                    six_relative=relative.label,
                    # ichingpy 0.1.10 当前没有六神数据
                    six_spirit=None,
                    is_shi=role == HexagramRole.SUBJECT,
                    is_ying=role == HexagramRole.OBJECT,
                )
            )

        return identity, tuple(normalized_lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _to_changed_line_value(value: LineValue) -> LineValue:
        """
        动爻变换的 canonical 规则。

        老阴 6 -> 少阳 7
        老阳 9 -> 少阴 8
        静爻保持不变
        """
        if value == LineValue.OLD_YIN:
            return LineValue.YOUNG_YANG

        if value == LineValue.OLD_YANG:
            return LineValue.YOUNG_YIN

        return value

    def resolve_facts(
        self,
        line_values: tuple[LineValue, ...],
    ) -> HexagramFacts:
        try:
            if len(line_values) != 6:
                raise HexagramCalculationError("六爻输入必须正好包含 6 爻")

            # -------------------------------------------------
            # 1. 本卦 + 装卦事实
            # -------------------------------------------------
            primary_identity, primary_line_facts = self._resolve_vendor_hexagram(line_values)

            hexagram_lines: list[HexagramLine] = []
            moving_positions: list[int] = []

            for position, (value, vendor_fact) in enumerate(
                zip(
                    line_values,
                    primary_line_facts,
                    strict=True,
                ),
                start=1,
            ):
                moving = value in {
                    LineValue.OLD_YIN,
                    LineValue.OLD_YANG,
                }

                polarity = (
                    YinYang.YIN
                    if value
                    in {
                        LineValue.OLD_YIN,
                        LineValue.YOUNG_YIN,
                    }
                    else YinYang.YANG
                )

                if moving:
                    moving_positions.append(position)

                hexagram_lines.append(
                    HexagramLine(
                        position=position,
                        value=value,
                        polarity=polarity,
                        moving=moving,
                        heavenly_stem=vendor_fact.heavenly_stem,
                        earthly_branch=vendor_fact.earthly_branch,
                        element=vendor_fact.element,
                        six_relative=vendor_fact.six_relative,
                        six_spirit=vendor_fact.six_spirit,
                        is_shi=vendor_fact.is_shi,
                        is_ying=vendor_fact.is_ying,
                    )
                )

            # -------------------------------------------------
            # 2. 变卦
            #
            # 仅执行最小 canonical line transformation，
            # 然后重新交给同一个 ichingpy path 构造卦象。
            # 不维护任何六十四卦映射表。
            # -------------------------------------------------
            changed_identity: HexagramIdentity | None = None

            if moving_positions:
                changed_line_values = tuple(self._to_changed_line_value(value) for value in line_values)

                changed_identity, _ = self._resolve_vendor_hexagram(changed_line_values)

            return HexagramFacts(
                lines=tuple(hexagram_lines),
                primary_hexagram=primary_identity,
                changed_hexagram=changed_identity,
                moving_line_positions=moving_positions,
            )

        except HexagramCalculationError:
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise HexagramCalculationError("六爻卦象构造与装卦推演失败") from exc


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\policy.py
================================================================================

from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import LiuyaoState
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    DecisionQuestion,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    NonBlankText,
)


class ConfirmationAction(StrEnum):
    CONFIRM = "confirm"
    MODIFY = "modify"
    CANCEL = "cancel"


class ConfirmationResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    action: ConfirmationAction
    modification: NonBlankText | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_action_payload(self) -> "ConfirmationResponse":
        if self.action != ConfirmationAction.MODIFY and self.modification is not None:
            raise ValueError("只有 MODIFY action 可以携带 modification")
        return self


class ClarificationAssessment(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    needs_clarification: bool
    clarified_question: NonBlankText | None = None
    reality_constraints: list[NonBlankText] = Field(default_factory=list)
    clarification_prompt: NonBlankText | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_assessment(self) -> "ClarificationAssessment":
        if self.needs_clarification:
            if self.clarification_prompt is None:
                raise ValueError("需要澄清时必须提供 clarification_prompt")
            if self.clarified_question is not None:
                raise ValueError("尚需澄清时不能提前形成 clarified_question")
        else:
            if self.clarified_question is None:
                raise ValueError("可进入确认时必须存在 clarified_question")
            if self.clarification_prompt is not None:
                raise ValueError("已形成候选问题时不能继续携带 clarification_prompt")
        return self


def rebuild_question(
    question: DecisionQuestion,
    **updates: Any,
) -> DecisionQuestion:
    """使用完整 model_validator 重新校验并构建 DecisionQuestion"""
    payload = question.model_dump()
    payload.update(updates)
    return DecisionQuestion.model_validate(payload)


def invalidate_downstream(state: LiuyaoState) -> LiuyaoState:
    """统一的下游结果失效清空 helper，修改问题会导致所有后续资产失效"""
    return {
        **state,
        "confirmed": False,
        "hexagram": None,
        "retrieved_context": [],
        "reflection": None,
        "citations": [],
        "final_response": None,
    }


def project_confirmed(question: DecisionQuestion) -> bool:
    """confirmed 状态永远由 question.status 确定性投影"""
    return question.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED


def validate_confirmation_projection(state: LiuyaoState) -> None:
    """State 一致性 Guard：防止 status 与 confirmed 出现孤立脱节"""
    question: DecisionQuestion | None = state.get("question")
    if not question:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError("六爻状态机中缺少 DecisionQuestion")

    expected = project_confirmed(question)
    if state.get("confirmed", False) != expected:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError("六爻确认状态不一致")


def can_cast(state: LiuyaoState) -> bool:
    """
    确定性起卦硬门槛：
    只有 question.status == CONFIRMED 且 confirmed projection 一致时才允许起卦。
    """
    validate_confirmation_projection(state)
    question: DecisionQuestion = state["question"]
    return question.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED


def apply_clarification_assessment(
    state: LiuyaoState,
    assessment: ClarificationAssessment,
) -> LiuyaoState:
    """根据 LLM 的 ClarificationAssessment 驱动状态机更新"""
    validate_confirmation_projection(state)
    question: DecisionQuestion = state["question"]

    # 防止非草稿/非澄清阶段直接通过评估结果重写状态，绕过显式 MODIFY 语义
    if question.status not in {
        DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT,
        DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
    }:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {question.status} 不允许直接应用澄清结果")

    # 若之前已处于非 DRAFT 状态，新 proposal 产生时需要先清理下游
    cleaned_state = invalidate_downstream(state)

    if assessment.needs_clarification:
        new_q = rebuild_question(
            question,
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
            clarified_question=None,
            reality_constraints=assessment.reality_constraints,
        )
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "clarification": assessment.clarification_prompt,
            "pending_user_input": None,  # 无论何种分支，澄清一旦成功应用，均视为已消费修改意见
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }
    else:
        new_q = rebuild_question(
            question,
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            clarified_question=assessment.clarified_question,
            reality_constraints=assessment.reality_constraints,
        )
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "clarification": None,
            "pending_user_input": None,  # 成功形成候选问题，清空 pending 缓冲
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }


def apply_confirmation_response(
    state: LiuyaoState,
    response: ConfirmationResponse,
) -> LiuyaoState:
    """根据用户的 ConfirmationResponse 驱动状态机更新（CONFIRM / MODIFY / CANCEL）"""
    validate_confirmation_projection(state)
    question: DecisionQuestion = state["question"]
    current_status = question.status

    # 1. 处理 CONFIRM
    if response.action == ConfirmationAction.CONFIRM:
        if current_status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED:
            return state
        if current_status != DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {current_status} 不允许直接确认")

        new_q = rebuild_question(question, status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED)
        return {
            **state,
            "question": new_q,
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }

    # 2. 处理 MODIFY
    elif response.action == ConfirmationAction.MODIFY:
        if current_status not in {
            DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        }:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {current_status} 不支持修改")

        cleaned_state = invalidate_downstream(state)
        new_q = rebuild_question(
            question,
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
            clarified_question=None,
        )

        modification = response.modification
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "clarification": (None if modification is not None else "请问您希望如何修改或补充当前问题？"),
            "pending_user_input": modification,
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }

    # 3. 处理 CANCEL
    elif response.action == ConfirmationAction.CANCEL:
        if current_status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED:
            return state
        if current_status not in {
            DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        }:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {current_status} 无法取消")

        cleaned_state = invalidate_downstream(state)
        new_q = rebuild_question(question, status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED)
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }

    raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"未知的确认动作: {response.action}")


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\retrieval.py
================================================================================

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, RetrievalFilter, RetrievalResult
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import HexagramResult

LIUYAO_RETRIEVAL_FILTER = RetrievalFilter(domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.LIUYAO, KnowledgeDomain.YIJING}))


def build_liuyao_queries(question_text: str, hexagram: HexagramResult) -> list[str]:
    queries = []

    # 1. 本卦查询
    p_hex = hexagram.primary_hexagram
    q1 = f"{p_hex.upper_trigram}上{p_hex.lower_trigram}下"
    if p_hex.name:
        q1 += f" {p_hex.name}卦"
    queries.append(f"卦象含义 {q1}")

    # 2. 动爻查询
    if hexagram.moving_line_positions:
        moves = "、".join([f"第{pos}爻动" for pos in hexagram.moving_line_positions])
        c_hex = hexagram.changed_hexagram
        c_str = f"变卦{c_hex.upper_trigram}上{c_hex.lower_trigram}下" if c_hex else ""
        queries.append(f"爻辞与动爻解析 {moves} {c_str}".strip())

    # 3. 事象映射
    queries.append(f"六爻决策参考 测事 测{question_text}")

    return queries


class LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder:
    def __init__(self, retrieval_service: RetrievalService):
        self._retrieval_service = retrieval_service

    async def retrieve(self, question_text: str, hexagram: HexagramResult) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
        queries = build_liuyao_queries(question_text, hexagram)
        all_results = []
        seen = set()

        for query in queries:
            results = await self._retrieval_service.retrieve(query, filters=LIUYAO_RETRIEVAL_FILTER)
            for res in results:
                if res.chunk_id not in seen:
                    seen.add(res.chunk_id)
                    all_results.append(res)

        return all_results


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\state.py
================================================================================

from typing import TypedDict

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation, RetrievalResult
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, DecisionQuestion, DecisionReflection, HexagramResult, LineValue


class LiuyaoState(TypedDict):
    """
    LangGraph 工作流的运行时状态 (Orchestration State)。
    """

    # 业务侧的 Source of Truth
    question: DecisionQuestion

    # 当前等待用户回答的系统澄清提示语（不需要保留历史）
    clarification: str | None

    # Transient Field：用于搭载 MODIFY 后用户修改/补充的内容，供下轮 clarify 消费
    pending_user_input: str | None

    # Routing 投影，必须始终保持与 question.status == CONFIRMED 同步
    confirmed: bool

    # 起卦策略，连接 Task 2 的确定性执行
    casting_method: CastingMethod
    manual_lines: tuple[LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue] | None

    # 确定性代码产生的排盘事实
    hexagram: HexagramResult | None

    # Transient: 传递给 Interpretation 节点的完整证据原文
    retrieved_context: list[RetrievalResult]

    # Transient: LLM 生成的结构化反思草稿
    reflection: DecisionReflection | None

    # 标准的引文溯源
    citations: list[Citation]

    # 最终发送给用户的决策反思文本
    final_response: str | None


def create_initial_liuyao_state(original_question: str) -> LiuyaoState:
    """
    纯函数：创建初始的六爻工作流状态。
    """
    question = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question=original_question,
    )

    return {
        "question": question,
        "clarification": None,
        "pending_user_input": None,
        "confirmed": False,
        "casting_method": CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
        "manual_lines": None,
        "hexagram": None,
        "retrieved_context": [],
        "reflection": None,
        "citations": [],
        "final_response": None,
    }

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\workflow.py

import re

from langgraph.checkpoint.base import BaseCheckpointSaver
from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver
from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
from langgraph.types import interrupt
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.casting import HexagramCastingService, ManualCastingInput
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.formatter import format_evidence_for_prompt, format_hexagram_facts_for_prompt
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import (
    ClarificationAssessment,
    ConfirmationResponse,
    apply_clarification_assessment,
    apply_confirmation_response,
    can_cast,
)
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.retrieval import LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import LiuyaoState
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, DecisionQuestionStatus, DecisionReflection
from guanchen_v2.prompts.liuyao_agent import (
    LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_USER_TEMPLATE,
    LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE,
)

_EVIDENCE_REF_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\[Evidence\s+(\d+)\]")


def validate_evidence_references(reflection: DecisionReflection, citations: list[Citation]) -> None:
    """验证 DecisionReflection 中 [Evidence N] 的引用是否真实存在"""
    refs = {int(match) for match in _EVIDENCE_REF_PATTERN.findall(reflection.traditional_reflection)}

    max_index = len(citations)
    invalid_refs = sorted(ref for ref in refs if ref < 1 or ref > max_index)

    if invalid_refs:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError("DecisionReflection 包含不存在的 Evidence 引用")


class LiuyaoAgentRuntime:
    def __init__(
        self,
        casting_service: HexagramCastingService,
        llm_client: LLMClient,
        retrieval_builder: LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder,
        checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver | None = None
    ):
        self._casting_service = casting_service
        self._llm_client = llm_client
        self._retrieval_builder = retrieval_builder
        self._checkpointer = checkpointer or InMemorySaver()

    async def clarify_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        q = state["question"]
        pending = state.get("pending_user_input", "")

        user_prompt = LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            original_question=q.original_question,
            reality_constraints="、".join(q.reality_constraints) if q.reality_constraints else "无",
            pending_user_input=pending if pending else "无",
        )

        assessment = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
            system_prompt=LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt=user_prompt, schema=ClarificationAssessment
        )

        return apply_clarification_assessment(state, assessment)

    def confirmation_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        q = state["question"]
        resume_payload = interrupt(
            {
                "type": "liuyao_confirmation",
                "question": q.clarified_question,
                "reality_constraints": q.reality_constraints,
                "allowed_actions": ["confirm", "modify", "cancel"],
            }
        )
        try:
            response = ConfirmationResponse.model_validate(resume_payload)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("无效的六爻确认响应") from exc
        return apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    def cast_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        if not can_cast(state):
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("状态机未就绪或未通过用户确认，拒绝执行起卦操作")

        method = state.get("casting_method")
        if method == CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES:
            manual_lines = state.get("manual_lines")
            if manual_lines is None or len(manual_lines) != 6:
                raise LiuyaoPolicyError("手工起卦必须提供完整 manual_lines")
            hexagram = self._casting_service.cast_manual(ManualCastingInput(lines=manual_lines))
        elif method == CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN:
            hexagram = self._casting_service.cast_random()
        else:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"不支持的起卦方式: {method}")

        return {"hexagram": hexagram}

    async def retrieve_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        if not can_cast(state):
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("未通过确认检查，禁止检索")

        hexagram = state.get("hexagram")
        if not hexagram:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少 HexagramResult，无法执行六爻知识检索")

        q = state["question"]
        if q.clarified_question is None:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少已确认的 clarified_question，无法执行六爻知识检索")

        results = await self._retrieval_builder.retrieve(q.clarified_question, hexagram)
        citations = [Citation.from_retrieval_result(r) for r in results]

        return {"retrieved_context": results, "citations": citations}

    async def interpret_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        if not can_cast(state):
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("未通过确认检查，禁止生成六爻决策反思")

        q = state["question"]
        hexagram = state.get("hexagram")

        if hexagram is None:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少 HexagramResult，无法生成六爻决策反思")
        if q.clarified_question is None:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少已确认的 clarified_question")

        evidence_results = state.get("retrieved_context", [])

        facts_str = format_hexagram_facts_for_prompt(hexagram)
        evidence_str = format_evidence_for_prompt(evidence_results)
        constraints_str = "、".join(q.reality_constraints) if q.reality_constraints else "无"

        user_prompt = LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            question=q.clarified_question,
            reality_constraints=constraints_str,
            hexagram_facts=facts_str,
            evidence=evidence_str,
        )

        reflection = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
            system_prompt=LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt=user_prompt, schema=DecisionReflection
        )
        return {"reflection": reflection}

    def finalize_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        reflection = state.get("reflection")
        if not reflection:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺失 DecisionReflection，无法渲染最终回复")

        citations = state.get("citations", [])

        # 确定性防伪造校验
        validate_evidence_references(reflection, citations)

        parts = [
            f"### 【现状评估】\n{reflection.situation_summary}\n",
            f"### 【文化推演】\n{reflection.traditional_reflection}\n",
            f"### 【现实校验】\n{reflection.reality_check}\n",
            "### 【决策参考】",
        ]

        for opt in reflection.decision_options:
            parts.append(f"- {opt}")

        parts.append(f"\n> *注：{reflection.uncertainty_note}*")

        if citations:
            parts.append("\n### 【参考来源】")
            for idx, cite in enumerate(citations, start=1):
                parts.append(f"[Evidence {idx}] {cite.source_title} - {cite.section}")

        return {"final_response": "\n".join(parts)}

    def route_after_clarify(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> str:
        status = state["question"].status
        if status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return END
        elif status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION:
            return "confirmation"

        raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"clarify 后出现非法状态 (状态机断裂): {status}")

    def route_after_confirmation(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> str:
        status = state["question"].status
        if status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED:
            return "cast"
        elif status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return "clarify"
        elif status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED:
            return END

        raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"confirmation 后出现非法状态 (状态机断裂): {status}")

    def build_graph(self) -> StateGraph:
        builder = StateGraph(LiuyaoState)

        builder.add_node("clarify", self.clarify_node)
        builder.add_node("confirmation", self.confirmation_node)
        builder.add_node("cast", self.cast_node)
        builder.add_node("retrieve", self.retrieve_node)
        builder.add_node("interpret", self.interpret_node)
        builder.add_node("finalize", self.finalize_node)

        builder.add_edge(START, "clarify")

        builder.add_conditional_edges("clarify", self.route_after_clarify)
        builder.add_conditional_edges("confirmation", self.route_after_confirmation)

        builder.add_edge("cast", "retrieve")
        builder.add_edge("retrieve", "interpret")
        builder.add_edge("interpret", "finalize")
        builder.add_edge("finalize", END)

        return builder.compile(checkpointer=self._checkpointer)

from typing import Any, TypeVar

from langchain_core.exceptions import OutputParserException
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda
from openai import APIConnectionError, APIError, APIStatusError
from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMEmptyResponseError,
    LLMError,
    LLMProviderError,
    LLMResponseError,
    LLMRetryExhaustedError,
    LLMStructuredOutputError,
    LLMTransientProviderError,
)
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model

StructuredT = TypeVar(
    "StructuredT",
    bound=BaseModel,
)


def _translate_provider_error(
    exc: Exception,
) -> LLMError:
    """
    将第三方异常转换为 GuanChen 稳定 LLM 异常。

    重要约束：
    - 不把 str(exc) 拼入稳定项目错误消息；
    - Provider 原始异常通过 exception chaining 保存在 __cause__；
    - 对外消息只表达应用需要知道的错误类别。
    """
    if isinstance(
        exc,
        (
            OutputParserException,
            ValidationError,
        ),
    ):
        return LLMStructuredOutputError("结构化输出失败")

    if isinstance(
        exc,
        APIConnectionError,
    ):
        return LLMTransientProviderError("LLM 网络连接失败")

    if isinstance(
        exc,
        APIStatusError,
    ):
        if (
            exc.status_code
            in (
                408,
                409,
                429,
            )
            or exc.status_code >= 500
        ):
            return LLMTransientProviderError("LLM 服务暂时不可用")

        return LLMProviderError("LLM 服务请求失败")

    if isinstance(
        exc,
        APIError,
    ):
        return LLMProviderError("LLM API 调用失败")

    return LLMProviderError("LLM 调用失败")


class LLMClient:
    def __init__(
        self,
        model: BaseChatModel,
        max_retries: int = 0,
        *,
        retry_wait_exponential_jitter: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self._model = model
        self._max_retries = max_retries
        self._retry_jitter = retry_wait_exponential_jitter

    @classmethod
    def from_settings(
        cls,
        settings: Settings,
    ) -> "LLMClient":
        model = build_chat_model(settings=settings)

        return cls(
            model=model,
            max_retries=(settings.llm_max_retries),
        )

    def _invoke_text_once(
        self,
        prompt: str,
    ) -> str:
        try:
            response = self._model.invoke(prompt)

            if not isinstance(
                response.content,
                str,
            ):
                raise LLMResponseError("模型调用返回了非文本内容")

            if not response.content.split():
                raise LLMEmptyResponseError("返回了空字符串结果")

            return response.content

        except (
            LLMResponseError,
            LLMEmptyResponseError,
        ):
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise _translate_provider_error(exc) from exc

    def text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
    ) -> str:
        runnable = RunnableLambda(self._invoke_text_once).with_retry(
            retry_if_exception_type=(
                LLMTransientProviderError,
                LLMEmptyResponseError,
            ),
            wait_exponential_jitter=(self._retry_jitter),
            stop_after_attempt=(self._max_retries + 1),
        )

        try:
            return runnable.invoke(prompt)

        except LLMTransientProviderError as exc:
            raise LLMRetryExhaustedError("文本生成重试次数已耗尽") from exc

    def _invoke_structured_once(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        prompt: str = payload["prompt"]
        schema: type[StructuredT] = payload["schema"]

        try:
            structured_llm = self._model.with_structured_output(schema)

            result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)

            if result is None:
                raise LLMEmptyResponseError("结构化输出返回了空结果")

            if not isinstance(
                result,
                schema,
            ):
                raise LLMStructuredOutputError(f"结构化输出类型不匹配，预期 Schema: {schema.__name__}")

            return result

        except (
            LLMEmptyResponseError,
            LLMStructuredOutputError,
        ):
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise _translate_provider_error(exc) from exc

    def structured(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        schema: type[StructuredT],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        runnable = RunnableLambda(self._invoke_structured_once).with_retry(
            retry_if_exception_type=(
                LLMTransientProviderError,
                LLMEmptyResponseError,
                LLMStructuredOutputError,
            ),
            wait_exponential_jitter=(self._retry_jitter),
            stop_after_attempt=(self._max_retries + 1),
        )

        try:
            return runnable.invoke(
                {
                    "prompt": prompt,
                    "schema": schema,
                }
            )

        except LLMTransientProviderError as exc:
            raise LLMRetryExhaustedError("结构化输出重试次数已耗尽") from exc

    async def _ainvoke_structured_once(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        system_prompt: str = payload["system_prompt"]
        user_prompt: str = payload["user_prompt"]
        schema: type[StructuredT] = payload["schema"]

        messages = [
            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
            HumanMessage(content=user_prompt),
        ]

        try:
            structured_llm = self._model.with_structured_output(schema)

            result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(messages)

            if result is None:
                raise LLMEmptyResponseError("结构化输出返回了空结果")

            if not isinstance(
                result,
                schema,
            ):
                raise LLMStructuredOutputError(f"结构化输出类型不匹配，预期 Schema: {schema.__name__}")

            return result

        except (
            LLMEmptyResponseError,
            LLMStructuredOutputError,
        ):
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise _translate_provider_error(exc) from exc

    async def generate_structured(
        self,
        *,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        schema: type[StructuredT],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        runnable = RunnableLambda(self._ainvoke_structured_once).with_retry(
            retry_if_exception_type=(
                LLMTransientProviderError,
                LLMEmptyResponseError,
                LLMStructuredOutputError,
            ),
            wait_exponential_jitter=(self._retry_jitter),
            stop_after_attempt=(self._max_retries + 1),
        )

        try:
            return await runnable.ainvoke(
                {
                    "system_prompt": (system_prompt),
                    "user_prompt": (user_prompt),
                    "schema": schema,
                }
            )

        except LLMTransientProviderError as exc:
            raise LLMRetryExhaustedError("结构化输出重试次数已耗尽") from exc

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class LLMError(ProjectError):
    """
    GuanChen LLM 基础设施异常根类型。

    所有稳定的大模型调用异常都应继承该类型。
    """

    pass


class LLMProviderError(LLMError):
    """
    LLM Provider 不可重试错误。

    例如：
    - 非瞬时 API 请求错误
    - 非法模型配置
    - Provider 明确拒绝请求
    """

    pass


class LLMTransientProviderError(LLMProviderError):
    """
    LLM Provider 瞬时错误。

    例如：
    - 网络连接失败
    - 429
    - 5xx
    - 临时服务不可用

    Retry policy 由 LLMClient 负责。
    """

    pass


class LLMResponseError(LLMError):
    """
    Provider 调用已经完成，
    但返回结果不满足 GuanChen 应用层契约。
    """

    pass


class LLMEmptyResponseError(LLMResponseError):
    """LLM 没有返回应用期望的有效内容。"""

    pass


class LLMStructuredOutputError(LLMResponseError):
    """LLM structured output 不满足目标 Schema。"""

    pass


class LLMRetryExhaustedError(LLMError):
    """可重试错误达到最大尝试次数后仍未恢复。"""

    pass
