from typing import Sequence

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeRetrievalError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import DenseSearchHit, RrfSearchHit, SparseSearchHit

# RRF 平滑常数
RRF_K = 60


class ReciprocalRankFusion:
    """
    倒数秩融合器。
    职责：
    1. 不碰数据库，不读 Query，完全隔离 I/O。
    2. 基于 1-based 排名计算 RRF，彻底摒弃原生底层的 Score (0 分或无限大皆不受影响)。
    3. - 单一阵营内绝对不允许重复 chunk_id。
       - 跨阵营对齐：同一 chunk_id 必须拥有绝对一致的 Metadata，否则立即触发崩溃以暴露快照脏数据。
    4. 执行 Deterministic 的 Tie-break (score DESC, chunk_id ASC)。
    """

    def _verify_metadata_consistency(self, hit_a: DenseSearchHit, hit_b: SparseSearchHit) -> None:
        """断言同一个 Chunk_ID 在不同阵营提交上来的数据必须如出一辙。"""
        if (
            hit_a.document_id != hit_b.document_id
            or hit_a.domain != hit_b.domain
            or hit_a.source_title != hit_b.source_title
            or hit_a.source_type != hit_b.source_type
            or hit_a.section != hit_b.section
            or hit_a.text != hit_b.text
        ):
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError(f"RRF 数据校验崩溃：来自两路的 chunk_id [{hit_a.chunk_id}] 元数据出现脏乱分歧！")

    def fuse(
        self,
        dense_hits: Sequence[DenseSearchHit],
        sparse_hits: Sequence[SparseSearchHit],
        limit: int,
    ) -> list[RrfSearchHit]:
        """执行名次聚合、校验与截断。"""
        if limit <= 0:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("RRF 融合后的检索数量必须大于 0。")

        # 以 chunk_id 为唯一识别枢纽构建聚合站
        # 结构: chunk_id -> dict(hit_obj, dense_rank, sparse_rank, score)
        fusion_map: dict[str, dict] = {}

        # -----------------------------------------------
        # 处理 Dense  (强转为 1-based rank)
        # -----------------------------------------------
        for rank, hit in enumerate(dense_hits, start=1):
            if hit.chunk_id in fusion_map:
                raise KnowledgeRetrievalError(f"RRF 数据校验崩溃：Dense 内出现了重复提交的 chunk_id [{hit.chunk_id}]。")

            fusion_map[hit.chunk_id] = {
                "base_obj": hit,
                "dense_rank": rank,
                "sparse_rank": None,
                "rrf_score": 1.0 / (RRF_K + rank),
            }

        # -----------------------------------------------
        # 处理 Sparse  (并接管与 Dense 的跨阵营核对)
        # -----------------------------------------------
        for rank, hit in enumerate(sparse_hits, start=1):
            record = fusion_map.get(hit.chunk_id)

            if record:
                if record["sparse_rank"] is not None:
                    raise KnowledgeRetrievalError(f"RRF 数据校验崩溃：Sparse 内出现了重复交卷的 chunk_id [{hit.chunk_id}]。")

                self._verify_metadata_consistency(record["base_obj"], hit)
                record["sparse_rank"] = rank
                record["rrf_score"] += 1.0 / (RRF_K + rank)
            else:
                fusion_map[hit.chunk_id] = {
                    "base_obj": hit,
                    "dense_rank": None,
                    "sparse_rank": rank,
                    "rrf_score": 1.0 / (RRF_K + rank),
                }

        # 根据 RRF_score 降序，发生平局则按 chunk_id 升序进行强制打断
        fused_records = sorted(fusion_map.items(), key=lambda x: (-x[1]["rrf_score"], x[0]))

        final_hits = []
        for chunk_id, data in fused_records[:limit]:
            obj = data["base_obj"]
            hit = RrfSearchHit(
                chunk_id=chunk_id,
                document_id=obj.document_id,
                domain=obj.domain,
                source_title=obj.source_title,
                source_type=obj.source_type,
                section=obj.section,
                text=obj.text,
                score=data["rrf_score"],
                dense_rank=data["dense_rank"],
                sparse_rank=data["sparse_rank"],
            )
            final_hits.append(hit)

        return final_hits

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\retrieval\service.py
================================================================================

from typing import Optional

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeRetrievalError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import RetrievalFilter, RetrievalResult
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.assembler import RetrievalResultAssembler
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.rerank import KnowledgeReranker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.rrf import ReciprocalRankFusion
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import SparseRetriever


class RetrievalService:
    """
    GuanChen 统一知识检索底座。
    职责：
    1. 固化检索流水线：Dense + Sparse -> RRF -> (Optional) Reranker -> Assembler。
    2. 对外隐藏一切内部策略参数（海选池大小、是否开启重排等）。
    3. 绝不做智能化包装（如改写 Query、揣测 Filter、二次检索）。
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        dense_retriever: DenseRetriever,
        sparse_retriever: SparseRetriever,
        rrf: ReciprocalRankFusion,
        reranker: KnowledgeReranker,
        assembler: RetrievalResultAssembler,
        candidate_limit: int = 20,
        result_limit: int = 5,
        reranker_enabled: bool = False,
    ):
        self._dense = dense_retriever
        self._sparse = sparse_retriever
        self._rrf = rrf
        self._reranker = reranker
        self._assembler = assembler

        if candidate_limit <= 0 or result_limit <= 0:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("候选项数量与结果数量必须大于 0。")
        if candidate_limit < result_limit:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("海选数量 (candidate_limit) 必须大于等于最终结果数量 (result_limit)。")

        self._candidate_limit = candidate_limit
        self._result_limit = result_limit
        self._reranker_enabled = reranker_enabled

    async def retrieve(
        self,
        query: str,
        filters: Optional[RetrievalFilter] = None,
    ) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
        if not query.strip():
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("检索查询 (Query) 不能为空。")

        # Sparse 为同步内存操作，Dense 为异步 DB 操作
        dense_hits = await self._dense.search(query, limit=self._candidate_limit, filters=filters)
        sparse_hits = self._sparse.search(query, limit=self._candidate_limit, filters=filters)

        rrf_hits = self._rrf.fuse(dense_hits, sparse_hits, limit=self._candidate_limit)
        if not rrf_hits:
            return []

        if self._reranker_enabled:
            reranked_hits = self._reranker.rerank(query, rrf_hits, limit=self._result_limit)
            return self._assembler.from_reranked(reranked_hits)
        else:
            final_rrf_hits = rrf_hits[: self._result_limit]
            return self._assembler.from_rrf(final_rrf_hits)


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\retrieval\sparse.py
================================================================================

import unicodedata
from typing import Any, Optional

import bm25s
import jieba
from sqlalchemy import select

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeRetrievalError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    SourceType,
    SparseIndexEntry,
    SparseSearchHit,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import async_session
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import KnowledgeChunkRow


class SparseRetriever:
    """
    GuanChen 稀疏检索器 (BM25 Lexical Match)。
    职责：
    1. 依托 PostgreSQL 当前 Chunk 快照构建原子化内存词汇索引。
    2. 使用 Jieba 分词器维持 Document 与 Query 切词规则的绝对一致性。
    3. 严禁分数归一化；严禁返回零分无相关内容。
    4. 采用一致的平局打破机制 (score DESC, chunk_id ASC)。
    """

    def __init__(self, session_factory=None):
        self._session_factory = session_factory or async_session
        self._is_initialized = False
        self._chunk_snapshots: list[SparseIndexEntry] = []
        self._bm25_index: Any = None

    def _tokenize_sparse_text(self, text: str) -> list[str]:
        """
        核心 Tokenizer：
        NFKC 规范化 -> Jieba 精确切词 -> 剔除纯空格字符。
        无论 Query 还是 Corpus 均须通过此漏斗。
        """
        normalized_text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text)
        tokens = jieba.lcut(normalized_text, cut_all=False)
        return [t.strip() for t in tokens if t.strip()]

    async def rebuild_index(self) -> int:
        """
        全量重新拉取数据库快照，并在内存中原子化重构 BM25 索引。
        绝不拉回无用的 embedding 向量，绝不在失败时破坏当前已有的内存缓存。
        返回：成功索引的块数量。
        """
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                stmt = select(
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.chunk_id,
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id,
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.domain,
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.source_title,
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.source_type,
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.section,
                    KnowledgeChunkRow.text,
                ).order_by(KnowledgeChunkRow.chunk_id.asc())

                result = await session.execute(stmt)

                new_chunk_snapshots = []
                for row in result:
                    entry = SparseIndexEntry(
                        chunk_id=row.chunk_id,
                        document_id=row.document_id,
                        domain=KnowledgeDomain(row.domain),
                        source_title=row.source_title,
                        source_type=SourceType(row.source_type),
                        section=row.section,
                        text=row.text,
                    )
                    new_chunk_snapshots.append(entry)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("从数据库拉取最新快照构建稀疏索引失败。") from exc

        # 第三方库的内存索引建立
        try:
            if not new_chunk_snapshots:
                new_index = None
            else:
                tokenized_corpus = []
                for entry in new_chunk_snapshots:
                    searchable_text = f"{entry.source_title} {entry.section} {entry.text}"
                    tokenized_corpus.append(self._tokenize_sparse_text(searchable_text))

                new_index = bm25s.BM25(method="lucene")
                new_index.index(tokenized_corpus)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("Jieba 分词与 BM25s 第三方构建过程崩溃。") from exc

        self._bm25_index = new_index
        self._chunk_snapshots = new_chunk_snapshots
        self._is_initialized = True

        return len(self._chunk_snapshots)

    def _matches_filter(self, entry: SparseIndexEntry, filters: Optional[RetrievalFilter]) -> bool:
        if not filters:
            return True
        if filters.domains and entry.domain not in filters.domains:
            return False
        if filters.source_types and entry.source_type not in filters.source_types:
            return False
        if filters.source_titles and entry.source_title not in filters.source_titles:
            return False
        if filters.sections and entry.section not in filters.sections:
            return False
        return True

    def search(self, query: str, limit: int, filters: Optional[RetrievalFilter] = None) -> list[SparseSearchHit]:
        """
        在当前内存快照中进行词汇搜索。
        """
        if limit <= 0:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("检索数量必须大于 0。")
        if not self._is_initialized:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("稀疏检索索引尚未初始化，请先执行 rebuild_index。")
        if not query.strip():
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("检索查询 (Query) 不能为空。")

        if not self._chunk_snapshots or self._bm25_index is None:
            return []

        try:
            query_tokens = self._tokenize_sparse_text(query)
            if not query_tokens:
                return []

            docs_indices, scores = self._bm25_index.retrieve([query_tokens], k=len(self._chunk_snapshots))

            indices = docs_indices[0].tolist()
            raw_scores = scores[0].tolist()

            candidates: list[tuple[float, str, SparseIndexEntry]] = []

            for i, score in zip(indices, raw_scores):
                if score <= 0:
                    continue
                entry = self._chunk_snapshots[i]

                if not self._matches_filter(entry, filters):
                    continue

                candidates.append((float(score), entry.chunk_id, entry))

            # Tie-break 排序: 按分数降序 (Score DESC), 如果一样按 chunk_id 升序 (chunk_id ASC)
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (-x[0], x[1]))

            # 截取最终的合法 Top-K
            final_hits = []
            for score, _, entry in candidates[:limit]:
                hit = SparseSearchHit(
                    chunk_id=entry.chunk_id,
                    document_id=entry.document_id,
                    domain=entry.domain,
                    source_title=entry.source_title,
                    source_type=entry.source_type,
                    section=entry.section,
                    text=entry.text,
                    score=score,
                )
                final_hits.append(hit)

            return final_hits
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRetrievalError("稀疏检索失败。") from exc

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import (
    MemoryAlreadyExistsError,
    MemoryNotFoundError,
)
from guanchen_v2.memory.store import MemoryStore


class InMemoryMemoryStore(MemoryStore):
    """
    第一版基于内存的 Store，仅用于验证 Contract 与测试。
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        # 使用 JSON 字符串存储，强制切断内存对象引用 (State Identity)，验证 Value Equality
        self._data: dict[str, str] = {}

    async def save(self, state: ConversationState) -> None:
        if state.session_id in self._data:
            raise MemoryAlreadyExistsError(f"Session {state.session_id} 已经存在，无法重复 save。")
        
        self._data[state.session_id] = state.model_dump_json()

    async def load(self, session_id: str) -> ConversationState | None:
        json_data = self._data.get(session_id)
        if json_data is None:
            return None
            
        return ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_data)

    async def update(self, state: ConversationState) -> None:
        if state.session_id not in self._data:
            raise MemoryNotFoundError(f"Session {state.session_id} 不存在，无法 update。")
            
        self._data[state.session_id] = state.model_dump_json()

    async def delete(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        # 幂等操作：存在则删除，不存在也返回成功
        self._data.pop(session_id, None)



================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\memory\implementations\postgres.py
================================================================================

from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import (
    MemoryAlreadyExistsError,
    MemoryNotFoundError,
)
from guanchen_v2.memory.models import (
    ConversationStateRecord,
)


class PostgresMemoryStore:


    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker,
    ):
        self.session_factory = session_factory



    async def save(
        self,
        state: ConversationState,
    ) -> None:

        async with self.session_factory() as session:

            existing = await session.execute(
                select(
                    ConversationStateRecord
                )
                .where(
                    ConversationStateRecord.session_id
                    ==
                    state.session_id
                )
            )


            if existing.scalar_one_or_none():

                raise MemoryAlreadyExistsError(
                    f"Session {state.session_id} 已经存在"
                )


            record = ConversationStateRecord(
                session_id=state.session_id,
                state_json=state.model_dump_json(),
            )


            session.add(record)

            await session.commit()



    async def load(
        self,
        session_id: str,
    ) -> ConversationState | None:


        async with self.session_factory() as session:

            result = await session.execute(
                select(
                    ConversationStateRecord
                )
                .where(
                    ConversationStateRecord.session_id
                    ==
                    session_id
                )
            )


            record = result.scalar_one_or_none()


            if record is None:
                return None


            return ConversationState.model_validate_json(
                record.state_json
            )



    async def update(
        self,
        state: ConversationState,
    ) -> None:


        async with self.session_factory() as session:


            result = await session.execute(
                select(
                    ConversationStateRecord
                )
                .where(
                    ConversationStateRecord.session_id
                    ==
                    state.session_id
                )
            )


            record = result.scalar_one_or_none()


            if record is None:

                raise MemoryNotFoundError(
                    f"Session {state.session_id} 不存在"
                )


            record.state_json = (
                state.model_dump_json()
            )


            await session.commit()



    async def delete(
        self,
        session_id: str,
    ) -> None:


        async with self.session_factory() as session:


            await session.execute(
                delete(
                    ConversationStateRecord
                )
                .where(
                    ConversationStateRecord.session_id
                    ==
                    session_id
                )
            )


            await session.commit()

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\router\adapters\bazi.py
================================================================================

from typing import Any

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import ReadyForBazi
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import BaziAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


class BaziAgentAdapter:
    def __init__(self, runtime: BaziAgentRuntime):
        self._runtime = runtime

    async def invoke(
        self,
        query: str,
        context: RequirementContext,
        agent_state: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> object:
        profile = context.available_data.get(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE)
        if profile is None:
            raise RouterDispatchError("缺少已声明可用的 BIRTH_PROFILE")
        if not isinstance(profile, BirthProfile):
            raise RouterDispatchError("数据类型不合法：BIRTH_PROFILE 必须是 BirthProfile 对象")
            
        ready_input = ReadyForBazi(question=query, profile=profile)
        return await self._runtime.run(ready_input)

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\router\adapters\dream.py
================================================================================

from typing import Any

from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


class DreamAgentAdapter:
    def __init__(self, runtime: DreamAgentRuntime):
        self._runtime = runtime

    async def invoke(
        self,
        query: str,
        context: RequirementContext,
        agent_state: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> object:
        content = context.available_data.get(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT)
        if content is None:
            raise RouterDispatchError("缺少已声明可用的 DREAM_CONTENT")
        if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
            raise RouterDispatchError("DREAM_CONTENT 必须为非空字符串")
            
        return await self._runtime.handle_turn(content)

from typing import Any

from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


class KnowledgeAgentAdapter:
    def __init__(self, agent: KnowledgeAgent):
        self._agent = agent

    async def invoke(
        self,
        query: str,
        context: RequirementContext,
        agent_state: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> object:
        return await self._agent.answer(query)



================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\router\adapters\liuyao.py
================================================================================

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any
from uuid import NAMESPACE_URL, uuid5

from langgraph.types import Command

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.checkpoint import LiuyaoCheckpointManager
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ConfirmationResponse
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionQuestionStatus, DecisionRecord
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiuyaoInvocationResult:
    thread_id: str
    output: object


class LiuyaoAgentAdapter:
    def __init__(
        self,
        runtime: LiuyaoAgentRuntime,
        record_store: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._graph = runtime.build_graph()
        self._record_store = record_store

    async def _persist_decision_record(
        self,
        thread_id: str,
        state_snapshot: Any,
    ) -> None:
        """将当前六爻决策状态持久化为 DecisionRecord（Stage 8 长期记忆）。"""
        if self._record_store is None:
            return

        values = state_snapshot.values if state_snapshot is not None else {}
        question = values.get("question")
        if question is None:
            return

        record = DecisionRecord(
            record_id=uuid5(NAMESPACE_URL, "guanchen:decision:" + thread_id),
            question=question,
            thread_id=thread_id,
            status=question.status,
            hexagram=values.get("hexagram"),
            reflection=values.get("reflection"),
            final_response=values.get("final_response"),
        )
        await self._record_store.save(record)

    async def resume(
        self,
        thread_id: str,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> object:
        """
        基于外部截获的 thread_id 与 HITL payload 接力恢复执行。

        供前端 / 外部系统在拿到 LiuyaoInvocationResult.thread_id 后，
        跨进程、跨会话继续同一 LangGraph 线程。
        """
        validated = ConfirmationResponse.model_validate(payload)
        config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}
        output = await self._graph.ainvoke(Command(resume=validated.model_dump(exclude_none=True)), config)
        snapshot = await self._graph.aget_state(config)
        await self._persist_decision_record(thread_id, snapshot)
        return output

    async def invoke(self, query: str, context: RequirementContext, agent_state: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> LiuyaoInvocationResult:
        if agent_state is None:
            agent_state = {}

        # A completed decision starts a new thread; never overwrite its checkpoint.
        if agent_state.get("status") == "COMPLETED":
            agent_state.pop("thread_id", None)
        # 1. 恢复或生成 LangGraph Thread
        thread_id = LiuyaoCheckpointManager.get_or_create_thread(agent_state)
        status = agent_state.get("status")

        config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

        # 2. 判断当前生命周期阶段
        if status == "WAITING_INPUT":
            snapshot = await self._graph.aget_state(config)
            continued = dict(snapshot.values)
            continued["pending_user_input"] = query
            output = await self._graph.ainvoke(continued, config)
        elif status == "INTERRUPTED":
            # 这是一个挂起后的继续执行（Resume 流程）
            try:
                payload = json.loads(query)
            except ValueError:
                # 后备机制：尝试处理基于文本的用户确认 (如果前端还没对接 JSON)
                text = query.strip()
                if text.lower() in ("确认", "确认起卦", "confirm", "yes", "y"):
                    payload = {"action": "confirm"}
                elif "取消" in text or text.lower() in ("不确认", "暂不确认", "cancel", "no", "n"):
                    payload = {"action": "cancel"}
                else:
                    payload = {"action": "modify", "modification": text}

            validated = ConfirmationResponse.model_validate(payload)
            output = await self._graph.ainvoke(Command(resume=validated.model_dump(exclude_none=True)), config)
        else:
            # 全新执行
            seed = context.available_data.get(RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION)
            if seed is None:
                raise RouterDispatchError("LiuyaoAgentAdapter 缺少已声明可用的 DECISION_QUESTION")
            if not isinstance(seed, str) or not seed.strip():
                raise RouterDispatchError("DECISION_QUESTION 必须为非空字符串")

            initial_state = create_initial_liuyao_state(seed)
            output = await self._graph.ainvoke(initial_state, config)

        # 3. 计算最新的 LangGraph 中断状态并更新回 ConversationState Namespace
        state_snapshot = await self._graph.aget_state(config)
        if state_snapshot and state_snapshot.next:
            new_status = "INTERRUPTED"
            pending_node = state_snapshot.next[0]
        elif (
            state_snapshot
            and isinstance(state_snapshot.values, dict)
            and getattr(state_snapshot.values.get("question"), "status", None) == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
        ):
            new_status = "WAITING_INPUT"
            pending_node = "clarify"
        else:
            new_status = "COMPLETED"
            pending_node = None

        LiuyaoCheckpointManager.save_checkpoint_reference(agent_state, thread_id, new_status, pending_node)

        # 4. 持久化决策记录（含中断态），供跨进程恢复
        await self._persist_decision_record(thread_id, state_snapshot)

        return LiuyaoInvocationResult(
            thread_id=thread_id,
            output=output,
        )

FILE: tests\application\test_factory_integration.py

"""Application assembly acceptance: real adapters/graph/repos with deterministic model responses."""

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.application.factory import GuanChenApplication
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamInterpretationDraft
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ClarificationAssessment
from guanchen_v2.memory.checkpointer import PostgresCheckpointerFactory
from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.postgres import PostgresMemoryStore
from guanchen_v2.memory.selector import MemorySelector
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamElements, DreamPatch, RealityBackground
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionReflection
from tests.conftest import TEST_DATABASE_URL

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def fake_llm():
    async def generate(**kwargs):
        schema = kwargs["schema"]
        if schema == DreamPatch:
            text = kwargs["user_prompt"]
            if "工作压力" in text:
                return DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["工作压力"]))
            return DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["海边"] if "海边" in text else ["山上"], emotions=["平静"]))
        if schema == DreamInterpretationDraft:
            return DreamInterpretationDraft(psychological_context_view="请结合现实感受反思。")
        if schema == ClarificationAssessment:
            return ClarificationAssessment(needs_clarification=False, clarified_question="是否接受新工作", reality_constraints=["收入稳定"])
        if schema == DecisionReflection:
            return DecisionReflection(
                situation_summary="工作选择",
                traditional_reflection="证据不足，不做具体传统判断",
                reality_check="比较收入和工作条件",
                decision_options=["比较后再决定"],
                uncertainty_note="仅供反思",
            )
        raise AssertionError(schema)

    return MagicMock(generate_structured=AsyncMock(side_effect=generate))


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_real_factory_dream_isolation_and_liuyao_restart(db_env):
    _, factory = db_env
    settings = Settings(deepseek_api_key="test", deepseek_model="test", database_url=TEST_DATABASE_URL)
    retrieval = MagicMock(retrieve=AsyncMock(return_value=[]))
    saver_a, pool_a = await PostgresCheckpointerFactory.create(TEST_DATABASE_URL)
    try:
        app = GuanChenApplication(settings, factory, saver_a, retrieval, fake_llm())
        await app.execute("dream-a", "我梦见在海边，非常平静")
        await app.execute("dream-b", "我梦见在山上，非常平静")
        await app.execute("dream-a", "最近工作压力很大")
        selector = MemorySelector(factory)
        record_a = await selector.load_dream_memory("dream-a")
        record_b = await selector.load_dream_memory("dream-b")
        assert record_a.dream_id != record_b.dream_id
        assert record_a.elements.scenes == ["海边"]
        assert record_b.elements.scenes == ["山上"]
        assert record_a.reality_background.stressors == ["工作压力"]
        first = await app.execute("decision", "帮我起一卦，是否接受新工作")
        assert first.status == "interrupted"
        assert (await selector.load_decision_memory("decision")).hexagram is None
    finally:
        await pool_a.close()
    saver_b, pool_b = await PostgresCheckpointerFactory.create(TEST_DATABASE_URL)
    try:
        rebuilt = GuanChenApplication(settings, factory, saver_b, retrieval, fake_llm())
        resumed = await rebuilt.execute("decision", "确认")
        assert resumed.status == "answered"
        assert resumed.thread_id == first.thread_id
        record = await selector.load_decision_memory("decision")
        assert record.hexagram is not None
        assert record.final_response
        state = await PostgresMemoryStore(factory).load("decision")
        assert state.agent_states.liuyao["status"] == "COMPLETED"
        assert state.pending_interrupt is None
        await rebuilt.execute("dream-a", "然后呢")
        assert (await selector.load_dream_memory("dream-a")).dream_id == record_a.dream_id
    finally:
        await pool_b.close()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_and_dual_application_roundtrip(db_env, monkeypatch):
    from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import BaziAgentResult
    from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import LunarPythonBaziAdapter
    from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesisDraft

    _, factory = db_env
    profiles = []

    class BaziRuntime:
        def __init__(self, *args):
            pass

        async def run(self, ready):
            profiles.append(ready.profile)
            return BaziAgentResult(
                question=ready.question, chart=LunarPythonBaziAdapter().calculate(ready.profile), answer="确定性排盘后的测试解释", citations=()
            )

    monkeypatch.setattr("guanchen_v2.application.factory.BaziAgentRuntime", BaziRuntime)
    llm = fake_llm()
    original = llm.generate_structured.side_effect

    async def generate(**kwargs):
        if kwargs["schema"] == SynthesisDraft:
            return SynthesisDraft(content="两种视角")
        return await original(**kwargs)

    llm.generate_structured.side_effect = generate
    settings = Settings(deepseek_api_key="test", deepseek_model="test", database_url=TEST_DATABASE_URL)
    retrieval = MagicMock(retrieve=AsyncMock(return_value=[]))
    saver, pool = await PostgresCheckpointerFactory.create(TEST_DATABASE_URL)
    try:
        app = GuanChenApplication(settings, factory, saver, retrieval, llm)
        await app.execute("birth-dual", "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看，我梦见海边，感觉平静")
        assert (await PostgresMemoryStore(factory).load("birth-dual")).requirement_result.status == "needs_input"
        await app.execute("birth-dual", "补充资料", birth_patch=dict(year=1992, month=8, day=18, hour=10, minute=0, gender="male"))
        assert (await PostgresMemoryStore(factory).load("birth-dual")).requirement_result.status == "ready"
        rebuilt = GuanChenApplication(settings, factory, saver, retrieval, llm)
        await rebuilt.execute("birth-dual", "我的八字事业怎么样", birth_patch={"hour": 16})
        assert profiles[-1].hour == 16
        assert (await MemorySelector(factory).load_birth_profile("birth-dual")).hour == 16
        rebuilt2 = GuanChenApplication(settings, factory, saver, retrieval, llm)
        await rebuilt2.execute("birth-dual", "我的八字事业怎么样")
        assert profiles[-1].hour == 16
        assert (await rebuilt2.execute("other-user", "我的八字事业怎么样")).status == "needs_input"
    finally:
        await pool.close()

FILE: tests\application\test_orchestrator.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.application.exceptions import ConversationExecutionError
from guanchen_v2.application.orchestrator import ConversationOrchestrator
from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.inmemory import InMemoryMemoryStore
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesizedResponse


@pytest.fixture
def memory_store():
    return InMemoryMemoryStore()

@pytest.fixture
def mocks():
    router = AsyncMock()
    single_disp = AsyncMock()
    dual_disp = AsyncMock()
    synth = AsyncMock()
    
    # Setup Default happy path: CHITCHAT bypassing
    router.route.return_value = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT,
        secondary_intents=(),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(),
        reason="Test Routing"
    )
    synth.synthesize.return_value = SynthesizedResponse(
        content="Test Synth Content",
        used_intents=(IntentType.CHITCHAT,)
    )
    
    return router, single_disp, dual_disp, synth

@pytest.fixture
def orchestrator(memory_store, mocks):
    router, single_disp, dual_disp, synth = mocks
    return ConversationOrchestrator(
        memory_store=memory_store,
        router=router,
        single_dispatcher=single_disp,
        dual_dispatcher=dual_disp,
        synthesizer=synth
    )

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_1_new_session_flow(orchestrator, memory_store):
    """Test 1: New Session Flow (无 session_id 初始化并正常走完流程)"""
    resp = await orchestrator.execute(session_id=None, user_query="Hello")
    
    assert resp.content == "Test Synth Content"
    
    # 验证底层的 Store 确实增加了一条状态
    assert len(memory_store._data) == 1
    session_id = list(memory_store._data.keys())[0]
    
    state = await memory_store.load(session_id)
    assert len(state.messages) == 2  # user + assistant
    assert state.messages[0].content == "Hello"
    assert state.messages[1].content == "Test Synth Content"

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_2_existing_session_recovery(orchestrator, memory_store):
    """Test 2: Existing Session Recovery (使用已有 session_id 拉起)"""
    # 提前跑一次创造 session
    await orchestrator.execute(session_id="known-id", user_query="1")
    
    # 复用已知 ID
    await orchestrator.execute(session_id="known-id", user_query="2")
    
    state = await memory_store.load("known-id")
    assert len(state.messages) == 4 # (User + Assistant) x 2 轮
    assert state.messages[-2].content == "2"

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_3_router_integration(orchestrator, mocks):
    """Test 3: Router Integration 确保 Orchestrator 调用了底层路由"""
    router, _, _, _ = mocks
    await orchestrator.execute(session_id="abc", user_query="请帮我解梦")
    
    router.route.assert_awaited_once_with("请帮我解梦")

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_4_agent_failure_boundary(orchestrator, mocks, memory_store):
    """Test 4: Agent Failure 边界，不应污染 State 持久化记录"""
    router, single_disp, _, _ = mocks
    
    # 模拟一个实际会触发 Dispatch 的 Intent: KNOWLEDGE
    router.route.return_value = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
        secondary_intents=(),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(),
        reason="Knowledge Check"
    )
    
    # 模拟 Dispatch 过程突然崩溃
    single_disp.dispatch.side_effect = ValueError("Agent Runtime crashed")
    
    with pytest.raises(ConversationExecutionError):
        await orchestrator.execute(session_id="fail-test", user_query="什么是易经")
        
    state = await memory_store.load("fail-test")
    # 确保保存的依然是最初始状态（仅包含 UUID 拉起时的初始状态），不应被半成品的 user_message 污染覆盖
    assert len(state.messages) == 0

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_6_session_isolation(orchestrator, memory_store):
    """Test 6: Session 物理隔离，互不干涉"""
    await orchestrator.execute(session_id="session-A", user_query="A 的问题")
    await orchestrator.execute(session_id="session-B", user_query="B 的问题")
    
    state_a = await memory_store.load("session-A")
    state_b = await memory_store.load("session-B")
    
    assert state_a.current_query == "A 的问题"
    assert state_b.current_query == "B 的问题"



================================================================================
FILE: tests\application\test_stage9_gate_regressions.py

import asyncio
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.application.orchestrator import ConversationOrchestrator
from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.inmemory import InMemoryMemoryStore
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import HighPrecisionRuleRouter, RouterDecision, TwoLayerIntentRouter, build_routing_result
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesizer import AgentSynthesizer
from tests.memory.test_checkpoint_recovery import build_mock_services

PROFILE = dict(year=1992, month=8, day=18, hour=10, minute=0, gender="male")


def build_orch(adapters, store=None):
    llm = MagicMock()
    llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(side_effect=AssertionError("unexpected LLM routing/synthesis"))
    return ConversationOrchestrator(
        store or InMemoryMemoryStore(),
        TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), llm),
        SingleAgentDispatcher(adapters),
        DualDomainDispatcher(adapters),
        AgentSynthesizer(llm),
    )


def adapter():
    return MagicMock(invoke=AsyncMock(return_value=SimpleNamespace(answer="已完成", citations=())))


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_new_dream_enters_agent_and_followup_stays_in_task():
    dream = adapter()
    orch = build_orch({IntentType.DREAM: dream})
    await orch.execute("dream", "我梦见在河里游泳，非常害怕")
    await orch.execute("dream", "最近工作压力很大")
    assert dream.invoke.await_count == 2
    assert dream.invoke.await_args.args[1].available_data == {RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "最近工作压力很大"}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_supplements_across_rebuild_keep_original_question():
    bazi = adapter()
    store = InMemoryMemoryStore()
    first = build_orch({IntentType.BAZI: bazi}, store)
    response = await first.execute("b", "我的八字事业怎么样")
    assert response.status == "needs_input"
    assert "hour" in response.content
    await first.execute("b", "补充资料", birth_patch={k: v for k, v in PROFILE.items() if k != "hour"})
    bazi.invoke.assert_not_awaited()
    rebuilt = build_orch({IntentType.BAZI: bazi}, store)
    await rebuilt.execute("b", "补充时间", birth_patch={"hour": 10})
    assert bazi.invoke.await_args.args[0] == "我的八字事业怎么样"
    assert bazi.invoke.await_args.args[1].available_data[RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE].hour == 10


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_pending_and_ready_roundtrip():
    bazi, dream = adapter(), adapter()
    # use real response object for dual synthesis, without a live model
    orch = build_orch({IntentType.BAZI: bazi, IntentType.DREAM: dream})
    from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesizedResponse

    orch._synthesizer._llm_client.generate_structured.side_effect = None
    orch._synthesizer._llm_client.generate_structured.return_value = SynthesizedResponse(content="综合", used_intents=())
    query = "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看，我梦见游泳"
    await orch.execute("dual", query)
    pending = await orch._memory_store.load("dual")
    assert pending.requirement_result.status == "needs_input"
    await orch.execute("dual", "补充资料", birth_patch=PROFILE)
    ready = await orch._memory_store.load("dual")
    assert ready.requirement_result.status == "ready"
    assert dream.invoke.await_args.args[1].available_data[RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT] == query
    ConversationState.model_validate_json(ready.model_dump_json())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_fanout_really_overlaps():
    arrived = set()
    both = asyncio.Event()

    def make(intent):
        async def invoke(*args):
            arrived.add(intent)
            if len(arrived) == 2:
                both.set()
            await asyncio.wait_for(both.wait(), timeout=2)
            return intent.value

        return MagicMock(invoke=invoke)

    dispatcher = DualDomainDispatcher({i: make(i) for i in (IntentType.BAZI, IntentType.DREAM)})
    route = build_routing_result(RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,), confidence=1, reason="test"))
    results = await dispatcher.dispatch(route, "query", RequirementContext())
    assert [r.intent for r in results] == [IntentType.BAZI, IntentType.DREAM]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["不确认", "暂不确认", "我不确认，取消", "先不要确认，我想改一下"])
async def test_negative_confirmation_never_casts(text):
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    liuyao = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval))
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "是否换工作"})
    state = {}
    await liuyao.invoke("开始", context, state)
    await liuyao.invoke(text, context, state)
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
@pytest.mark.parametrize("action", ["confirm", "cancel", "modify"])
async def test_direct_resume_persists_latest_record(action):
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    records = MagicMock(save=AsyncMock())
    liuyao = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval), records)
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "是否换工作"})
    first = await liuyao.invoke("开始", context, {})
    output = await liuyao.resume(first.thread_id, {"action": action, **({"modification": "加上家庭约束"} if action == "modify" else {})})
    saved = records.save.await_args.args[0]
    assert saved.status == output["question"].status
    assert saved.thread_id == first.thread_id
    assert records.save.await_count == 2
    assert saved.hexagram == output.get("hexagram")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_new_liuyao_and_confirmation_through_real_orchestrator():
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    liuyao = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval))
    orch = build_orch({IntentType.LIUYAO: liuyao})
    first = await orch.execute("l", "帮我起一卦，是否换工作")
    assert first.status == "interrupted"
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0
    result = await orch.execute("l", "确认")
    assert result.status == "answered"
    assert result.thread_id == first.thread_id
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 1
    state = await orch._memory_store.load("l")
    assert state.pending_interrupt is None

    assert not report["passed"]

    assert report["evaluated"] < report["total"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_clarification_continues_original_question():
    from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ClarificationAssessment

    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    llm.generate_structured.side_effect = [
        ClarificationAssessment(needs_clarification=True, clarification_prompt="请补充具体选项"),
        ClarificationAssessment(needs_clarification=False, clarified_question="是否接受新职位", reality_constraints=[]),
    ]
    liuyao = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval))
    orch = build_orch({IntentType.LIUYAO: liuyao})
    first = await orch.execute("clarify", "帮我起一卦，工作问题")
    assert first.status == "needs_input"
    second = await orch.execute("clarify", "现在有一个新的职位邀请")
    assert second.status == "interrupted"
    assert second.thread_id == first.thread_id
    assert "现在有一个新的职位邀请" in llm.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs["user_prompt"]
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_completed_workflow_uses_new_thread_for_next_question():
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    liuyao = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval))
    orch = build_orch({IntentType.LIUYAO: liuyao})
    first = await orch.execute("thread", "帮我起一卦，是否换工作")
    await orch.execute("thread", "取消")
    second = await orch.execute("thread", "帮我起一卦，是否搬家")
    assert first.thread_id != second.thread_id
    assert second.status == "interrupted"

    liuyao = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval))
    bazi = adapter()

    orch._router._llm_client.generate_structured.side_effect = None
    orch._router._llm_client.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, secondary_intents=(IntentType.LIUYAO,), confidence=1, reason="显式双域"
    )
    first = await orch.execute("multi", "结合八字与六爻，分析工作选择", birth_patch=PROFILE)
    assert first.status == "interrupted"
    await orch.execute("multi", "确认")
    assert bazi.invoke.await_count == 1
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 1

from datetime import datetime
from typing import NamedTuple, Optional

from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender, LuckDirection


class BaziCase(NamedTuple):
    case_id: str
    rationale: str
    profile: BirthProfile
    expected_pillars: tuple[str, str, str, str]
    expected_day_master: str
    expected_direction: Optional[LuckDirection] = None
    expected_start_offset: Optional[tuple[int, int, int]] = None
    expected_start_at: Optional[datetime] = None


BAZI_GOLDEN_CASES = [
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F01",
        rationale="普通四柱 mapping，来源官方 Python Test",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2005, month=12, day=23, hour=8, minute=37, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("乙酉", "戊子", "辛巳", "壬辰"),
        expected_day_master="辛",
        expected_direction=LuckDirection.BACKWARD,
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F02",
        rationale="23:30 晚子时 sect=2，来源官方 Python Test",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=1988, month=2, day=15, hour=23, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("戊辰", "甲寅", "庚子", "戊子"),
        expected_day_master="庚",
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F03",
        rationale="22:30 晚子时前边界对照",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=1988, month=2, day=15, hour=22, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("戊辰", "甲寅", "庚子", "丁亥"),
        expected_day_master="庚",
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F04",
        rationale="女运与起运日期，来源官方 YunTest",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=1981, month=1, day=29, hour=23, minute=37, gender=Gender.FEMALE),
        expected_pillars=("庚申", "己丑", "丁未", "壬子"),
        expected_day_master="丁",
        expected_start_offset=(8, 0, 20),
        expected_start_at=datetime(1989, 2, 18, 23, 37),
        expected_direction=LuckDirection.BACKWARD,
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F05",
        rationale="男运 yun_sect=1，GuanChen baseline，官方断言起运日期",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2022, month=3, day=9, hour=20, minute=51, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("壬寅", "癸卯", "辛酉", "戊戌"),
        expected_day_master="辛",
        expected_start_offset=(8, 9, 10),
        expected_start_at=datetime(2030, 12, 19, 20, 51),
        expected_direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F06",
        rationale="男运：与 F07 对照，性别不污染四柱",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2024, month=5, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("甲辰", "戊辰", "乙丑", "壬午"),
        expected_day_master="乙",
        expected_direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F07",
        rationale="女运：与 F06 对照，性别不污染四柱",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2024, month=5, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE),
        expected_pillars=("甲辰", "戊辰", "乙丑", "壬午"),
        expected_day_master="乙",
        expected_direction=LuckDirection.BACKWARD,
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F08",
        rationale="闰二月中段 regression fixture",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2023, month=4, day=6, hour=10, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("癸卯", "丙辰", "甲午", "己巳"),
        expected_day_master="甲",
    ),
    BaziCase(
        case_id="F09",
        rationale="真正闰二月开始边界 (2023-03-22 闰二月初一)",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2023, month=3, day=22, hour=10, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE),
        expected_pillars=("癸卯", "乙卯", "己卯", "己巳"),
        expected_day_master="己",
    ),
]


================================================================================
FILE: tests\bazi\test_adapter.py
================================================================================

from importlib.metadata import version

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import LunarPythonBaziAdapter
from guanchen_v2.bazi.exceptions import BaziAdapterError
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender


@pytest.fixture
def adapter():
    return LunarPythonBaziAdapter()


def test_adapter_happy_path(adapter):
    """Case 1: 验证普通 BirthProfile 能正常走通整条排盘映射链。"""
    profile = BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    chart = adapter.calculate(profile)

    assert chart.year_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == "戊寅"
    # 1998年7月21日的日柱是“己卯”，所以日干（日主）应该是“己”
    assert chart.day_master.value == "己"
    # DaYun mapping: 确保 index 0 已被抛弃，第一个运是 index 1
    assert chart.da_yun.periods[0].index == 1


def test_calculation_method_provenance(adapter):
    """Case 2: 验证产生的 calculation_method 完全符合 Stage 2.3 的硬编码契约。"""
    profile = BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    method = adapter.calculate(profile).calculation_method

    assert method.library == "lunar_python"
    assert method.late_zi_sect == 2
    assert method.yun_sect == 1
    assert method.library_version == version("lunar_python")
    assert method.true_solar_time is False


def test_day_stem_ten_god_is_none(adapter):
    """Case 3: 验证日柱十神按要求置空。"""
    profile = BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    chart = adapter.calculate(profile)

    assert chart.year_pillar.stem_ten_god is not None
    assert chart.day_pillar.stem_ten_god is None  # 日主自身不需要十神


def test_gender_mapping_female(adapter):
    """Case 4: 验证女性排盘同样可以正确执行。"""
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=0, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE)
    chart = adapter.calculate(profile)
    assert chart is not None


def test_adapter_error_translation(adapter, monkeypatch):
    """Case 5: 验证第三方崩溃时能正确封装为 BaziAdapterError。"""

    def mock_get_eight_char(*args, **kwargs):
        raise ValueError("Simulated third-party crash")

    # 猴子补丁破坏底层库调用
    import lunar_python

    monkeypatch.setattr(lunar_python.Lunar, "getEightChar", mock_get_eight_char)

    profile = BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    with pytest.raises(BaziAdapterError, match="lunar_python 排盘或字段映射失败") as exc_info:
        adapter.calculate(profile)

    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, ValueError)
    assert str(exc_info.value.__cause__) == "Simulated third-party crash"


def test_adapter_hidden_stems_mapping_drift(adapter, monkeypatch):
    """Case 6: 验证藏干与十神数量不一致时（API 漂移），Adapter 会拒绝静默截断并抛出异常。"""
    import lunar_python

    # 猴子补丁：强行修改类的底层方法，让年柱的地支十神永远只返回 1 个元素
    monkeypatch.setattr(lunar_python.EightChar, "getYearShiShenZhi", lambda self: ["正财"])

    # 1998年是戊寅年，寅（YIN）的真实藏干有 3 个（戊、丙、甲）。
    # 此时 zip(strict=True) 会因为 3 != 1 抛出 ValueError，并被 Adapter 捕获转化为 BaziAdapterError
    profile = BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)

    with pytest.raises(BaziAdapterError, match="lunar_python 排盘或字段映射失败。"):
        adapter.calculate(profile)

FILE: tests\bazi\test_bazi_fixtures.py

import pytest
from lunar_python import Solar

from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import LunarPythonBaziAdapter
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender, LuckDirection
from tests.bazi.cases import BAZI_GOLDEN_CASES


@pytest.fixture
def adapter():
    return LunarPythonBaziAdapter()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", BAZI_GOLDEN_CASES, ids=lambda c: c.case_id)
def test_golden_fixtures(adapter, case):
    """验证所有核心签名，确保底层库升级时 GuanChen 能发现行为漂移。"""
    chart = adapter.calculate(case.profile)

    assert chart.year_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == case.expected_pillars[0]
    assert chart.month_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == case.expected_pillars[1]
    assert chart.day_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == case.expected_pillars[2]
    assert chart.hour_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == case.expected_pillars[3]
    assert chart.day_master.value == case.expected_day_master

    if case.expected_start_offset:
        y, m, d = case.expected_start_offset
        assert chart.da_yun.start_offset.years == y
        assert chart.da_yun.start_offset.months == m
        assert chart.da_yun.start_offset.days == d

    if case.expected_start_at:
        assert chart.da_yun.start_at == case.expected_start_at

    if case.expected_direction:
        assert chart.da_yun.direction == case.expected_direction


def test_full_mapping_f01(adapter):
    """对 F01 案例执行极其完整的全量结构断言。"""
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2005, month=12, day=23, hour=8, minute=37, gender=Gender.MALE)
    chart = adapter.calculate(profile)

    # 1. 四柱干支与十神映射校验
    assert chart.year_pillar.stem_ten_god.value == "偏财"
    assert chart.month_pillar.stem_ten_god.value == "正印"
    assert chart.day_pillar.stem_ten_god is None  # 日主自身无十神
    assert chart.hour_pillar.stem_ten_god.value == "伤官"

    # 2. 每柱 hidden_stems 的 stem + ten_god 极其严格的校验
    # 年柱 (酉)
    assert [(h.stem.value, h.ten_god.value) for h in chart.year_pillar.hidden_stems] == [("辛", "比肩")]
    # 月柱 (子)
    assert [(h.stem.value, h.ten_god.value) for h in chart.month_pillar.hidden_stems] == [("癸", "食神")]
    # 日柱 (巳) -> 校验 lunar_python 1.4.8 Issue #28 锁定行为：丙、庚、戊
    assert [(h.stem.value, h.ten_god.value) for h in chart.day_pillar.hidden_stems] == [
        ("丙", "正官"),
        ("庚", "劫财"),
        ("戊", "正印"),
    ]
    # 时柱 (辰)
    assert [(h.stem.value, h.ten_god.value) for h in chart.hour_pillar.hidden_stems] == [
        ("戊", "正印"),
        ("乙", "偏财"),
        ("癸", "食神"),
    ]

    # 3. 大运起步与第一条 DaYun 细节校验
    assert chart.da_yun.direction == LuckDirection.BACKWARD
    assert chart.da_yun.start_offset.years is not None
    assert chart.da_yun.start_at is not None

    first_da_yun = chart.da_yun.periods[0]
    assert first_da_yun.index == 1
    assert first_da_yun.start_year > 2005
    assert first_da_yun.start_age > 0
    assert first_da_yun.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == "丁亥"  # 戊子退一位

    # 4. calculation_method 全量校验
    method = chart.calculation_method
    assert method.library == "lunar_python"
    assert isinstance(method.library_version, str)
    assert method.late_zi_sect == 2
    assert method.yun_sect == 1
    assert method.true_solar_time is False
    assert method.birth_place_affects_calculation is False


def test_male_female_difference(adapter):
    """F06/F07 对照：验证同一个时刻的不同性别仅影响大运，绝不能污染四柱。"""
    male_prof = BirthProfile(year=2024, month=5, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)
    female_prof = BirthProfile(year=2024, month=5, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE)

    male_chart = adapter.calculate(male_prof)
    female_chart = adapter.calculate(female_prof)

    assert male_chart.year_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi == female_chart.year_pillar.gan_zhi.gan_zhi
    assert male_chart.day_master == female_chart.day_master

    assert male_chart.da_yun.direction != female_chart.da_yun.direction
    assert male_chart.da_yun.start_at != female_chart.da_yun.start_at


def test_birth_place_non_effect(adapter):
    """防御：验证出生地文本不参与运算，防止暗中接入真实经纬度修正。"""
    prof_none = BirthProfile(year=1990, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)
    prof_chengdu = BirthProfile(year=1990, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE, birth_place_text="成都")

    chart_a = adapter.calculate(prof_none)
    chart_b = adapter.calculate(prof_chengdu)

    assert chart_a.model_dump() == chart_b.model_dump()


def test_differential_mapping(adapter):
    """Differential Test：确保 Adapter 的值就是库原始吐出的值，没有串台。"""
    profile = BirthProfile(year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)

    solar = Solar.fromYmdHms(1998, 7, 21, 14, 30, 0)
    raw_eight = solar.getLunar().getEightChar()
    raw_eight.setSect(2)

    chart = adapter.calculate(profile)
    assert chart.year_pillar.gan_zhi.stem.value == raw_eight.getYearGan()
    assert chart.day_master.value == raw_eight.getDayGan()

FILE: tests\bazi\test_bazi_retrieval.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi_retrieval import BaziKnowledgeQuery, build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool


def test_query_validation_rejects_empty():
    """验证空白字符在工具门槛外就会被阻断。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不能为空或纯空格"):
        BaziKnowledgeQuery(query="   ")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_tool_happy_path_and_domain_locking():
    """核心：确认强制锁定了 BAZI 过滤，并且将结果组装成了不包含分数的证据文本。"""
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    res = RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id="c1",
        document_id="d1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="卷一",
        text="测试文本",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=1,
    )
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[res])

    tool = build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool(mock_service)
    content, artifact = await tool.coroutine(query="测试搜索")

    # 验证底层被呼叫时，死死锁定了领域
    call_kwargs = mock_service.retrieve.call_args.kwargs
    assert call_kwargs["query"] == "测试搜索"
    assert call_kwargs["filters"].domains == frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.BAZI})

    # 验证证据文本对 LLM 做到了极简隔离
    assert "chunk_id: c1" in content
    assert "测试文本" in content
    assert "rrf_score" not in content  # 决不暴露分数给 LLM

    # Artifact 完整留存供下游溯源
    assert artifact.results == [res]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_tool_empty_results_fallback():
    """验证查无资料时能够给予模型温和退让指引。"""
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    tool = build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool(mock_service)
    content, artifact = await tool.coroutine(query="天外飞仙")

    assert "当前知识库未检索到足够依据" in content
    assert artifact.results == []


================================================================================
FILE: tests\bazi\test_bazi_tool.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BaziChart
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi import build_calculate_bazi_tool


def test_calculate_bazi_tool_schema():
    """验证工具暴露给大模型的 Schema，确保必填项齐全，且没有泄漏排盘配置信息。"""
    mock_service = Mock()
    bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(service=mock_service)

    # 1. 验证提供给模型的 Tool 名称
    assert bazi_tool.name == "calculate_bazi"

    schema = bazi_tool.args_schema.model_json_schema()
    properties = schema.get("properties", {})
    required = schema.get("required", [])

    # 2. 验证大模型必须要生成的必填字段
    expected_required = {"year", "month", "day", "hour", "minute", "gender"}
    for req_field in expected_required:
        assert req_field in properties
        assert req_field in required

    # 3. 验证存在但不强制生成的选填字段
    assert "birth_place_text" in properties
    assert "birth_place_text" not in required

    # 4. 严格断言：大模型绝不能看到或干预的系统级溯源及流派参数
    internal_fields = [
        "late_zi_sect",
        "yun_sect",
        "library",
        "library_version",
        "true_solar_time",
        "birth_place_affects_calculation",
    ]
    for internal in internal_fields:
        assert internal not in properties


def test_calculate_bazi_tool_successful_invocation():
    """验证 Tool 调用链路返回一个可序列化的 JSON 兼容字典对象。"""
    mock_service = Mock()
    mock_chart = Mock(spec=BaziChart)
    mock_chart.model_dump_json.return_value = '{"day_master": "甲"}'
    mock_service.calculate.return_value = mock_chart

    bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(service=mock_service)

    assert bazi_tool.response_format == "content_and_artifact"

    # 验证底层函数确实返回了 (字符串，原实体对象) 元组
    content, artifact = bazi_tool.func(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender="male")
    assert content == '{"day_master": "甲"}'
    assert artifact is mock_chart


def test_calculate_bazi_tool_missing_fields_rejected():
    """验证缺少字段时，LangChain 工具的 args_schema 会直接拦截，不会调用底层业务逻辑。"""
    mock_service = Mock()
    bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(service=mock_service)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        # 故意缺失 hour 和 minute 等核心字段
        bazi_tool.invoke({"year": 2000, "month": 1, "day": 1, "gender": "male"})

    mock_service.calculate.assert_not_called()

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.bazi.exceptions import BaziAdapterError, BaziCalculationError
from guanchen_v2.bazi.service import BaziService
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender


def test_bazi_service_happy_path():
    """验证服务正常代理到 Adapter，并正确返回数据。"""
    mock_adapter = Mock()
    mock_chart = Mock()
    mock_adapter.calculate.return_value = mock_chart

    service = BaziService(adapter=mock_adapter)
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)

    result = service.calculate(profile)
    assert result is mock_chart
    mock_adapter.calculate.assert_called_once_with(profile=profile)


def test_bazi_service_error_translation():
    """验证服务将 Adapter 异常正确地翻译为 Calculation 异常，并保留根因。"""
    mock_adapter = Mock()
    mock_adapter.calculate.side_effect = BaziAdapterError("Simulated Adapter Error")

    service = BaziService(adapter=mock_adapter)
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)

    with pytest.raises(BaziCalculationError) as exc_info:
        service.calculate(profile)

    assert str(exc_info.value) == "八字排盘业务执行失败。"
    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, BaziAdapterError)

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import (
    DatabaseSettings,
    Settings,

def test_settings_instantiation_with_valid_kwargs():
    settings = Settings(
        _env_file=None,
        deepseek_api_key="test-secret-key",
        deepseek_model="deepseek-chat",
    )

    assert settings.deepseek_api_key.get_secret_value() == "test-secret-key"

    assert settings.deepseek_model == "deepseek-chat"

    assert settings.app_env == "development"


def test_settings_missing_required_fields_fails(
    monkeypatch,
):

        "GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_API_KEY",
        raising=False,
    )

    monkeypatch.delenv(
        "GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_MODEL",
        raising=False,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Settings(_env_file=None)


def test_settings_type_coercion(
    monkeypatch,
):
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_API_KEY",
        "test-key",
    )

    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_MODEL",
        "deepseek-chat",
    )

    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "GUANCHEN_LLM_MAX_RETRIES",
        "4",
    )

    settings = Settings(_env_file=None)

    assert settings.llm_max_retries == 4

    assert isinstance(
        settings.llm_max_retries,
        int,
    )

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.dream.context import DreamContextService, classify_dream_boundary
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import merge_dream_record, start_new_dream
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamContextStatus,
    DreamCorrection,
    DreamElements,
    DreamField,
    DreamPatch,
    DreamTurnBoundary,
    RealityBackground,
)


@pytest.fixture
def mock_extractor():
    extractor = MagicMock()
    extractor.extract = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamPatch())
    return extractor


@pytest.fixture
def context_service(mock_extractor):
    return DreamContextService(extractor=mock_extractor)


def test_explicit_same_dream_negation_does_not_reset():
    """测试带有 '不是另一个梦' 等否定句时，不会触发错误的新梦标记"""
    result = classify_dream_boundary(
        "这不是另一个梦，是刚才那个梦的补充",
        has_current_dream=True,
    )
    assert result == DreamTurnBoundary.SAME


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_3_consecutive_turns_same_dream(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """连续 3 轮梦境补充（场景 -> 现实背景 -> 修正人物），不丢旧字段"""
    # 轮次 1：初始梦境
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["旧学校", "走廊"]))
    res1 = await context_service.apply_turn("我梦见在学校走廊", None)

    assert res1.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert "旧学校" in res1.record.elements.scenes

    # 轮次 2：补充现实压力（注意这里不会触发 raw_description 的追加）
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["考试"]))
    res2 = await context_service.apply_turn("最近考试压力大", res1.record)

    assert res2.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert "旧学校" in res2.record.elements.scenes
    assert "考试" in res2.record.reality_background.stressors
    assert len(res2.record.raw_descriptions) == 1

    # 轮次 3：错误修正
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(corrections=[DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.SCENES, old_value="旧学校", new_value="小学")])
    res3 = await context_service.apply_turn("不是旧学校，是小学", res2.record)

    assert res3.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert "旧学校" not in res3.record.elements.scenes
    assert "小学" in res3.record.elements.scenes
    assert "考试" in res3.record.reality_background.stressors
    assert res3.record.dream_id == res1.record.dream_id


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_explicit_new_dream(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """明确的新梦标记应重启 DreamRecord 且 严格屏蔽旧上下文"""

    # 构建一个有脏数据的旧梦境
    old_record = merge_dream_record(
        start_new_dream(),
        DreamPatch(
            element_additions=DreamElements(
                people=["妈妈"],
                scenes=["旧学校"],
            )
        ),
    )

    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["海边"]))
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("换一个梦，我梦到了海边", old_record)

    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert res.started_new_dream is True
    assert res.record.dream_id != old_record.dream_id

    # 核心验证 1：Extractor 收到的 current_record 必须被完全置空
    kwargs = mock_extractor.extract.call_args.kwargs
    extraction_record = kwargs["current_record"]

    assert extraction_record.dream_id != old_record.dream_id
    assert extraction_record.elements.people == []
    assert extraction_record.elements.scenes == []

    # 核心验证 2：最终的 ContextResult 也绝对不能包含前一个梦的元素
    assert "妈妈" not in res.record.elements.people
    assert "旧学校" not in res.record.elements.scenes
    assert res.record.elements.scenes == ["海边"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ambiguous_dream_boundary_clarification(context_service):
    """边界模糊时不猜，直接触发澄清追问"""
    old_record = start_new_dream()
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("昨晚我又梦见在树林", old_record)

    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert "还是一个新的梦" in res.clarification_question


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_merge_conflict_clarification(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """明确修正但目标缺失时，转为澄清追问，绝不污染原状态"""
    old_record = start_new_dream()
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(corrections=[DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.PEOPLE, old_value="妈妈", new_value="同事")])

    res = await context_service.apply_turn("不是妈妈，是同事", old_record)

    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert "哪一项信息" in res.clarification_question
    assert res.record == old_record


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_no_dream_content_clarification(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """仅包含现实压力，完全没有梦境实体时，触发补充追问"""
    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["加班"]))

    res = await context_service.apply_turn("最近疯狂加班", None)

    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert "哪怕只描述一两个最鲜明的片段" in res.clarification_question


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_patch_then_what(context_service, mock_extractor):
    """'然后呢'导致的空补丁应正常延续状态，不引发无意义的强制问卷调查"""
    record = start_new_dream()
    record.elements.scenes.append("天台")

    mock_extractor.extract.return_value = DreamPatch()
    res = await context_service.apply_turn("然后呢？", record)

    assert res.status == DreamContextStatus.UPDATED
    assert res.started_new_dream is False
    assert "天台" in res.record.elements.scenes


================================================================================
FILE: tests\dream\test_dream_merge.py

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import mark_interpreted, merge_dream_record, start_new_dream
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamMergeConflictError
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamCorrection,
    DreamElements,
    DreamField,
    DreamPatch,
    InterpretationStatus,
    RealityBackground,
)


def test_consecutive_appends_retain_old_fields():
    record = start_new_dream()

    # Turn 1: Add Scene
    patch1 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["旧学校"]))
    record1 = merge_dream_record(record, patch1, raw_description_fragment="梦到在学校")
    assert "旧学校" in record1.elements.scenes

    # Turn 2: Add Person
    patch2 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["姐姐"]))
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record1, patch2, raw_description_fragment="里面还有我姐姐")
    assert "旧学校" in record2.elements.scenes
    assert "姐姐" in record2.elements.people

    # Turn 3: Add Background (Stressors). 纯现实背景不写入 raw_descriptions.
    patch3 = DreamPatch(background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["最近工作压力大"]))
    record3 = merge_dream_record(record2, patch3)
    assert "旧学校" in record3.elements.scenes
    assert "姐姐" in record3.elements.people
    assert "最近工作压力大" in record3.reality_background.stressors

    # 证明第 3 轮未提供 raw 文本，因此 raw_descriptions 长度仍为 2
    assert len(record3.raw_descriptions) == 2


def test_merge_does_not_mutate_input_record():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(
        record,
        DreamPatch(
            element_additions=DreamElements(
                scenes=["旧学校"],
                people=["姐姐"],
            )
        ),
    )

    original = record.model_copy(deep=True)

    patch = DreamPatch(
        element_additions=DreamElements(objects=["红门"]),
        corrections=[
            DreamCorrection(
                field=DreamField.PEOPLE,
                old_value="姐姐",
                new_value="同事",
            )
        ],
    )

    result = merge_dream_record(record, patch)

    # 断言 1：入参 record 完全没变
    assert record == original
    # 断言 2：返回的是全新对象内存地址
    assert result is not record
    # 断言 3：原来的 record 依然保留旧值
    assert record.elements.people == ["姐姐"]
    assert record.elements.objects == []


def test_reject_blank_structured_values():
    """测试 NonBlankText 能否有效拦截大模型可能输出的脏数据"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DreamElements(people=["   "])

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.PEOPLE, old_value="", new_value=None)


# ====== 以下为原有的其它测试，只需修改报错捕获的 match 文案 ======


def test_unmentioned_fields_remain_unchanged():
    record = start_new_dream()
    patch1 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["教室"], objects=["黑板"]))
    record1 = merge_dream_record(record, patch1)

    patch2 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["老师"]))
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record1, patch2)

    assert record2.elements.scenes == ["教室"]
    assert record2.elements.objects == ["黑板"]
    assert record2.elements.people == ["老师"]


def test_stable_dedupe():
    record = start_new_dream()
    patch1 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["同事"]))
    record1 = merge_dream_record(record, patch1)

    # Append the same concept with different spacing/case (simulate normalization target)
    patch2 = DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=[" 同事 "]))
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record1, patch2)

    assert len(record2.elements.people) == 1
    assert record2.elements.people[0] == "同事"


def test_explicit_correction_replace():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["姐姐"])))

    correction = DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.PEOPLE, old_value="姐姐", new_value="以前的同事")
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(corrections=[correction]))

    assert "姐姐" not in record2.elements.people
    assert "以前的同事" in record2.elements.people


def test_explicit_correction_remove():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(objects=["红门"])))

    correction = DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.OBJECTS, old_value="红门", new_value=None)
    record2 = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(corrections=[correction]))

    assert "红门" not in record2.elements.objects
    assert len(record2.elements.objects) == 0


def test_invalid_correction_raises_conflict():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["同事"])))

    correction = DreamCorrection(field=DreamField.PEOPLE, old_value="姐姐", new_value="路人")
    # match 文案已被修改为与核心逻辑中一致
    with pytest.raises(DreamMergeConflictError, match="当前梦境记录中不存在待修正目标"):
        merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(corrections=[correction]))


def test_interpretation_stale_logic():
    record = start_new_dream()
    record = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["学校"])))

    # Simulate interpretation complete
    record = mark_interpreted(record)
    assert record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.CURRENT

    # Empty patch (no-op) should not change revision or stale status
    record_noop = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch())
    assert record_noop.revision == record.revision
    assert record_noop.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.CURRENT

    # Effective change should bump revision and stale the status
    record_changed = merge_dream_record(record, DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["同事"])))
    assert record_changed.revision == record.revision + 1
    assert record_changed.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.STALE


def test_new_dream_reset():
    old_record = start_new_dream()
    old_record = merge_dream_record(
        old_record,
        DreamPatch(
            element_additions=DreamElements(people=["妈妈"]),
            background_additions=RealityBackground(stressors=["考试"]),
        ),
        raw_description_fragment="梦见考试",
    )
    old_record = mark_interpreted(old_record)

    new_record = start_new_dream()
    assert new_record.dream_id != old_record.dream_id
    assert new_record.elements.people == []
    assert new_record.reality_background.stressors == []
    assert new_record.raw_descriptions == []
    assert new_record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.NOT_STARTED
    assert new_record.revision == 1


================================================================================
FILE: tests\dream\test_extractor.py
