from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import (
    merge_dream_record,
    start_new_dream,
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import (
    DreamExtractionError,
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.extractor import (
    DreamExtractor,
)
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMProviderError,
    LLMStructuredOutputError,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamCorrection,
    DreamElements,
    DreamField,
    DreamPatch,
)
from guanchen_v2.prompts.dream_agent import (
    DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
)


@pytest.fixture
def fake_llm_client():
    client = MagicMock(spec_set=LLMClient)

    client.generate_structured = AsyncMock(return_value=DreamPatch())

    return client


@pytest.fixture
def extractor(
    fake_llm_client,
):
    return DreamExtractor(llm_client=fake_llm_client)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_extract_returns_dream_patch_and_uses_llm_client_contract(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    expected = DreamPatch(
        element_additions=DreamElements(
            scenes=["旧学校"],
            emotions=["害怕"],
        )
    )

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.return_value = expected

    result = await extractor.extract("我梦到回到旧学校，梦里很害怕")

    assert result == expected

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()

    kwargs = fake_llm_client.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs

    assert set(kwargs) == {
        "system_prompt",
        "user_prompt",
        "schema",
    }

    assert kwargs["system_prompt"] == DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT

    assert kwargs["schema"] is DreamPatch

    assert "我梦到回到旧学校" in kwargs["user_prompt"]

    # System instruction 与用户数据保持分离。
    assert "旧学校" not in kwargs["system_prompt"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_input_raises_error_without_calling_llm(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    with pytest.raises(
        DreamExtractionError,
        match="抽取输入不能为空",
    ):
        await extractor.extract("   ")

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.assert_not_awaited()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_failure_is_wrapped_and_chained(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    provider_error = LLMProviderError("LLM provider failure")

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.side_effect = provider_error

    with pytest.raises(
        DreamExtractionError,
        match="梦境信息结构化抽取失败",
    ) as exc_info:
        await extractor.extract("我梦见了一只猫")

    assert exc_info.value.__cause__ is provider_error


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_structured_output_failure_is_wrapped_and_chained(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    structured_error = LLMStructuredOutputError("structured output failure")

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.side_effect = structured_error

    with pytest.raises(
        DreamExtractionError,
        match="梦境信息结构化抽取失败",
    ) as exc_info:
        await extractor.extract("我梦见了一只猫")

    assert exc_info.value.__cause__ is structured_error


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_context_selection_hides_raw_history(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    record = start_new_dream()

    record = merge_dream_record(
        record,
        DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(scenes=["旧学校"])),
        raw_description_fragment=("第一句话，啰嗦的历史信息，不该发给模型"),
    )

    await extractor.extract(
        "第二句话",
        current_record=record,
    )

    user_prompt = fake_llm_client.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs["user_prompt"]

    assert "旧学校" in user_prompt
    assert "elements" in user_prompt

    assert "啰嗦的历史信息" not in user_prompt

    assert "raw_descriptions" not in user_prompt
    assert "interpretation_state" not in user_prompt
    assert '"revision"' not in user_prompt


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_extraction_does_not_mutate_state(
    extractor,
):
    record = start_new_dream()

    record = merge_dream_record(
        record,
        DreamPatch(element_additions=DreamElements(people=["姐姐"])),
    )

    original = record.model_copy(deep=True)

    await extractor.extract(
        "其实不是姐姐，是同事",
        current_record=record,
    )

    assert record == original


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_defensive_invalid_return_type_fails(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    """
    LLMClient 正常情况下已经保证 schema 类型。

    该测试保留 DreamExtractor 自身的最后一道
    defensive boundary，防止未来错误实现或测试替身
    绕过 LLMClient contract。
    """
    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.return_value = {"not": "dream_patch"}

    with pytest.raises(
        DreamExtractionError,
        match="未返回合法的 DreamPatch",
    ):
        await extractor.extract("我梦见一只猫")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_cascading_correction_intent(
    extractor,
    fake_llm_client,
):
    """
    回归测试：
    DreamExtractor 应接受一次包含多个 correction 的 DreamPatch，
    后续 merge 应能正确应用级联字段修正。
    """
    record = start_new_dream()

    record.elements.people = ["妈妈"]
    record.elements.actions = ["妈妈站在门边"]

    expected_patch = DreamPatch(
        corrections=[
            DreamCorrection(
                field=DreamField.PEOPLE,
                old_value="妈妈",
                new_value="班主任",
            ),
            DreamCorrection(
                field=DreamField.ACTIONS,
                old_value="妈妈站在门边",
                new_value="班主任站在门边",
            ),
        ]
    )

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.return_value = expected_patch

    patch = await extractor.extract(
        "不是妈妈，是以前的班主任",
        current_record=record,
    )

    assert patch == expected_patch

    fake_llm_client.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()

    kwargs = fake_llm_client.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs

    assert kwargs["schema"] is DreamPatch
    assert "不是妈妈，是以前的班主任" in kwargs["user_prompt"]

    updated_record = merge_dream_record(
        record,
        patch,
    )

    assert "妈妈" not in updated_record.elements.people
    assert "班主任" in updated_record.elements.people

    assert "妈妈站在门边" not in updated_record.elements.actions
    assert "班主任站在门边" in updated_record.elements.actions

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import (
    DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER,
    DreamRetrievalEvidence,
    DreamRetrievalService,
    build_dream_retrieval_queries,
    dedupe_retrieval_results,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import (
    RetrievalService,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamElements,
    DreamRecord,
    DreamRetrievalAspect,
    RealityBackground,
)


def create_mock_retrieval_result(
    chunk_id: str,
) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id=str(uuid4()),
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.DREAM,
        source_title="《周公解梦》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="动物",
        text="梦见蛇。",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=None,
        sparse_rank=None,
    )


def test_query_builder_all_aspects():
    record = DreamRecord(
        elements=DreamElements(
            objects=["黑猫"],
            emotions=["害怕"],
        ),
        reality_background=RealityBackground(stressors=["考试压力"]),
        raw_descriptions=["不要把我放进检索文本里"],
    )

    queries = build_dream_retrieval_queries(record)

    assert len(queries) == 3

    aspects = {query.aspect: query.query for query in queries}

    assert "黑猫" in aspects[DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL]

    assert "害怕" in aspects[DreamRetrievalAspect.EMOTION]

    assert "考试压力" in aspects[DreamRetrievalAspect.REALITY_CONTEXT]

    assert not any("不要把我放进检索文本里" in query.query for query in queries)


def test_query_builder_missing_aspects():
    record = DreamRecord(elements=DreamElements(objects=["蛇"]))

    queries = build_dream_retrieval_queries(record)

    assert len(queries) == 1
    assert queries[0].aspect == DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL


def test_dream_retrieval_filter_is_domain_locked():
    assert DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER.domains == frozenset(
        {
            KnowledgeDomain.DREAM,
        }
    )

    assert DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER.source_types is None
    assert DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER.source_titles is None
    assert DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER.sections is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_forces_dream_domain():
    shared_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_result = create_mock_retrieval_result("chunk-1")

    shared_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[mock_result])

    service = DreamRetrievalService(shared_service)

    record = DreamRecord(
        elements=DreamElements(
            objects=["蛇"],
            emotions=["恐惧"],
        )
    )

    evidence = await service.retrieve(record)

    assert len(evidence) == 2

    assert shared_service.retrieve.await_count == 2

    for call in shared_service.retrieve.await_args_list:
        assert call.kwargs["filters"] == DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER

        assert call.kwargs["filters"].domains == frozenset(
            {
                KnowledgeDomain.DREAM,
            }
        )

    for evidence_group in evidence:
        assert evidence_group.results[0].chunk_id == "chunk-1"


def test_stable_chunk_dedupe():
    result_a = create_mock_retrieval_result("A")
    result_b = create_mock_retrieval_result("B")
    result_c = create_mock_retrieval_result("C")

    symbol_evidence = DreamRetrievalEvidence(
        aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL,
        query="q1",
        results=[
            result_a,
            result_b,
        ],
    )

    emotion_evidence = DreamRetrievalEvidence(
        aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.EMOTION,
        query="q2",
        results=[
            result_b,
            result_c,
        ],
    )

    deduped = dedupe_retrieval_results(
        [
            symbol_evidence,
            emotion_evidence,
        ]
    )

    assert len(deduped) == 3

    assert [result.chunk_id for result in deduped] == [
        "A",
        "B",
        "C",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_retrieval_is_valid():
    shared_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    shared_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    service = DreamRetrievalService(shared_service)

    record = DreamRecord(elements=DreamElements(objects=["独角兽"]))

    evidence = await service.retrieve(record)

    assert len(evidence) == 1
    assert evidence[0].results == []

    shared_service.retrieve.assert_awaited_once()

    filters = shared_service.retrieve.await_args.kwargs["filters"]

    assert filters.domains == frozenset(
        {
            KnowledgeDomain.DREAM,
        }
    )


================================================================================
FILE: tests\dream\test_runtime.py
================================================================================

from langchain.agents import create_agent
from langchain.agents.middleware import (
    ModelCallLimitMiddleware,
    ToolCallLimitMiddleware,
)
from langchain.agents.middleware.model_call_limit import (
    ModelCallLimitExceededError,
)
from langchain.agents.middleware.tool_call_limit import (
    ToolCallLimitExceededError,
)
from langchain.agents.structured_output import ToolStrategy
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.exceptions import (
    BaziAgentCitationError,
    BaziAgentExecutionError,
    BaziAgentInvariantError,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziInterpretation,
    ReadyForBazi,
)
from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import LunarPythonBaziAdapter
from guanchen_v2.bazi.service import BaziService
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BaziChart
from guanchen_v2.prompts.bazi_agent import BAZI_AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi import build_calculate_bazi_tool
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi_retrieval import (
    BaziKnowledgeArtifact,
    build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool,
)


class BaziAgentRuntime:
    def __init__(
        self,
        settings: Settings,
        retrieval_service: RetrievalService,
    ) -> None:
        self._model = build_chat_model(settings=settings)

        self._service = BaziService(LunarPythonBaziAdapter())

        self._bazi_tool = build_calculate_bazi_tool(self._service)

        self._retrieve_tool = build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool(retrieval_service)

        self._agent = create_agent(
            model=self._model,
            tools=[
                self._bazi_tool,
                self._retrieve_tool,
            ],
            system_prompt=BAZI_AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
            middleware=[
                ModelCallLimitMiddleware(
                    run_limit=3,
                    exit_behavior="error",
                ),
                ToolCallLimitMiddleware(
                    tool_name="calculate_bazi",
                    run_limit=1,
                    exit_behavior="error",
                ),
                ToolCallLimitMiddleware(
                    tool_name=("retrieve_bazi_knowledge"),
                    run_limit=1,
                    exit_behavior="error",
                ),
            ],
            response_format=ToolStrategy(
                BaziInterpretation,
                handle_errors=False,
            ),
        )

    async def run(
        self,
        ready: ReadyForBazi,
    ) -> BaziAgentResult:
        content = (
            "=== USER QUESTION ===\n"
            f"{ready.question}\n\n"
            "=== VERIFIED BIRTH INPUT ===\n"
            f"公历 {ready.profile.year}-"
            f"{ready.profile.month}-"
            f"{ready.profile.day} "
            f"{ready.profile.hour}:"
            f"{ready.profile.minute:02d}\n"
            f"性别：{ready.profile.gender.value}\n"
        )

        if ready.profile.birth_place_text:
            content += f"出生地文本：{ready.profile.birth_place_text}\n"

        try:
            state = await self._agent.ainvoke({"messages": [HumanMessage(content=content)]})
        except (
            ModelCallLimitExceededError,
            ToolCallLimitExceededError,
        ) as exc:
            raise BaziAgentExecutionError("Agent 工具调用超载") from exc
        except Exception as exc:
            raise BaziAgentExecutionError("Agent 基础执行失败") from exc

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Tool artifact collection
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        bazi_chart_artifact: BaziChart | None = None

        bazi_knowledge_artifact: BaziKnowledgeArtifact | None = None

        calc_bazi_count = 0
        retrieve_count = 0

        for message in state.get(
            "messages",
            [],
        ):
            if (
                getattr(
                    message,
                    "type",
                    "",
                )
                != "tool"
            ):
                continue

            tool_name = getattr(
                message,
                "name",
                "",
            )

            if tool_name == "calculate_bazi":
                calc_bazi_count += 1

                if hasattr(
                    message,
                    "artifact",
                ):
                    bazi_chart_artifact = message.artifact

            elif tool_name == "retrieve_bazi_knowledge":
                retrieve_count += 1

                if hasattr(
                    message,
                    "artifact",
                ):
                    bazi_knowledge_artifact = message.artifact

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Tool call invariants
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if calc_bazi_count != 1:
            raise BaziAgentInvariantError(f"calculate_bazi tool 必须只调用 1 次，实际调用 {calc_bazi_count} 次")

        if retrieve_count > 1:
            raise BaziAgentInvariantError(f"retrieve_bazi_knowledge tool 最多只能调用 1 次，实际调用 {retrieve_count} 次")

        if not isinstance(
            bazi_chart_artifact,
            BaziChart,
        ):
            raise BaziAgentInvariantError("未在 ToolMessage 中找到合法的 BaziChart artifact")

        if retrieve_count == 1 and not isinstance(
            bazi_knowledge_artifact,
            BaziKnowledgeArtifact,
        ):
            raise BaziAgentInvariantError("retrieve_bazi_knowledge 未返回合法的 BaziKnowledgeArtifact")

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Structured response invariant
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        structured_response = state.get("structured_response")

        if not isinstance(
            structured_response,
            BaziInterpretation,
        ):
            raise BaziAgentInvariantError("Agent 未生成合法的 BaziInterpretation 结构化输出")

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Citation grounding
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        legal_chunk_ids = {}

        if bazi_knowledge_artifact is not None:
            for result in bazi_knowledge_artifact.results:
                legal_chunk_ids[result.chunk_id] = result

        deduped_ids: list[str] = []
        seen: set[str] = set()

        for chunk_id in structured_response.used_chunk_ids:
            if chunk_id in seen:
                continue

            seen.add(chunk_id)
            deduped_ids.append(chunk_id)

        for chunk_id in deduped_ids:
            if chunk_id not in legal_chunk_ids:
                raise BaziAgentCitationError("模型返回了未授权的证据编号。")

        citations: list[Citation] = []

        for chunk_id in deduped_ids:
            result = legal_chunk_ids[chunk_id]

            citations.append(Citation.from_retrieval_result(result))

        return BaziAgentResult(
            question=ready.question,
            chart=bazi_chart_artifact,
            answer=structured_response.answer,
            citations=tuple(citations),
        )

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import (
    DreamInterpretationDraft,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamPatch,
)


def test_dream_patch_rejects_top_level_extra():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="Extra inputs are not permitted",
    ):
        DreamPatch.model_validate(
            {
                "unexpected_field": "illegal",
            }
        )


def test_dream_patch_rejects_extra_inside_elements():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="Extra inputs are not permitted",
    ):
        DreamPatch.model_validate(
            {
                "element_additions": {
                    "scenes": ["旧学校"],
                    "hallucinated_field": ["illegal"],
                }
            }
        )


def test_dream_patch_rejects_extra_inside_background():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="Extra inputs are not permitted",
    ):
        DreamPatch.model_validate(
            {
                "background_additions": {
                    "stressors": ["工作压力"],
                    "diagnosis": ("模型不应创建这个字段"),
                }
            }
        )


def test_dream_patch_rejects_extra_inside_correction():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="Extra inputs are not permitted",
    ):
        DreamPatch.model_validate(
            {
                "corrections": [
                    {
                        "field": ("elements.people"),
                        "old_value": "姐姐",
                        "new_value": "同事",
                        "reason": "非法额外字段",
                    }
                ]
            }
        )


def test_dream_interpretation_draft_rejects_extra():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="Extra inputs are not permitted",
    ):
        DreamInterpretationDraft.model_validate(
            {
                "traditional_view": ("传统资料中的解释。"),
                "psychological_context_view": ("结合用户明确提供的现实背景进行反思。"),
                "used_chunk_ids": ["chunk-1"],
                "confidence": 0.99,
            }
        )


def test_dream_patch_json_schema_is_strict():
    schema = DreamPatch.model_json_schema()

    assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False

    definitions = schema["$defs"]

    assert definitions["DreamElements"]["additionalProperties"] is False

    assert definitions["RealityBackground"]["additionalProperties"] is False

    assert definitions["DreamCorrection"]["additionalProperties"] is False


def test_dream_interpretation_json_schema_is_strict():
    schema = DreamInterpretationDraft.model_json_schema()

    assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False

import pytest

TEST_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/guanchen_test"


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def isolate_knowledge_database(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    """
    Knowledge tests 默认指向独立测试数据库。

    使用 function-scoped monkeypatch，避免在 pytest collection 阶段
    修改进程级环境变量并污染 tests/knowledge 之外的测试。
    """
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "GUANCHEN_DATABASE_URL",
        TEST_DATABASE_URL,
    )

import math
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import numpy as np
import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeEmbeddingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeChunk, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


def _create_dummy_chunk(chunk_id: str, title: str, section: str, text: str) -> KnowledgeChunk:
    # 使用 model_construct 绕过文本清洗以便专心测试 Adapter 容错
    return KnowledgeChunk.model_construct(
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title=title,
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section=section,
        text=text,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Mock 隔离测试区
# ---------------------------------------------------------


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_model_initialization_failure(mock_st_class):
    """验证模型加载失败时抛出正确的项目异常，并保留底层根因"""
    mock_st_class.side_effect = RuntimeError("Disk full")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="Embedding 模型加载失败") as exc_info:
        BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, RuntimeError)


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_documents_input_and_order_contract(mock_st_class):
    """核心防线：确保输入不乱序，且输出能够严格按位置对齐 mapping"""
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model

    # 构造极具辨识度的数据以验证是否乱序：
    # Row 0 开头为 1.0, Row 1 开头为 2.0
    row_1 = np.full((512,), 1.0, dtype=np.float32)
    row_2 = np.full((512,), 2.0, dtype=np.float32)
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.array([row_1, row_2])

    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    chunks = [
        _create_dummy_chunk("c1", "《书1》", "第一章", "正文1"),
        _create_dummy_chunk("c2", "《书2》", "第二章", "正文2"),
    ]
    vectors = adapter.embed_documents(chunks)

    # 验证传入模型的参数
    call_args, _ = mock_model.encode.call_args
    texts_passed = call_args[0]
    assert texts_passed[0] == "《书1》\n第一章\n正文1"
    assert texts_passed[1] == "《书2》\n第二章\n正文2"

    # 验证输出的位置映射是否完美
    assert len(vectors) == 2
    assert vectors[0][0] == 1.0
    assert vectors[1][0] == 2.0


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_documents_fails_on_shape_mismatch(mock_st_class):
    """防御三方库诡异返回：比如只返回了一行，或者维度变成 768"""
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model
    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    chunks = [
        _create_dummy_chunk("c1", "书", "章", "正文"),
        _create_dummy_chunk("c2", "书", "章", "正文"),
    ]

    # 场景1：数量不对 (2个文本只返回1行)
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(1, 512)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="形状异常，预期为 \\(2, 512\\)"):
        adapter.embed_documents(chunks)

    # 场景2：维度不对
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(2, 768)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="形状异常，预期为 \\(2, 512\\)"):
        adapter.embed_documents(chunks)


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_documents_fails_on_empty_chunk_text(mock_st_class):
    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    chunks = [_create_dummy_chunk("c1", "《书》", "章", "   ")]
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="尝试对空文本进行 Embedding"):
        adapter.embed_documents(chunks)


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_query_happy_path(mock_st_class):
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(512).astype(np.float32)

    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()
    vector = adapter.embed_query("八字怎么看？")
    assert isinstance(vector, list)
    assert len(vector) == 512


@patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings.SentenceTransformer")
def test_embed_query_fails_on_empty_or_shape_mismatch(mock_st_class):
    mock_model = MagicMock()
    mock_st_class.return_value = mock_model
    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()

    # 场景1：空 Query
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="不能为空"):
        adapter.embed_query("   ")

    # 场景2：返回二维数组 (例如被错误地包裹了一层)
    mock_model.encode.return_value = np.random.rand(1, 512)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="输出了错误的形状.*预期应为 \\(512,\\)"):
        adapter.embed_query("正当查询")


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# 真实模型 Smoke Test
# ---------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.smoke
def test_real_bge_model_smoke():
    """
    真实的模型推理测试。只在明确指定 uv run pytest -m smoke 时运行。
    任何网络/模型加载问题在此处都应该 FAIL，而不是静默 skip。
    """
    from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter

    adapter = BgeEmbeddingAdapter()

    chunks = [
        _create_dummy_chunk("1", "书", "章", "真实大模型推理测试 A"),
        _create_dummy_chunk("2", "书", "章", "真实大模型推理测试 B"),
    ]
    vectors = adapter.embed_documents(chunks)
    assert len(vectors) == 2
    assert len(vectors[0]) == 512

    norm = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in vectors[0]))
    assert math.isclose(norm, 1.0, rel_tol=1e-5)

    query_vector = adapter.embed_query("测试 Query")
    assert len(query_vector) == 512
    q_norm = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in query_vector))
    assert math.isclose(q_norm, 1.0, rel_tol=1e-5)

def test_embedding_model_identity_contract(
    mock_st_class,
):

    assert adapter.MODEL_NAME == "BAAI/bge-small-zh-v1.5"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\test_eval_metrics.py
================================================================================

import math

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.eval_metrics import (
    calculate_mrr_at_k,
    calculate_percentile,
    calculate_recall_at_k,
    calculate_relative_mrr_gain,
)


def test_recall_at_k_logic():
    gt = {"A", "B", "C"}
    assert calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["X", "A", "Y", "C", "Z"], k=5) == 2 / 3
    assert calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["X", "A", "Y", "C", "Z"], k=2) == 1 / 3
    assert calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["X", "Y", "Z"], k=5) == 0.0

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="k 必须大于 0"):
        calculate_recall_at_k(gt, ["A"], k=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="不能为空"):
        calculate_recall_at_k(set(), ["A"], k=5)


def test_mrr_at_k_logic():
    gt = {"A", "B"}
    assert calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["X", "Y", "A", "B"], k=5) == 1 / 3
    assert calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["B", "Y", "X", "A"], k=5) == 1.0
    assert calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["X", "Y", "A", "B"], k=2) == 0.0

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="k 必须大于 0"):
        calculate_mrr_at_k(gt, ["A"], k=-1)


def test_relative_mrr_gain():
    assert math.isclose(calculate_relative_mrr_gain(0.70, 0.735), 0.05, rel_tol=1e-5)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="baseline MRR 必须大于 0，才能计算 relative MRR gain"):
        calculate_relative_mrr_gain(0.0, 0.5)


def test_percentile_logic():
    latencies = [10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0, 50.0]
    assert calculate_percentile(latencies, 50) == 30.0
    assert calculate_percentile(latencies, 95) > 45.0

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="没有 latency observation，无法计算 percentile"):
        calculate_percentile([], 95)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="percentile 必须位于"):
        calculate_percentile([10.0], 105)


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\test_models.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeChunk,
    KnowledgeDocument,
    KnowledgeDomain,
    SourceType,
    generate_deterministic_chunk_id,
)


def test_document_rejects_empty_text_or_whitespace():
    """测试文本规范：阻断纯空格或被剔除后为空的垃圾文本"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不能为空"):
        KnowledgeDocument(
            document_id="doc_1",
            domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
            source_title="书籍",
            source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
            section="章一",
            text="   \n   \t  ",
        )


def test_deterministic_chunk_id_consistency():
    """核心：相同来源、章节和内容，必须产生完全一致的 ID。"""
    id1 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "这是一段相同的文本")
    id2 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "这是一段相同的文本")
    assert id1 == id2


def test_deterministic_chunk_id_change_awareness():
    """核心：内容发生变动（哪怕只是一个字）或源头改变，指纹必定改变"""
    base_id = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "一段文本")
    assert base_id != generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "一段文本，加了个字")
    assert base_id != generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec2", "一段文本")


def test_knowledge_chunk_from_document_part_happy_path():
    """验证官方构造路径能够无缝组合各类元数据，并正确挂载 Deterministic ID"""
    chunk = KnowledgeChunk.from_document_part(
        document_id="yuanhai_ziping__juan_01",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="卷一",
        text="   天干者，甲乙丙丁也。   \n\n  ",
    )

    # 元数据应清洗并透传
    assert chunk.document_id == "yuanhai_ziping__juan_01"
    assert chunk.domain == KnowledgeDomain.BAZI
    # 因为保存时只做基础 strip，不会压缩换行，所以 \n\n 应该被保全
    assert chunk.text == "天干者，甲乙丙丁也。"

    # ID 仍由底层的 canonicalize 计算得出，不受外层空格影响
    expected_id = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("yuanhai_ziping__juan_01", "卷一", "天干者，甲乙丙丁也。   \n\n  ")
    assert chunk.chunk_id == expected_id


def test_chunk_id_whitespace_semantics():
    """验证 Chunk ID canonicalization 语义：纯空白符变化哈希不变，但有意义的分割保留"""
    # 这三者都被化简成 "甲 乙"，因此 ID 完全相同
    id1 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲   乙")
    id2 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲\t乙")
    id3 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲\n乙")
    assert id1 == id2 == id3

    # 原生单空格自然也等于化简后的单空格
    id4 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲 乙")
    assert id1 == id4

    # 但完全粘连的词汇是另一种语义，ID 必须不同！
    id5 = generate_deterministic_chunk_id("doc1", "sec1", "甲乙")
    assert id1 != id5


def test_knowledge_domain_includes_dream():
    assert KnowledgeDomain.DREAM.value == "dream"
    assert KnowledgeDomain("dream") is KnowledgeDomain.DREAM

from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.casting import (
    HexagramCastingService,
    ManualCastingInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import HexagramCalculationError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.iching_adapter import (
    IChingPyAdapter,
    VendorLineFacts,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinFace,
    HexagramIdentity,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)


class FakeCoinSource:
    def __init__(self, seq: list[CoinFace]):
        self._seq = seq
        self._idx = 0

    def toss(self) -> CoinFace:
        face = self._seq[self._idx]
        self._idx += 1
        return face


@pytest.fixture
def adapter():
    return IChingPyAdapter()


def make_vendor_line_facts() -> tuple[VendorLineFacts, ...]:
    """
    给不关心真实装卦内容的纯逻辑测试提供合法 vendor facts。
    """
    return tuple(
        VendorLineFacts(
            heavenly_stem="甲",
            earthly_branch="子",
            element="水",
            six_relative="兄弟",
        )
        for _ in range(6)
    )


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 验证手工输入边界
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_manual_input_validation():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ManualCastingInput(lines=(LineValue.OLD_YIN, LineValue.OLD_YANG))

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ManualCastingInput(lines=(7, 7, 7, 7, 7, 5))

    valid = ManualCastingInput(lines=(6, 7, 8, 9, 6, 7))
    assert len(valid.lines) == 6


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 验证 6/7/8/9 到 阴阳与动静 的语义映射
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_line_semantics(adapter):
    service = HexagramCastingService(
        adapter,
        FakeCoinSource([]),
    )

    mock_identity = HexagramIdentity(
        name="Mock",
        upper_trigram="U",
        lower_trigram="L",
    )

    with patch.object(
        adapter,
        "_resolve_vendor_hexagram",
        return_value=(
            mock_identity,
            make_vendor_line_facts(),
        ),
    ):
        result = service.cast_manual(
            ManualCastingInput(
                lines=(6, 7, 8, 9, 8, 7),
            )
        )

        l6, l7, l8, l9, _, _ = result.lines

        assert l6.polarity == YinYang.YIN
        assert l6.moving is True

        assert l7.polarity == YinYang.YANG
        assert l7.moving is False

        assert l8.polarity == YinYang.YIN
        assert l8.moving is False

        assert l9.polarity == YinYang.YANG
        assert l9.moving is True


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 验证第三方异常被安全拦截并保留根因
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_adapter_exception_boundary():
    real_adapter = IChingPyAdapter()
    service = HexagramCastingService(real_adapter, FakeCoinSource([]))

    with patch.object(real_adapter, "_resolve_vendor_hexagram", side_effect=Exception("ichingpy 内部越界")):
        # 修正正则匹配字符串，与 adapter 中抛出的一致
        with pytest.raises(HexagramCalculationError, match="六爻卦象构造与装卦推演失败") as exc_info:
            service.cast_manual(ManualCastingInput(lines=(7, 7, 7, 7, 7, 7)))

    assert str(exc_info.value.__cause__) == "ichingpy 内部越界"


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 验证 CoinTrace 数学溯源的一致性防篡改
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_deterministic_fake_coin_sequence(adapter):
    seq = [
        CoinFace.HEAD,
        CoinFace.TAIL,
        CoinFace.TAIL,
    ] * 6

    mock_primary = HexagramIdentity(
        name="Mock",
        upper_trigram="U",
        lower_trigram="L",
    )

    with patch.object(
        adapter,
        "_resolve_vendor_hexagram",
        return_value=(
            mock_primary,
            make_vendor_line_facts(),
        ),
    ):
        service = HexagramCastingService(
            adapter,
            FakeCoinSource(seq),
        )

        result = service.cast_random()

        assert result.casting_method == CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN
        assert len(result.coin_tosses) == 6

        assert result.coin_tosses[0].position == 1
        assert result.coin_tosses[0].line_value == LineValue.YOUNG_YANG
        assert result.lines[0].value == LineValue.YOUNG_YANG

        assert result.moving_line_positions == []


# =========================================================================
# 真实 Fixtures 验证：不再瞎猜，打印后人工核查并填入硬编码断言！
# =========================================================================


def test_fixed_real_ichingpy_fixture_no_moving_lines(adapter):
    """
    固定六爻输入 -> 真实 ichingpy -> 稳定乾卦装卦事实。
    """
    service = HexagramCastingService(
        adapter,
        FakeCoinSource([]),
    )

    result = service.cast_manual(
        ManualCastingInput(
            lines=(7, 7, 7, 7, 7, 7),
        )
    )

    assert result.casting_method == CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    assert result.moving_line_positions == []
    assert result.changed_hexagram is None

    # SixLinesDivinationEngine 没有提供稳定完整六十四卦名，
    # 不自行制造 name。
    assert result.primary_hexagram.name is None
    assert result.primary_hexagram.upper_trigram == "乾"
    assert result.primary_hexagram.lower_trigram == "乾"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 真实纳甲 / 五行 / 六亲
    # bottom -> top
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    expected = [
        ("甲", "子", "水", "子孙"),
        ("甲", "寅", "木", "妻财"),
        ("甲", "辰", "土", "父母"),
        ("壬", "午", "火", "官鬼"),
        ("壬", "申", "金", "兄弟"),
        ("壬", "戌", "土", "父母"),
    ]

    for line, (
        expected_stem,
        expected_branch,
        expected_element,
        expected_relative,
    ) in zip(result.lines, expected, strict=True):
        assert line.heavenly_stem == expected_stem
        assert line.earthly_branch == expected_branch
        assert line.element == expected_element
        assert line.six_relative == expected_relative

        # ichingpy 0.1.10 当前没有六神
        assert line.six_spirit is None

    # 乾为八纯卦：
    # 上爻为世，三爻为应。
    assert result.lines[5].is_shi is True
    assert result.lines[2].is_ying is True

    assert sum(line.is_shi for line in result.lines) == 1
    assert sum(line.is_ying for line in result.lines) == 1


def test_fixed_real_ichingpy_fixture_with_moving_lines(adapter):
    """
    固定动爻：
    (6, 7, 7, 9, 7, 7)

    初爻老阴、四爻老阳。
    """
    service = HexagramCastingService(
        adapter,
        FakeCoinSource([]),
    )

    result = service.cast_manual(
        ManualCastingInput(
            lines=(6, 7, 7, 9, 7, 7),
        )
    )

    assert result.moving_line_positions == [1, 4]

    # 本卦：上乾，下巽
    assert result.primary_hexagram.name is None
    assert result.primary_hexagram.upper_trigram == "乾"
    assert result.primary_hexagram.lower_trigram == "巽"

    # 变卦必须真实存在
    assert result.changed_hexagram is not None

    # 6 -> 7
    # 9 -> 8
    #
    # 变后：
    # 下乾，上巽
    assert result.changed_hexagram.name is None
    assert result.changed_hexagram.upper_trigram == "巽"
    assert result.changed_hexagram.lower_trigram == "乾"

import json
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.casting import HexagramCastingService
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ClarificationAssessment
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.retrieval import LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    DecisionReflection,
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


@pytest.fixture
def mock_services():
    casting = MagicMock(spec=HexagramCastingService)
    llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)

    async def mock_generate_structured(*args, **kwargs):
        schema = kwargs.get("schema")
        if schema == ClarificationAssessment:
            return ClarificationAssessment(
                needs_clarification=False,
                clarified_question="确认要离职吗？",
                reality_constraints=[]
            )
        elif schema == DecisionReflection:
            return DecisionReflection(
                situation_summary="情况",
                traditional_reflection="推演",
                reality_check="现实",
                decision_options=["选项"],
                uncertainty_note="免责"
            )
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown schema: {schema}")

    llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(side_effect=mock_generate_structured)
    
    lines = tuple(
        HexagramLine(
            position=i, value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG, polarity=YinYang.YANG, moving=False,
            heavenly_stem="甲", earthly_branch="子", element="水", six_relative="父母",
            is_shi=False, is_ying=False
        )
        for i in range(1, 7)
    )
    mock_hex = HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
        lines=lines,
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="乾", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="乾"),
        changed_hexagram=None,
        moving_line_positions=[],
        coin_tosses=[]
    )
    
    casting.cast_random.return_value = mock_hex
    casting.cast_manual.return_value = mock_hex
    
    retrieval = MagicMock(spec=LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder)
    retrieval.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    
    return casting, llm, retrieval


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_1_2_3_create_interrupt_resume(mock_services):
    casting, llm, retrieval = mock_services
    shared_saver = InMemorySaver()

    # ====== 阶段一：新请求发起 (Test 1 & Test 2) ======
    runtime1 = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=shared_saver)
    adapter1 = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(runtime1)
    
    agent_state = {}
    ctx = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "测辞职"})
    
    await adapter1.invoke("测辞职", ctx, agent_state)
    
    # Test 1: 验证 ConversationState 中已经具备了 thread_id
    assert "thread_id" in agent_state
    thread_id = agent_state["thread_id"]
    
    # Test 2: 验证到达确认节点并发生了 Interrupt Persistence
    assert agent_state["status"] == "INTERRUPTED"
    assert agent_state["pending_node"] == "confirmation"

    # ====== 阶段二：模拟进程重启后的第二个恢复请求 (Test 3) ======
    runtime2 = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=shared_saver)
    adapter2 = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(runtime2)
    
    confirm_payload = json.dumps({"action": "confirm"})
    await adapter2.invoke(confirm_payload, ctx, agent_state)
    
    # Test 3: Resume 成功恢复后跑完所有节点，Thread 不变
    assert agent_state["status"] == "COMPLETED"
    assert agent_state["thread_id"] == thread_id
    assert "pending_node" not in agent_state


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_4_session_isolation(mock_services):
    """Test 4: Session Isolation (不同 Session 绝对不产生交叉污染的 Thread)"""
    casting, llm, retrieval = mock_services
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=InMemorySaver())
    adapter = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(runtime)
    
    ctx = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "测辞职"})
    
    agent_state_a = {}
    agent_state_b = {}
    
    await adapter.invoke("测辞职 A", ctx, agent_state_a)
    await adapter.invoke("测辞职 B", ctx, agent_state_b)
    
    assert agent_state_a["thread_id"] != agent_state_b["thread_id"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_5_missing_thread_recovery(mock_services):
    """Test 5: Missing Thread Recovery (发生意外导致 state 丢失 thread 时，能安全拉起新 thread)"""
    casting, llm, retrieval = mock_services
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=InMemorySaver())
    adapter = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(runtime)
    
    ctx = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "测辞职"})
    agent_state = {}
    
    await adapter.invoke("测辞职", ctx, agent_state)
    original_thread = agent_state["thread_id"]
    
    # 手动破坏 State，模拟信息丢失
    del agent_state["thread_id"]
    
    # 修复验证 3：此时再次调用，应触发重新起步，不会出现 KeyError: 'question' 崩溃
    await adapter.invoke("测辞职", ctx, agent_state)
    new_thread = agent_state["thread_id"]
    
    assert new_thread != original_thread



================================================================================
FILE: tests\liuyao\test_policy.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import (
    ClarificationAssessment,
    ConfirmationAction,
    ConfirmationResponse,
    apply_clarification_assessment,
    apply_confirmation_response,
    can_cast,
)
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionQuestion, DecisionQuestionStatus


def test_clarification_needed_transition():
    """1. 测试 needs_clarification=True 时转为 NEEDS_CLARIFICATION 且 confirmed=false"""
    q = DecisionQuestion(original_question="我该辞职吗？")
    state = {"question": q, "clarification": None, "confirmed": False, "hexagram": None, "citations": [], "final_response": None}

    assessment = ClarificationAssessment(
        needs_clarification=True,
        clarification_prompt="请问你的主要考量是什么？",
    )
    new_state = apply_clarification_assessment(state, assessment)

    assert new_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert new_state["question"].clarified_question is None
    assert new_state["clarification"] == "请问你的主要考量是什么？"
    assert new_state["confirmed"] is False
    assert can_cast(new_state) is False


def test_candidate_ready_transition():
    """2. 测试 candidate ready 时转为 PENDING_CONFIRMATION"""
    q = DecisionQuestion(original_question="我该辞职吗？")
    state = {"question": q, "clarification": None, "confirmed": False, "hexagram": None, "citations": [], "final_response": None}

    assessment = ClarificationAssessment(
        needs_clarification=False,
        clarified_question="未来三个月内是否应当辞职并转向自由职业？",
        reality_constraints=["有房贷", "无其他被动收入"],
    )
    new_state = apply_clarification_assessment(state, assessment)

    assert new_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert new_state["question"].clarified_question == "未来三个月内是否应当辞职并转向自由职业？"
    assert new_state["question"].reality_constraints == ["有房贷", "无其他被动收入"]
    assert new_state["clarification"] is None
    assert new_state["confirmed"] is False
    assert can_cast(new_state) is False


def test_explicit_confirm_transition():
    """3. 测试只有 PENDING_CONFIRMATION 状态下才能显式 CONFIRM 转为 CONFIRMED"""
    q = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="我该辞职吗？",
        clarified_question="未来三个月内是否应当辞职？",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
    )
    state = {"question": q, "clarification": None, "confirmed": False, "hexagram": None, "citations": [], "final_response": None}

    response = ConfirmationResponse(action=ConfirmationAction.CONFIRM)
    new_state = apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    assert new_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert new_state["confirmed"] is True
    assert can_cast(new_state) is True

    # 验证幂等性（再次 CONFIRM 应当是安全 no-op）
    idempotent_state = apply_confirmation_response(new_state, response)
    assert idempotent_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert can_cast(idempotent_state) is True


def test_modify_invalidates_downstream():
    """4. 测试 MODIFY 导致确认失效，下游结果全清，并退回 NEEDS_CLARIFICATION"""
    q = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="我该辞职吗？",
        clarified_question="未来三个月内是否应当辞职？",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    state = {
        "question": q,
        "clarification": None,
        "confirmed": True,
        "hexagram": "mock_hexagram_obj",
        "citations": ["some_citation"],
        "final_response": "some_response",
    }

    response = ConfirmationResponse(
        action=ConfirmationAction.MODIFY,
        modification="我其实能接受两个月没有收入",
    )
    new_state = apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    assert new_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert new_state["question"].clarified_question is None
    assert new_state["confirmed"] is False
    assert new_state["hexagram"] is None
    assert new_state["citations"] == []
    assert new_state["final_response"] is None
    assert can_cast(new_state) is False
    assert new_state["clarification"] is None  # 带有实际修正意图时，无需等待进一步用户澄清提示


def test_cancel_transition():
    """5. 测试 CANCEL 转换为 CANCELLED，保留审计上下文且绝对不可起卦"""
    q = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="我该辞职吗？",
        clarified_question="未来三个月内是否应当辞职？",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
    )
    state = {"question": q, "clarification": None, "confirmed": False, "hexagram": "some_data", "citations": [1]}

    response = ConfirmationResponse(action=ConfirmationAction.CANCEL)
    new_state = apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    assert new_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED
    assert new_state["question"].original_question == "我该辞职吗？"
    assert new_state["question"].clarified_question == "未来三个月内是否应当辞职？"
    assert new_state["confirmed"] is False
    assert new_state["hexagram"] is None
    assert can_cast(new_state) is False

    # 验证 CANCEL 幂等
    idempotent_state = apply_confirmation_response(new_state, response)
    assert idempotent_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED


def test_cast_guard_projection():
    """6. 验证 state consistency guard：只有一致的 confirmed state 才允许 can_cast=True"""
    q_confirmed = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="测试",
        clarified_question="测试澄清",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )

    # 正常一致状态
    valid_state = {"question": q_confirmed, "confirmed": True}
    assert can_cast(valid_state) is True

    # 异常不一致状态：状态是 CONFIRMED 但 confirmed 投影标志被错误伪造成 False
    invalid_state_1 = {"question": q_confirmed, "confirmed": False}
    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="六爻确认状态不一致"):
        can_cast(invalid_state_1)

    # 异常不一致状态：状态是 PENDING_CONFIRMATION 但 confirmed 被错误置为 True
    q_pending = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="测试",
        clarified_question="测试澄清",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
    )
    invalid_state_2 = {"question": q_pending, "confirmed": True}
    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="六爻确认状态不一致"):
        can_cast(invalid_state_2)


def test_confirm_rejects_non_pending_state():
    """7. 验证 DRAFT 状态直接确认会被拒绝"""
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试")

    with pytest.raises(
        LiuyaoPolicyError,
        match="不允许直接确认",
    ):
        apply_confirmation_response(
            state,
            ConfirmationResponse(
                action=ConfirmationAction.CONFIRM,
            ),
        )


def test_clarification_rejects_confirmed_state():
    """8. 验证不可直接向 CONFIRMED 等已收敛状态应用澄清结果"""
    q = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="测试",
        clarified_question="未来三个月是否辞职？",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )

    state = {
        "question": q,
        "clarification": None,
        "confirmed": True,
        "hexagram": None,
        "citations": [],
        "final_response": None,
    }

    assessment = ClarificationAssessment(
        needs_clarification=False,
        clarified_question="另一个问题",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        LiuyaoPolicyError,
        match="不允许直接应用澄清结果",
    ):
        apply_clarification_assessment(
            state,
            assessment,
        )


def test_constraints_preserved_while_still_clarifying():
    """9. 验证如果尚未形成最终候选，reality_constraints 也绝不会被静默丢失"""
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("我有房贷，想考虑辞职")

    assessment = ClarificationAssessment(
        needs_clarification=True,
        reality_constraints=["有房贷"],
        clarification_prompt="你希望考虑多长的时间范围？",
    )

    new_state = apply_clarification_assessment(
        state,
        assessment,
    )

    assert new_state["question"].reality_constraints == ["有房贷"]

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, RetrievalResult, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.retrieval import LIUYAO_RETRIEVAL_FILTER, LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, HexagramIdentity, HexagramLine, HexagramResult, LineValue, YinYang


def create_mock_result(chunk_id: str) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.LIUYAO,
        source_title="测试书",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="第一章",
        text="测试文本",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=1,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_retrieval_builder_dedupes_and_filters():
    """验证检索建造器能过滤重复 chunk_id 并传递正确的 domain 限制过滤"""
    mock_shared_service = AsyncMock()

    # 模拟底层的多次检索返回了重复的 Chunk_ID
    # 根据我们 retrieval_builder 的逻辑，静卦会产生 2 条 Query (本卦查询、事象映射)
    mock_shared_service.retrieve.side_effect = [
        [create_mock_result("A"), create_mock_result("B")],
        [create_mock_result("B"), create_mock_result("C")],
    ]

    # 构造一个符合所有 Pydantic 严格校验的真实合法的假卦象
    mock_hexagram = HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
        lines=tuple(HexagramLine(position=i, value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG, polarity=YinYang.YANG, moving=False) for i in range(1, 7)),
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="否", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="坤"),
    )

    builder = LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder(mock_shared_service)
    results = await builder.retrieve("我要不要离职", mock_hexagram)

    # 去重后应只有 A, B, C
    assert len(results) == 3
    assert {r.chunk_id for r in results} == {"A", "B", "C"}

    # 验证底层被传了合法的 Filter
    assert mock_shared_service.retrieve.call_count == 2
    for call in mock_shared_service.retrieve.call_args_list:
        assert call.kwargs["filters"] == LIUYAO_RETRIEVAL_FILTER

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.types import Command

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation, KnowledgeDomain, RetrievalResult, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ClarificationAssessment, rebuild_question

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionReflection,
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)


@pytest.fixture
def mock_deps():
    caster = MagicMock()
    llm = AsyncMock()
    retrieval = AsyncMock()
    return caster, llm, retrieval


def create_dummy_hexagram() -> HexagramResult:
    """提供一个极其标准的静卦用于骗过格式化器"""
    return HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
        lines=tuple(HexagramLine(position=i, value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG, polarity=YinYang.YANG, moving=False) for i in range(1, 7)),
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="乾"),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_clarify_node_populates_assessment_correctly(mock_deps):
    """验证 LLM 提案通过 apply_policy 正确写入状态"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps

    # 模拟 LLM 成功结构化提取
    llm.generate_structured.return_value = ClarificationAssessment(
        needs_clarification=True, reality_constraints=["不想异地"], clarification_prompt="具体是什么工作类型？"
    )

    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("外地有个 offer 去不去")

    new_state = await runtime.clarify_node(state)

    assert new_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert new_state["clarification"] == "具体是什么工作类型？"
    assert new_state["question"].reality_constraints == ["不想异地"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieve_fails_fast_without_hexagram(mock_deps):
    """验证 retrieve_node 自身硬门槛：缺失卦象绝不调用数据库"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试检索")
    state["question"] = rebuild_question(state["question"], status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED, clarified_question="已确认")
    state["confirmed"] = True
    state["hexagram"] = None  # 漏掉了卦象

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="缺少 HexagramResult，无法执行六爻知识检索"):
        await runtime.retrieve_node(state)

    assert retrieval.retrieve.call_count == 0


def test_finalize_node_deterministic_rendering():
    """验证 Finalize 节点没有使用大模型，只做确定性拼接"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(None, None, None)

    reflection = DecisionReflection(
        situation_summary="面临职业分水岭",
        traditional_reflection="卦象平平",
        reality_check="资金不足，建议按兵不动",
        decision_options=["先干着", "积累人脉"],
        uncertainty_note="玄学参考不可尽信",
    )

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试问题")
    state["reflection"] = reflection

    out = runtime.finalize_node(state)
    final_text = out["final_response"]

    assert "### 【文化推演】\n卦象平平" in final_text
    assert "### 【现实校验】\n资金不足，建议按兵不动" in final_text
    assert "- 先干着" in final_text
    assert "> *注：玄学参考不可尽信*" in final_text


def test_finalize_rejects_fake_evidence_reference():
    """验证 Finalize 节点能通过 _EVIDENCE_REF_PATTERN 精准抓取越界的伪造 Evidence"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(None, None, None)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试")

    state["reflection"] = DecisionReflection(
        situation_summary="测试情境",
        traditional_reflection="某传统解释。[Evidence 3] 这句话越界了。",
        reality_check="现实条件优先",
        decision_options=["继续收集信息"],
        uncertainty_note="仅作文化反思参考",
    )

    state["citations"] = [Citation(chunk_id="1", document_id="1", source_title="测试来源", section="测试章节")]

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="不存在的 Evidence"):
        runtime.finalize_node(state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieve_node_success_path_mappings(mock_deps):
    """验证 retrieve_node 正确转换 RetrievalResult -> Context & Citations"""
    caster, llm, retrieval_builder = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval_builder)

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试映射")
    state["question"] = rebuild_question(state["question"], status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED, clarified_question="正式的已确认问题")
    state["confirmed"] = True
    state["hexagram"] = create_dummy_hexagram()  # 使用真实的 Dummy Hexagram

    mock_res1 = RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id="chunkA",
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.LIUYAO,
        source_title="Book A",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="S1",
        text="Txt",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=1,
    )
    mock_res2 = RetrievalResult(
        rank=2,
        chunk_id="chunkB",
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.LIUYAO,
        source_title="Book A",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="S2",
        text="Txt",
        rrf_score=0.8,
        rrf_rank=2,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=2,
        sparse_rank=2,
    )
    retrieval_builder.retrieve.return_value = [mock_res1, mock_res2]

    out = await runtime.retrieve_node(state)

    # 验证透传原文
    assert len(out["retrieved_context"]) == 2
    assert out["retrieved_context"][0] == mock_res1

    # 验证 Citation 映射顺序
    assert len(out["citations"]) == 2
    assert out["citations"][0].chunk_id == "chunkA"
    assert out["citations"][1].chunk_id == "chunkB"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_full_runtime_integration(mock_deps):
    """验证整张 LangGraph 的完整串联：Pause -> Resume -> Tool -> Retrieve -> LLM -> Finalize"""
    caster, llm, retrieval_builder = mock_deps

    # --- Mocks ---
    dummy_hex = create_dummy_hexagram()
    caster.cast_random.return_value = dummy_hex  # 注入真实的 HexagramResult

    # 第一次 LLM 返回澄清问题，第二次 LLM 返回解释草稿
    llm.generate_structured.side_effect = [
        ClarificationAssessment(needs_clarification=False, clarified_question="未来一周可否跳槽？", reality_constraints=["房贷"]),
        DecisionReflection(
            situation_summary="现状", traditional_reflection="推演", reality_check="现实", decision_options=["方向A"], uncertainty_note="玄学参考"
        ),
    ]

    retrieval_builder.retrieve.return_value = []  # 模拟未查到资料的边界

    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval_builder)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_e2e_runtime"}}

    # --- Step 1: Execute till Pause ---
    initial_state = create_initial_liuyao_state("该跳槽吗？")
    await graph.ainvoke(initial_state, config)

    paused_state = graph.get_state(config)
    assert paused_state.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert paused_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION

    # --- Step 2: Resume with Confirm ---
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)

    # --- Asserts ---
    assert len(final_state.next) == 0  # 到达 END
    assert final_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert final_state.values["hexagram"] == dummy_hex
    assert "### 【决策参考】" in final_state.values["final_response"]
    assert "- 方向A" in final_state.values["final_response"]

    # Tool and LLM Call count validation
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    assert llm.generate_structured.call_count == 2

    caster = MagicMock(spec=HexagramCastingService)

    # 构造合法的静卦，防止格式化器抛错
    dummy_hex = HexagramResult(
        # 【修复处】：改为 MANUAL_SIX_LINES 即可合法地跳过 coin_tosses 校验
        casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
        lines=tuple(HexagramLine(position=i, value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG, polarity=YinYang.YANG, moving=False) for i in range(1, 7)),
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="否", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="坤"),

    caster.cast_random.return_value = dummy_hex

    llm = AsyncMock()
    # 第一次提问澄清，第二次形成解释草稿

            rrf_score=0.9,
            rrf_rank=1,
            reranker_score=None,
            dense_rank=1,
            sparse_rank=1,
        )
    ]

    return caster, llm, retrieval


class DummyCoinSource:
    """仅用于填充真实 HexagramCastingService 的入参契约"""

    def toss(self) -> CoinFace:
        return CoinFace.HEAD


# =================================================================================
# Stage 6 Acceptance Tests

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A01_pause_before_confirm(mock_deps):
    """1. pause before confirm：第一次 run 停在 confirmation，未确认时 cast=0"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a01_pause"}}

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("我想跳槽")
    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)

    paused_state = graph.get_state(config)
    assert paused_state.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert paused_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A02_confirm_resume(mock_deps):
    """2. confirm/resume：同 thread resume 后 cast=1，并完成 final response"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a02_confirm"}}

    # 1. 停在确认点
    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("我想跳槽"), config)

    # 2. 发起确认
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)

    # 图走到了尽头
    assert len(final_state.next) == 0
    # 状态是最终态
    assert final_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    # 工具被调用刚好 1 次
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    # 输出了最终组合的文本
    final_text = final_state.values["final_response"]
    assert "由于房贷压力，不可裸辞" in final_text
    assert "[Evidence 1] 《增删卜易》 - 断跳槽" in final_text


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A03_modify_invalidates_downstream_and_re_interrupts(mock_deps):
    """
    3. modify/resume 特别关注场景：
       修改内容不丢，旧 hexagram/citations/reflection/final_response 全清；
       形成新 candidate 后仍然 cast=0，直到用户再次 CONFIRM。
    """
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps

    # 准备一个“被历史调用污染过”的满载状态
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("原问题")
    state["question"] = rebuild_question(state["question"], clarified_question="被历史污染的已确认问题", status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED)
    state["confirmed"] = True
    state["hexagram"] = "old_hexagram"
    state["retrieved_context"] = ["old_context"]
    state["citations"] = ["old_citation"]
    state["reflection"] = "old_reflection"
    state["final_response"] = "old_response"

    # [核心断言 1]：Policy 层面上，MODIFY 必须瞬间清空所有下游资产
    response = ConfirmationResponse(action=ConfirmationAction.MODIFY, modification="不测事业了，测感情")
    new_state = apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    assert new_state["confirmed"] is False
    assert new_state["hexagram"] is None
    assert new_state["retrieved_context"] == []
    assert new_state["citations"] == []
    assert new_state["reflection"] is None
    assert new_state["final_response"] is None
    assert new_state["pending_user_input"] == "不测事业了，测感情"

    # [核心断言 2]：Graph 运行时再次推入新一轮
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a03_modify"}}

    # 把清理完的状态放入 Graph 开始新一轮流转 (会触发 clarify_node)
    await graph.ainvoke(new_state, config)
    current_state = graph.get_state(config)

    # 再次挂起在 confirmation
    assert current_state.next == ("confirmation",)

    # 确认 modification 已经被 clarify 消费清空
    assert current_state.values["pending_user_input"] is None

    # 最重要：由于没走过 CONFIRM 节点，Caster 坚决不许碰
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A04_cancel_routes_to_end(mock_deps):
    """4. cancel/resume：END，cast=0，无 downstream"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a04_cancel"}}

    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("测运势"), config)

    # 取消动作
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "cancel"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)
    assert len(final_state.next) == 0
    assert final_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED

    # 彻底断开，没有起卦，没有引文
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
    assert final_state.values["hexagram"] is None
    assert final_state.values["final_response"] is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A05_manual_fixed_fixture(mock_deps):
    """5. manual fixed fixture：固定六爻输入得到稳定本卦/变卦/装卦结构。注入真实的起卦引擎测试全连通。"""
    _, llm, retrieval = mock_deps

    # 使用真实的起卦器！
    adapter = IChingPyAdapter()
    real_caster = HexagramCastingService(adapter, DummyCoinSource())

    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(real_caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a05_manual_fixed"}}

    # 给初始状态强制插入手工指定模式 (7,7,7,7,7,7 为纯乾卦静卦)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试手工模式流转")
    state["casting_method"] = CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    state["manual_lines"] = (
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
    )

    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)

    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)
    hexagram = final_state.values["hexagram"]

    # 验证真实的起卦算法被触发，装卦信息正确留存
    assert hexagram.primary_hexagram.upper_trigram == "乾"
    assert hexagram.moving_line_positions == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A06_projection_and_security_gates(mock_deps):
    """6. projection/security gate：非 confirmed、投影不一致、非法 resume 都不能穿透到 cast"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a06_security"}}

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试越权")
    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)

    # 1. 发送非法的 resume payload -> Confirmation Response Schema 会阻拦并抛出 PolicyError
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "illegal_action"})
    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="无效的六爻确认响应"):
        await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    # 2. 直接构造破损的状态投喂给 cast_node
    broken_state = create_initial_liuyao_state("恶意穿透")
    broken_state["question"] = rebuild_question(
        broken_state["question"], clarified_question="恶意穿透的假问题", status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    )
    broken_state["confirmed"] = False  # 投影不一致！

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="确认状态不一致"):
        runtime.cast_node(broken_state)

    broken_state["question"] = rebuild_question(
        broken_state["question"], clarified_question="恶意穿透的假问题", status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    )
    broken_state["confirmed"] = False

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="未就绪或未通过用户确认"):
        runtime.cast_node(broken_state)

    # 从头到尾，假工具从未被真正调用过
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
    assert caster.cast_manual.call_count == 0

FILE: tests\liuyao\test_workflow.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.types import Command

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import rebuild_question
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinToss,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionReflection,
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)


def _fake_hexagram() -> HexagramResult:
    """构造与 line_value 严格一致的合法 HexagramResult。"""
    lines = tuple(
        HexagramLine(
            position=i,
            value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            polarity=YinYang.YANG,
            moving=False,
            heavenly_stem="甲",
            earthly_branch="子",
            element="水",
            six_relative="父母",
        )
        for i in range(1, 7)
    )
    return HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
        lines=lines,
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="乾", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="乾"),
        changed_hexagram=None,
        moving_line_positions=[],
        coin_tosses=[
            CoinToss(
                position=i,
                coins=("head", "tail", "tail"),
                line_value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            )
            for i in range(1, 7)
        ],
    )


@pytest.fixture
def mock_caster():
    caster = MagicMock()
    caster.cast_random.return_value = _fake_hexagram()
    caster.cast_manual.return_value = _fake_hexagram()
    return caster


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    llm = MagicMock()
    llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(
        return_value=DecisionReflection(
            situation_summary="现状总结",
            traditional_reflection="传统推演",
            reality_check="现实校验",
            decision_options=["参考选项"],
            uncertainty_note="仅供参考",
        )
    )
    return llm


@pytest.fixture
def mock_retrieval():
    retrieval = MagicMock()
    retrieval.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    return retrieval


@pytest.fixture
def runtime(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """共享的 LiuyaoAgentRuntime，携带完整依赖（llm / retrieval 在纯 cast 测试中不触发）"""
    return LiuyaoAgentRuntime(
        casting_service=mock_caster,
        llm_client=mock_llm,
        retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval,
    )


def test_cast_node_guard_blocks_unconfirmed(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """1. 验证 cast_node() 独立防御：即使绕过路由，也会因 can_cast 失败死死防住。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试直接进入 cast")

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="未就绪或未通过用户确认"):
        runtime.cast_node(state)

    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


def test_cast_node_allows_confirmed(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """2. 验证 cast_node() 对完全合法的已确认状态放行，并直接调用 Tool。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("合法进入")

    q = rebuild_question(
        state["question"],
        clarified_question="被用户彻底确认的问题",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    state["question"] = q
    state["confirmed"] = True

    result = runtime.cast_node(state)
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    assert result["hexagram"].casting_method == CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN
    assert result["hexagram"].primary_hexagram.name == "乾"


async def test_workflow_confirmation_interrupt_and_resume_confirm(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """3. 验证 Graph 中断契约及确认后调用起卦服务。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)

    def mock_clarify(state):
        q = rebuild_question(
            state["question"],
            clarified_question="未来一周是否该跳槽？",
            reality_constraints=["薪资要求"],
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )
        return {"question": q, "pending_user_input": None}

    runtime.clarify_node = mock_clarify
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_confirm"}}
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("跳槽？")

    # [首次执行] 触发流程，将被 interrupt() 挂起
    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)
    current_state = await graph.aget_state(config)

    assert current_state.next == ("confirmation",)

    interrupt_payload = current_state.tasks[0].interrupts[0].value
    assert interrupt_payload["type"] == "liuyao_confirmation"
    assert interrupt_payload["question"] == "未来一周是否该跳槽？"
    assert interrupt_payload["reality_constraints"] == ["薪资要求"]
    assert "confirm" in interrupt_payload["allowed_actions"]

    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0

    # [外部恢复]
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    final_state = await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    assert final_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert final_state["hexagram"].primary_hexagram.name == "乾"


async def test_workflow_resume_modify_preserves_user_input_and_routes(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """4. 验证 MODIFY 保留用户修改内容，并在 Clarify 被消费后产生新候选问题重新挂起等待确认。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)

    def mock_clarify(state):
        if state.get("pending_user_input"):
            runtime.test_captured_input = state["pending_user_input"]
            q = rebuild_question(
                state["question"],
                clarified_question="测事业？",
                status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            )
            # LLM 消费完毕，清空
            return {"question": q, "pending_user_input": None}

        q = rebuild_question(
            state["question"],
            clarified_question="初始候选问题",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )
        return {"question": q}

    runtime.clarify_node = mock_clarify
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_modify"}}

    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("测感情"), config)

    # [外部恢复] MODIFY 并带修改意见
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "modify", "modification": "别测感情了，改测事业吧。"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    current_state = await graph.aget_state(config)

    # 验证新问题必须重新挂起确认（不可直接起卦）
    assert current_state.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert current_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert current_state.values["question"].clarified_question == "测事业？"

    # 验证 pending_user_input 在 clarify 节点确实被消费/清空了
    assert current_state.values["pending_user_input"] is None

    # 验证修改后工具仍未调用
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
    assert getattr(runtime, "test_captured_input", None) == "别测感情了，改测事业吧。"


async def test_workflow_resume_cancel_routes_to_end(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """5. 验证 CANCEL 切断流向 END。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)

    def mock_clarify(state):
        q = rebuild_question(
            state["question"],
            clarified_question="初始候选问题",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )
        return {"question": q}

    runtime.clarify_node = mock_clarify
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_cancel"}}

    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("算卦"), config)

    # [外部恢复]
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "cancel"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    current_state = await graph.aget_state(config)
    assert len(current_state.next) == 0
    assert current_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


def test_cast_node_dispatches_manual_cast(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """6. 验证 Task 2 的 Manual Six Lines 正确分发"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试手工起卦")

    state["question"] = rebuild_question(
        state["question"],
        clarified_question="已确认的问题",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    state["confirmed"] = True
    state["casting_method"] = CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    state["manual_lines"] = (
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
    )

    runtime.cast_node(state)

    assert mock_caster.cast_manual.call_count == 1
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
