from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import httpx
import pytest
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, HumanMessage, SystemMessage
from openai import APIStatusError
from pydantic import BaseModel

import guanchen_v2.llm.client
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMEmptyResponseError,
    LLMProviderError,
    LLMRetryExhaustedError,
    LLMStructuredOutputError,
)


class DummySchema(BaseModel):
    field: str


@pytest.fixture
def fake_model():
    return Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)


@pytest.fixture
def llm_client(fake_model):
    return LLMClient(
        model=fake_model,
        max_retries=2,
        retry_wait_exponential_jitter=False,
    )


def test_text_happy_path(llm_client, fake_model):
    fake_model.invoke.return_value = AIMessage(content="ok")

    result = llm_client.text("hello")

    assert result == "ok"
    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 1


def test_non_retryable_provider_failure(llm_client, fake_model):
    mock_request = httpx.Request(
        "POST",
        "https://api.deepseek.com/chat",
    )

    fake_model.invoke.side_effect = APIStatusError(
        message="Bad Request",
        response=httpx.Response(
            400,
            request=mock_request,
        ),
        body=None,
    )

    with pytest.raises(LLMProviderError):
        llm_client.text("hello")

    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 1


def test_empty_structured_output_exhausts_retries(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    structured_mock = Mock()
    structured_mock.invoke.return_value = None

    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    with pytest.raises(LLMEmptyResponseError):
        llm_client.structured(
            "hello",
            DummySchema,
        )

    assert structured_mock.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_structured_parse_failure_exhausts_retries(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    structured_mock = Mock()
    structured_mock.invoke.return_value = {
        "field": "wrong type",
    }

    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    with pytest.raises(LLMStructuredOutputError):
        llm_client.structured(
            "hello",
            DummySchema,
        )

    assert structured_mock.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_transient_provider_failure_exhausts_retries(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    mock_request = httpx.Request(
        "POST",
        "https://api.deepseek.com/chat",
    )

    fake_model.invoke.side_effect = APIStatusError(
        message="Bad Gateway",
        response=httpx.Response(
            502,
            request=mock_request,
        ),
        body=None,
    )

    with pytest.raises(LLMRetryExhaustedError):
        llm_client.text("hello")

    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_from_settings_delegates_to_builder(monkeypatch):
    fake_model = Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)

    mock_build_chat_model = Mock(
        return_value=fake_model,
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.llm.client,
        "build_chat_model",
        mock_build_chat_model,
    )

    mock_settings = Mock()
    mock_settings.llm_max_retries = 3

    client = LLMClient.from_settings(mock_settings)

    mock_build_chat_model.assert_called_once_with(settings=mock_settings)

    assert client._model is fake_model
    assert client._max_retries == 3


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generate_structured_happy_path_preserves_message_roles(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    structured_mock = Mock()
    structured_mock.ainvoke = AsyncMock(return_value=DummySchema(field="ok"))

    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    result = await llm_client.generate_structured(
        system_prompt="SYSTEM RULES",
        user_prompt="USER DATA",
        schema=DummySchema,
    )

    assert result == DummySchema(field="ok")

    fake_model.with_structured_output.assert_called_once_with(DummySchema)

    structured_mock.ainvoke.assert_awaited_once()

    messages = structured_mock.ainvoke.await_args.args[0]

    assert len(messages) == 2

    assert isinstance(messages[0], SystemMessage)
    assert messages[0].content == "SYSTEM RULES"

    assert isinstance(messages[1], HumanMessage)
    assert messages[1].content == "USER DATA"

FILE: tests\llm\test_provider.py

from unittest.mock import Mock

import guanchen_v2.llm.provider
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model


def test_build_chat_model_contract(monkeypatch):
    """
    验证基础模型工厂是否严格遵循 Stage 1 的构造契约。
    重点防御底层重试泄露 (max_retries=0) 和 thinking mode 禁用。
    """
    mock_chat_deepseek = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.llm.provider, "ChatDeepSeek", mock_chat_deepseek)

    # 使用 Mock 对象彻底隔离 Pydantic Settings 对 .env 文件的磁盘读取
    mock_settings = Mock()
    mock_settings.deepseek_model = "deepseek-test"
    # llm_api_key 是 SecretStr，因此我们要 mock 它的 get_secret_value 方法
    mock_settings.deepseek_api_key.get_secret_value.return_value = "sk-fake-key"
    mock_settings.llm_temperature = 0.8
    mock_settings.llm_timeout_seconds = 45.0

    # 调用工厂
    build_chat_model(mock_settings)

    mock_chat_deepseek.assert_called_once()
    call_kwargs = mock_chat_deepseek.call_args.kwargs

    # 验证常规透传
    assert call_kwargs["model"] == "deepseek-test"
    assert call_kwargs["api_key"] == "sk-fake-key"
    assert call_kwargs["temperature"] == 0.8
    assert call_kwargs["timeout"] == 45.0

    # 验证底线契约
    assert call_kwargs["max_retries"] == 0
    assert call_kwargs["extra_body"].get("thinking", {}).get("type") == "disabled"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_birth_profile_repository.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 必测 1：BirthProfile 持久化集成测试（写入 / 读取 / 更正）。

验收点：
- 应用重启后 BirthProfile 仍可恢复（repository 写入 -> 全新 repository 实例读取）；
- 后写的明确更正覆盖当前有效事实，并保留 updated_at（用户更正语义）。
"""
import pytest

from guanchen_v2.memory.repositories import BirthProfileRepository
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_profile_write_read_correct(postgres_session_factory):
    """写入 -> 读取（模拟重启）-> 更正 -> 再次读取，且 updated_at 随更正推进。"""
    repo = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)

    user_id = "user-birth-1"

    # 1. 用户明确提供出生资料
    original = BirthProfile(
        year=1990,
        month=5,
        day=20,
        hour=14,
        minute=30,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="北京",
    )
    await repo.upsert(user_id, original)

    # 2. “应用重启”：完全新的 repository 实例读取（同一数据库）
    repo_after_restart = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)
    loaded = await repo_after_restart.get(user_id)

    assert loaded is not None
    assert loaded.year == 1990
    assert loaded.month == 5
    assert loaded.day == 20
    assert loaded.hour == 14
    assert loaded.minute == 30
    assert loaded.gender == Gender.MALE
    assert loaded.birth_place_text == "北京"

    # 3. 用户更正出生时间（后写覆盖）
    corrected = BirthProfile(
        year=1990,
        month=5,
        day=20,
        hour=16,
        minute=0,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="北京",
    )
    await repo.upsert(user_id, corrected)

    loaded_corrected = await repo.get(user_id)
    assert loaded_corrected is not None
    assert loaded_corrected.hour == 16
    assert loaded_corrected.minute == 0

    # 4. 更正后 updated_at 必须推进（保留 updated_at 语义，行级维护）
    from sqlalchemy import select

    from guanchen_v2.memory.models import BirthProfileRow

    async def fetch_row():
        async with postgres_session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id)
            )
            return result.scalar_one()

    row_after_first = await fetch_row()
    await repo.upsert(user_id, corrected)
    row_after_correction = await fetch_row()

    assert row_after_first.updated_at is not None
    assert row_after_correction.updated_at is not None
    assert row_after_correction.updated_at >= row_after_first.updated_at


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_profile_single_current_fact(postgres_session_factory):
    """单用户只保留一条当前有效事实（唯一约束 + upsert 覆盖）。"""
    repo = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-birth-2"

    first = BirthProfile(
        year=1988, month=1, day=1, hour=8, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE
    )
    second = BirthProfile(
        year=1988, month=1, day=1, hour=10, minute=15, gender=Gender.FEMALE
    )

    await repo.upsert(user_id, first)
    await repo.upsert(user_id, second)

    loaded = await repo.get(user_id)
    assert loaded is not None
    assert (loaded.hour, loaded.minute) == (10, 15)

    from sqlalchemy import func, select

    from guanchen_v2.memory.models import BirthProfileRow

    async with postgres_session_factory() as session:
        count = (await session.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id))).scalar_one()
    assert count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_profile_missing_returns_none(postgres_session_factory):
    """从未提供过出生资料的用户返回 None，而不是异常。"""
    repo = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)
    assert await repo.get("user-never-existed") is None


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_checkpoint_recovery.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 必测 2：LangGraph HITL 跨进程恢复（PostgreSQL checkpointer）。

验收点：
- 六爻流程 interrupt 后模拟进程重建，再用同 thread_id resume；
- LangGraph interrupted workflow 可跨进程 resume（官方 AsyncPostgresSaver）。
"""
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.checkpoint.postgres.aio import AsyncPostgresSaver
from langgraph.types import Command
from psycopg_pool import AsyncConnectionPool

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.casting import HexagramCastingService
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ClarificationAssessment
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.retrieval import LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.memory.checkpointer import to_psycopg_conninfo
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinToss,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionReflection,
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)
from tests.conftest import TEST_DATABASE_URL

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def build_mock_services():
    casting = MagicMock(spec=HexagramCastingService)

    lines = tuple(
        HexagramLine(
            position=i,
            value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            polarity=YinYang.YANG,
            moving=False,
            heavenly_stem="甲",
            earthly_branch="子",
            element="水",
            six_relative="父母",
        )
        for i in range(1, 7)
    )
    mock_hex = HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
        lines=lines,
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="乾", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="乾"),
        changed_hexagram=None,
        moving_line_positions=[],
        coin_tosses=[
            CoinToss(
                position=i,
                # 3+2+2=7 => 少阳（Young Yang），与 line_value 严格一致
                coins=("head", "tail", "tail"),
                line_value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            )
            for i in range(1, 7)
        ],
    )
    casting.cast_random.return_value = mock_hex
    casting.cast_manual.return_value = mock_hex

    llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)

    async def mock_generate_structured(*args, **kwargs):
        schema = kwargs.get("schema")
        if schema == ClarificationAssessment:
            return ClarificationAssessment(
                needs_clarification=False,
                clarified_question="确认要离职吗？",
                reality_constraints=[],
            )
        if schema == DecisionReflection:
            return DecisionReflection(
                situation_summary="情况",
                traditional_reflection="推演",
                reality_check="现实",
                decision_options=["选项"],
                uncertainty_note="免责",
            )
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown schema: {schema}")

    llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(side_effect=mock_generate_structured)

    retrieval = MagicMock(spec=LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder)
    retrieval.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    return casting, llm, retrieval


async def open_saver() -> tuple[AsyncPostgresSaver, AsyncConnectionPool]:
    pool = AsyncConnectionPool(
        conninfo=to_psycopg_conninfo(TEST_DATABASE_URL),
        kwargs={"autocommit": True},
        open=False,
    )
    await pool.open()
    saver = AsyncPostgresSaver(pool)
    await saver.setup()
    return saver, pool


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_hitl_cross_process_resume(db_env):
    """进程 A 挂起 -> 进程彻底重建 -> 进程 B 用同 thread_id resume 完成全流程。"""
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    thread_id = "liuyao_cross_process_resume_1"
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

    # ====== 进程 A：首次执行，停在 confirmation interrupt ======
    saver_a, pool_a = await open_saver()
    runtime_a = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(
        casting_service=casting,
        llm_client=llm,
        retrieval_builder=retrieval,
        checkpointer=saver_a,
    )
    graph_a = runtime_a.build_graph()

    initial = create_initial_liuyao_state("我该不该辞职")
    await graph_a.ainvoke(initial, config)

    state_a = await graph_a.aget_state(config)
    assert state_a.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert state_a.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0  # 确认前禁止起卦

    # 进程 A 关闭（模拟服务彻底重启）
    await pool_a.close()

    # ====== 进程 B：全新 runtime + 全新 saver，同一数据库 / 同一 thread_id ======
    saver_b, pool_b = await open_saver()
    runtime_b = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(
        casting_service=casting,
        llm_client=llm,
        retrieval_builder=retrieval,
        checkpointer=saver_b,
    )
    graph_b = runtime_b.build_graph()

    final_state = await graph_b.ainvoke(
        Command(resume={"action": "confirm"}),
        config,
    )

    # 验证：基于同一个 thread 恢复了图状态并跑完全部节点
    assert final_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert final_state["hexagram"] is not None
    assert final_state["final_response"] is not None
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 1

    # 进程 B 的 saver 能看到同一个 thread（未新建线程）
    threads = [c async for c in saver_b.alist({"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}})]
    assert any(t.config["configurable"]["thread_id"] == thread_id for t in threads)

    await pool_b.close()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_cancel_after_restart(db_env):
    """进程重建后拒绝确认（cancel）也能正确走到 END。"""
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    thread_id = "liuyao_cross_process_cancel_1"
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

    saver_a, pool_a = await open_saver()
    runtime_a = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=saver_a)
    graph_a = runtime_a.build_graph()
    await graph_a.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("要不要投资"), config)
    await pool_a.close()

    saver_b, pool_b = await open_saver()
    runtime_b = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=saver_b)
    graph_b = runtime_b.build_graph()

    final_state = await graph_b.ainvoke(Command(resume={"action": "cancel"}), config)

    assert final_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0  # 取消后绝不触发起卦
    await pool_b.close()

"""Stage 8 域记录持久化：DreamRecord / DecisionRecord 跨进程可恢复。"""
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import DreamRetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.memory.stores import (
    PostgresDecisionRecordStore,
    PostgresDreamRecordStore,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamContextStatus, DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    DecisionQuestion,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionRecord,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def make_decision_record(thread_id: str = "liuyao_thread_x") -> DecisionRecord:
    return DecisionRecord(
        question=DecisionQuestion(
            original_question="要不要换工作",
            clarified_question="今年要不要换工作",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        ),
        thread_id=thread_id,
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        final_response="参考推演",
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_record_round_trip_across_restart(postgres_session_factory):
    """DreamRecord 写入 -> 新 store 实例（模拟重启）读取，内容完整。"""
    user_id = "user-dream-1"
    store_a = PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)

    record = DreamRecord()
    record.raw_descriptions.append("梦见在旧教学楼里迷路")
    record.elements.objects.append("旧教学楼")
    record.elements.emotions.append("焦虑")

    await store_a.save(record)

    # 模拟应用重启：全新 store 实例 + 全新 repository
    store_b = PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)
    recovered = await store_b.load_latest()

    assert recovered is not None
    assert recovered.dream_id == record.dream_id
    assert recovered.raw_descriptions == record.raw_descriptions
    assert recovered.elements.objects == ["旧教学楼"]
    assert recovered.elements.emotions == ["焦虑"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_decision_record_round_trip_across_restart(postgres_session_factory):
    """DecisionRecord 写入 -> 新 store 实例读取（含 thread_id 引用）。"""
    user_id = "user-decision-1"
    store_a = PostgresDecisionRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)

    record = make_decision_record(thread_id="liuyao_thread_recovery")
    await store_a.save(record)

    store_b = PostgresDecisionRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)
    recovered = await store_b.load_latest()

    assert recovered is not None
    assert recovered.record_id == record.record_id
    assert recovered.thread_id == "liuyao_thread_recovery"
    assert recovered.question.clarified_question == "今年要不要换工作"
    assert recovered.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_runtime_restores_record_after_restart(postgres_session_factory):
    """解梦运行时重启后自动恢复当前 DreamRecord 并继续多轮会话（硬门禁）。"""

    class FakeContextService:
        """每轮仅追加描述并进入澄清态（避免触发解释链路，专注验证记忆恢复）。"""

        async def apply_turn(self, user_text, current_record=None):
            record = current_record if current_record is not None else DreamRecord()
            record.raw_descriptions.append(user_text)
            return MagicMock(
                status=DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=record,
                patch=None,
                clarification_question="请补充更多梦境细节",
                started_new_dream=False,
            )

    context_service = FakeContextService()
    retrieval = MagicMock(spec=DreamRetrievalService)
    retrieval.retrieve.return_value = []
    llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)

    user_id = "user-dream-runtime-1"
    store = PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)

    # 进程 A：第一轮
    runtime_a = DreamAgentRuntime(
        context_service=context_service,
        retrieval_service=retrieval,
        llm_client=llm,
        record_store=store,
    )
    result_a = await runtime_a.handle_turn("梦见一条大蛇")
    assert result_a.record.raw_descriptions == ["梦见一条大蛇"]

    # 进程 B：完全新的 runtime（同一 store 绑定同一 user）
    runtime_b = DreamAgentRuntime(
        context_service=context_service,
        retrieval_service=retrieval,
        llm_client=llm,
        record_store=PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory),
    )
    result_b = await runtime_b.handle_turn("蛇变成了龙")

    # 恢复后继续：raw_descriptions 包含上一轮的梦境描述
    assert result_b.record.raw_descriptions == ["梦见一条大蛇", "蛇变成了龙"]


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_inmemory_store.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import (
    MemoryAlreadyExistsError,
    MemoryNotFoundError,
)
from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.inmemory import InMemoryMemoryStore


@pytest.fixture
def store() -> InMemoryMemoryStore:
    return InMemoryMemoryStore()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_1_save_and_load(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 1: 验证 Session 保存与加载，且必须切断 Object Identity"""
    original_state = ConversationState(session_id="session-1")
    await store.save(original_state)
    
    loaded_state = await store.load("session-1")
    
    assert loaded_state is not None
    assert loaded_state.session_id == "session-1"
    # 【核心验证】: 不能是同一个内存对象（模拟 DB 序列化边界）
    assert loaded_state is not original_state 
    # 数据等价
    assert loaded_state.model_dump() == original_state.model_dump()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_2_duplicate_save(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 2: 验证重复 Save 必须失败，保护现有数据"""
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-2")
    await store.save(state)
    
    with pytest.raises(MemoryAlreadyExistsError):
        await store.save(state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_3_update_existing(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 3: 验证存在的 Session 可以被正常 Update"""
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-3", current_query="旧问题")
    await store.save(state)
    
    state.current_query = "新问题"
    await store.update(state)
    
    loaded_state = await store.load("session-3")
    assert loaded_state is not None
    assert loaded_state.current_query == "新问题"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_4_update_missing(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 4: 验证 Update 不存在的 Session 必须抛错"""
    non_exist_state = ConversationState(session_id="missing-4")
    
    with pytest.raises(MemoryNotFoundError):
        await store.update(non_exist_state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_5_delete_idempotent(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 5: 验证删除功能，并确保其具备幂等性 (Idempotent)"""
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-5")
    await store.save(state)
    
    # 第一次删除
    await store.delete("session-5")
    assert await store.load("session-5") is None
    
    # 第二次删除不存在的 ID，必须静默成功，不报错
    await store.delete("session-5") 
    await store.delete("never-existed")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_6_session_isolation(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 6: 验证不同的 Session ID 之间绝对隔离"""
    state_a = ConversationState(session_id="A", current_query="Query A")
    state_b = ConversationState(session_id="B", current_query="Query B")
    
    await store.save(state_a)
    await store.save(state_b)
    
    loaded_a = await store.load("A")
    loaded_b = await store.load("B")
    
    assert loaded_a is not None and loaded_b is not None
    assert loaded_a.current_query == "Query A"
    assert loaded_b.current_query == "Query B"



================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_memory_selector.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 必测 3：Memory Selector 领域隔离测试。

验收点：
- DreamRecord 不出现在 Bazi Agent 的 memory context；
- Memory Selector 不把全历史无脑塞给模型；
- 每个领域只能看到自己命名空间的数据。
"""
import pytest

from guanchen_v2.memory.selector import MemorySelector
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def make_dream_record() -> DreamRecord:
    record = DreamRecord()
    record.raw_descriptions.append("梦见一条大蛇在追我")
    record.elements.people.append("陌生人")
    record.elements.objects.append("大蛇")
    return record


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_record_never_leaks_into_bazi_context(postgres_session_factory):
    """核心隔离断言：梦境数据绝不进入八字 memory context。"""
    selector = MemorySelector(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-isolation-1"

    # 1. 同一个用户同时拥有梦境记录与出生资料
    dream = make_dream_record()
    from guanchen_v2.memory.repositories import DreamRecordRepository

    await DreamRecordRepository(postgres_session_factory).save(user_id, dream)

    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=1995, month=3, day=10, hour=9, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE
    )
    await selector.persist_birth_profile(user_id, profile)

    # 2. 梦境记忆确实存在（持久层可恢复）
    loaded_dream = await selector.load_dream_memory(user_id)
    assert loaded_dream is not None
    assert any("蛇" in obj for obj in loaded_dream.elements.objects)

    # 3. Bazi 的 memory context 只包含出生资料，绝无梦境内容
    bazi_context = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.BAZI, user_id)
    assert RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE in bazi_context.available_data
    assert len(bazi_context.available_data) == 1
    assert RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT not in bazi_context.available_data
    assert "dream" not in bazi_context.available_data


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_selector_does_not_inject_history_for_other_intents(postgres_session_factory):
    """Knowledge / 普通请求不注入任何用户历史记忆。"""
    selector = MemorySelector(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-isolation-2"

    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE
    )
    await selector.persist_birth_profile(user_id, profile)

    knowledge_ctx = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.KNOWLEDGE, user_id)
    assert knowledge_ctx.available_data == {}

    dream_ctx = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.DREAM, user_id)
    # 当前轮梦境内容来自用户本轮输入；历史 DreamRecord 由运行时 record_store 维护
    assert dream_ctx.available_data == {}

    liuyao_ctx = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.LIUYAO, user_id)
    assert liuyao_ctx.available_data == {}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_bazi_memory_prefill_enables_ready_without_reask(postgres_session_factory):
    """八字长期记忆预填：已有出生资料时，Requirement Check 直接 READY（重启后无需再问）。"""
    from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RoutingResult
    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementStatus, check_requirements

    selector = MemorySelector(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-isolation-3"

    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=1990, month=6, day=15, hour=20, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE
    )
    await selector.persist_birth_profile(user_id, profile)

    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
        reason="八字测试",
    )

    context = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.BAZI, user_id)
    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_postgres_memory_store.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.postgres import (
    PostgresMemoryStore,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgres_memory_save_load(
    postgres_session_factory,
    sample_state,
):

    store = PostgresMemoryStore(
        postgres_session_factory
    )

    await store.save(sample_state)

    loaded = await store.load(
        sample_state.session_id
    )

    assert loaded is not None

    assert (
        loaded.session_id
        ==
        sample_state.session_id
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgres_memory_save_existing_raises(
    postgres_session_factory,
    sample_state,
):
    """save 重复 session 必须拒绝覆盖。"""
    from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import MemoryAlreadyExistsError

    store = PostgresMemoryStore(postgres_session_factory)
    await store.save(sample_state)

    with pytest.raises(MemoryAlreadyExistsError):
        await store.save(sample_state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgres_memory_update_and_delete(
    postgres_session_factory,
    sample_state,
):
    """update / delete 生命周期：不存在时 update 必须报错，delete 幂等。"""
    from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import MessageState
    from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import MemoryNotFoundError

    store = PostgresMemoryStore(postgres_session_factory)

    with pytest.raises(MemoryNotFoundError):
        await store.update(sample_state)

    await store.save(sample_state)

    sample_state.messages.append(MessageState(role="user", content="你好"))
    await store.update(sample_state)

    loaded = await store.load(sample_state.session_id)
    assert loaded is not None
    assert len(loaded.messages) == 1

    await store.delete(sample_state.session_id)
    assert await store.load(sample_state.session_id) is None

    # delete 幂等
    await store.delete(sample_state.session_id)

from datetime import datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BaziCalculationMethod,
    BaziChart,
    BirthProfile,
    DaYunPeriod,
    DaYunResult,
    EarthlyBranch,
    FiveElement,
    GanZhi,
    Gender,
    HeavenlyStem,
    HiddenStem,
    LuckDirection,
    LuckStartOffset,
    Pillar,
    TenGod,
)


def test_valid_birth_profile():
    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=2024,
        month=2,
        day=29,
        hour=14,
        minute=30,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="  成都  ",
    )
    assert profile.year == 2024
    assert profile.gender == Gender.MALE
    assert profile.birth_place_text == "成都"  # 验证清洗


def test_birth_profile_empty_place():
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2024, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE, birth_place_text="   ")
    assert profile.birth_place_text is None  # 空白字符串转为 None


def test_impossible_calendar_date():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="非法的日期.*组合"):
        BirthProfile(year=2023, month=2, day=29, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="非法的日期.*组合"):
        BirthProfile(year=2025, month=2, day=30, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)


def test_illegal_calculation_method():
    # 验证严格禁用的排盘规则会被拒绝
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BaziCalculationMethod(
            library_version="1.0",
            late_zi_sect=1,
            yun_sect=1,
            true_solar_time=True,  # Stage 2 锁定禁止
        )


def test_empty_lists_rejected():
    gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem.JIA, branch=EarthlyBranch.ZI)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Pillar(gan_zhi=gz, stem_ten_god=None, hidden_stems=[])  # 必须包含藏干

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DaYunResult(
            direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
            start_offset=LuckStartOffset(years=0, months=0, days=0, hours=0),
            start_at=datetime(2030, 1, 1, 0, 0),
            periods=[],  # 必须包含大运步
        )


def test_derived_fields_and_invariants():
    # 验证甲子干支正确派生出五行为：水、木
    gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem.JIA, branch=EarthlyBranch.ZI)
    assert gz.gan_zhi == "甲子"

    hidden = HiddenStem(stem=HeavenlyStem.GUI, ten_god=TenGod.ZHENG_YIN)
    assert hidden.element == FiveElement.WATER

    pillar = Pillar(gan_zhi=gz, stem_ten_god=None, hidden_stems=[hidden])
    assert pillar.stem_element == FiveElement.WOOD
    assert pillar.branch_element == FiveElement.WATER

    dummy_method = BaziCalculationMethod(library_version="1.0", late_zi_sect=2, yun_sect=1)
    dummy_dayun = DaYunResult(
        direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
        start_offset=LuckStartOffset(years=0, months=0, days=0, hours=0),
        start_at=datetime(2030, 1, 1, 0, 0),
        periods=[DaYunPeriod(index=1, start_year=2025, start_age=1, gan_zhi=gz)],
    )

    chart = BaziChart(
        year_pillar=pillar,
        month_pillar=pillar,
        day_pillar=pillar,
        hour_pillar=pillar,
        da_yun=dummy_dayun,
        calculation_method=dummy_method,
    )
    # 日主自动从日柱天干甲读取
    assert chart.day_master == HeavenlyStem.JIA

FILE: tests\models\test_liuyao_models.py

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionQuestion, DecisionQuestionStatus


def test_decision_question_draft_allows_empty_clarified():
    """DRAFT 和 NEEDS_CLARIFICATION 状态下允许 clarified_question 为空"""
    dq = DecisionQuestion(original_question="我要不要辞职？")
    assert dq.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT
    assert dq.clarified_question is None

    dq.status = DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert dq.clarified_question is None


def test_decision_question_pending_confirmation_requires_clarified():
    """防线测试：如果标记为待确认，决不能拿着一个空问题去问用户"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须存在 clarified_question"):
        DecisionQuestion(
            original_question="我要不要辞职？",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )


def test_decision_question_confirmed_requires_clarified():
    """防线测试：如果标记为已确认，问题实体必须实质存在"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须存在 clarified_question"):
        DecisionQuestion(
            original_question="我要不要辞职？",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        )


def test_decision_question_valid_confirmation():
    """合法的完整确认状态应被 Pydantic 顺利放行"""
    dq = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="我要辞职吗？近期房贷压力大。",
        clarified_question="是否应该在未来三个月内离职？",
        reality_constraints=["近期有房贷压力，不能断供"],
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    assert dq.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert len(dq.reality_constraints) == 1


def test_create_initial_liuyao_state():
    """验证工作流入口必须产出纯净的、未被污染的初始基线状态"""
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("工作太累了，算一卦")

    assert state["question"].original_question == "工作太累了，算一卦"
    assert state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT
    assert state["question"].clarified_question is None

    assert state["clarification"] is None
    assert state["confirmed"] is False
    assert state["hexagram"] is None
    assert state["citations"] == []
    assert state["final_response"] is None


def test_cannot_assign_confirmed_without_clarified():
    """防线测试：严格拦截在实例化之后，被违规赋值为 CONFIRMED 状态"""
    dq = DecisionQuestion(original_question="我要不要辞职？")

    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="必须存在 clarified_question",
    ):
        # 如果 validate_assignment=False，这行代码将无视验证规则强制修改成功
        dq.status = DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED

FILE: tests\router\test_adapters.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.types import Command

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import BaziAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.bazi import BaziAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.dream import DreamAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.knowledge import KnowledgeAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_bazi_adapter_happy_path_and_invariants():
    """验证 Bazi Adapter 准确提取参数并转换为 ReadyForBazi；缺失时 fail-fast"""
    mock_runtime = AsyncMock(spec=BaziAgentRuntime)
    adapter = BaziAgentAdapter(mock_runtime)

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="缺少已声明可用"):
        await adapter.invoke("查事业", RequirementContext())

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="数据类型不合法"):
        context_wrong_type = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "Not A Profile Object"})
        await adapter.invoke("查事业", context_wrong_type)

    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)
    context_valid = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: profile})

    await adapter.invoke("查事业", context_valid)

    mock_runtime.run.assert_awaited_once()
    ready_input = mock_runtime.run.await_args.args[0]
    assert ready_input.question == "查事业"
    assert ready_input.profile == profile


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_adapter_happy_path_and_invariants():
    """验证 Dream Adapter 正确校验和传递纯文本内容；缺失或类型错误时 fail-fast"""
    mock_runtime = AsyncMock(spec=DreamAgentRuntime)
    adapter = DreamAgentAdapter(mock_runtime)

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="缺少已声明可用"):
        await adapter.invoke("解梦", RequirementContext())

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="必须为非空字符串"):
        context_wrong = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: {"not": "a string"}})
        await adapter.invoke("解梦", context_wrong)

    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "梦见一条大蛇"})
    await adapter.invoke("我要解梦", context)

    mock_runtime.handle_turn.assert_awaited_once_with("梦见一条大蛇")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_knowledge_adapter_bypasses_context_entirely():
    """验证 Knowledge Adapter 完全忽略 Context 数据"""
    mock_agent = AsyncMock(spec=KnowledgeAgent)
    adapter = KnowledgeAgentAdapter(mock_agent)

    dirty_context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "八字机密数据"})

    await adapter.invoke("什么是正印", dirty_context)
    mock_agent.answer.assert_awaited_once_with("什么是正印")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_adapter_preserves_resume_handle():
    """验证 Liuyao Adapter 初次唤醒能透出含 thread_id 的句柄，且外部可通过同句柄接力 resume"""
    mock_runtime = MagicMock(spec=LiuyaoAgentRuntime)
    mock_graph = AsyncMock()

    # 定义两次 graph ainvoke 的返回：第一次遇到 interrupt，第二次恢复执行完返回 done
    mock_graph.ainvoke.side_effect = [
        {"__interrupt__": ["pending"]},
        {"final_response": "done"},
    ]
    mock_runtime.build_graph.return_value = mock_graph

    adapter = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(mock_runtime)

    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "我该辞职吗"})

    # 第一次 Invoke 停在打断点，暴露出句柄
    first_result = await adapter.invoke("帮我起一卦", context)

    assert mock_graph.ainvoke.call_count == 1
    assert first_result.thread_id.startswith("liuyao_thread_")

    # 第二次直接基于刚才截获的 thread_id 和外部构造的 payload 进行 resume 接力
    resumed_result = await adapter.resume(first_result.thread_id, {"action": "confirm"})

    assert mock_graph.ainvoke.call_count == 2

    # 精确拦截并断言：第二次 ainvoke 时传入了准确无误的 Command 和同个 Thread ID Config
    resume_command = mock_graph.ainvoke.await_args_list[1].args[0]
    resume_config = mock_graph.ainvoke.await_args_list[1].args[1]

    assert isinstance(resume_command, Command)
    assert resume_command.resume == {"action": "confirm"}
    assert resume_config["configurable"]["thread_id"] == first_result.thread_id

    # 断言确实拿到了完成后的最终状态
    assert resumed_result == {"final_response": "done"}

FILE: tests\router\test_contracts.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import (
    IntentType,
    RequiredDataType,
    RoutingResult,
)


def test_valid_single_domain_bazi():
    """1. 单领域 BAZI -> BIRTH_PROFILE 合法"""
    result = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        confidence=0.9,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
        reason="用户明确要求排八字",
    )
    assert result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert result.secondary_intents == ()


def test_invalid_requirement_for_knowledge():
    """2. KNOWLEDGE + BIRTH_PROFILE 被拒绝。大模型不能凭空给 Knowledge 塞入出生要求"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须严格匹配意图所要求的数据集合"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
            confidence=0.8,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),  # 非法多余
            reason="用户问什么是十神",
        )


def test_valid_dual_domain():
    """3. 显式 contract BAZI + DREAM -> BirthProfile + DreamContent 合法"""
    result = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=0.85,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="用户希望结合八字来解梦",
    )
    assert result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert result.secondary_intents == (IntentType.DREAM,)
    assert set(result.required_data) == {RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT}


def test_invalid_secondary_counts_and_overlap():
    """4. secondary 超过 1 个或与 primary 重复被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Tuple should have at most 1 item"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM, IntentType.LIUYAO),
            confidence=0.8,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT, RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION),
            reason="太多领域了",
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="primary_intent 不能出现在 secondary_intents 中"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.BAZI,),
            confidence=0.9,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            reason="重复领域",
        )


def test_invalid_isolation_mixing():
    """5. CHITCHAT / OUT_OF_SCOPE 与专业 Intent 混搭被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="secondary_intents 不能包含 chitchat"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.CHITCHAT,),
            confidence=0.9,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            reason="不能在八字时闲聊",
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="当 primary_intent 为 out_of_scope 时，secondary_intents 必须为空"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
            confidence=0.9,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
            reason="出界了不能再挂个解梦",
        )


def test_confidence_out_of_bounds():
    """6. confidence 越界被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Input should be less than or equal to 1"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
            confidence=1.1,
            reason="过于自信",
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Input should be greater than or equal to 0"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT,
            confidence=-0.5,
            reason="不够自信",
        )


def test_duplicate_required_data_rejected():
    """7. required_data 存在重复项被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不允许包含重复项"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            confidence=0.8,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE),
            reason="重复提供出生要求",
        )

FILE: tests\router\test_core.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import (
    HighPrecisionRuleRouter,
    RouterDecision,
    TwoLayerIntentRouter,
)


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    llm = AsyncMock()
    # 默认兜底 mock，确保走 LLM 时的基础返回类型
    llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE, confidence=0.8, reason="Mock fallback")
    return llm


@pytest.fixture
def router(mock_llm):
    return TwoLayerIntentRouter(
        rule_router=HighPrecisionRuleRouter(),
        llm_client=mock_llm,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_bazi_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 帮我看看我的八字事业 -> BAZI, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("帮我看看我的八字事业")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_dream_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 昨晚梦到一条蛇，帮我解释一下 -> DREAM, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("昨晚梦到一条蛇，帮我解释一下")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_liuyao_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 帮我起一卦看看要不要离职 -> LIUYAO, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("帮我起一卦看看要不要离职")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.LIUYAO
    assert RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_knowledge_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 正官是什么意思？ -> KNOWLEDGE, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("正官是什么意思？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.KNOWLEDGE
    assert res.required_data == ()  # Knowledge 绝不能请求其他资料
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_chitchat_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 你好 -> CHITCHAT, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("你好")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.CHITCHAT
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_out_of_scope_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 帮我写一段Python代码 -> OOS, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("帮我写一段Python代码")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_llm_fallback_enrichment(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 规则未命中，进入 LLM Fallback 消歧 -> LLM call=1"""
    # 模拟大模型判定为 LIUYAO
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(
        primary_intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, confidence=0.85, reason="用户围绕具体工作选择请求传统占卜式决策参考"
    )

    # 采用真正需要 LLM 语义消歧，且业务意图高度贴合六爻的表达
    res = await router.route("最近有两个工作机会拿不定主意，想用传统占卜方式参考一下")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.LIUYAO
    assert RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_dual_domain_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 显式结合规则 -> Dual Domain, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看看")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert IntentType.DREAM in res.secondary_intents
    # 双重 requirement 自动化拼装
    assert set(res.required_data) == {RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT}
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_mention_does_not_trigger_dual(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 我梦见有人给我算八字 -> LLM Fallback (DREAM), LLM call=1"""
    # 规则无法命中，交给大模型
    # 模拟大模型遵从 Prompt 契约，仅判定为 DREAM，而没有附加 BAZI
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=0.88, reason="提及算命，但本身是一个梦境")

    res = await router.route("昨天睡觉的时候，梦里有个道士帮我排八字，这是啥意思？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert res.secondary_intents == ()  # 大模型只输出了 DREAM

    # Python 代码自动基于 Intent 计算 Requirement，安全可信，DREAM 绝对不含 BirthProfile
    assert res.required_data == (RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,)
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_domain_comparison_is_knowledge(router, mock_llm):
    """验证包含领域词的概念对比请求会被优先分发给 Knowledge"""
    res = await router.route("看看八字和六爻有什么区别？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.KNOWLEDGE
    assert res.secondary_intents == ()
    assert res.required_data == ()
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_personal_dream_meaning_not_knowledge(router, mock_llm):
    """验证带有明确个人暗示特征的解梦不会被 Knowledge 抢走，并能命中增强后的 Dream 规则"""
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(
        primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM,
        confidence=0.9,
        reason="用户请求解释自己的具体梦境",
    )

    res = await router.route("我昨晚梦到蛇是什么意思？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert res.secondary_intents == ()
    # 命中扩充后的 DREAM 规则，不需要耗费一次 LLM 调用
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


def test_router_decision_rejects_non_combinable_secondary():
    """验证 RouterDecision schema 自身能够死守非法组合"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="secondary_intents 只能包含专业领域"):
        RouterDecision(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.CHITCHAT,),
            confidence=0.9,
            reason="非法组合",
        )

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementCheckResult, RequirementContext, RequirementStatus


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: AsyncMock(),
        IntentType.DREAM: AsyncMock(),
        IntentType.KNOWLEDGE: AsyncMock(),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: AsyncMock(),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def dispatcher(mock_adapters):
    return SingleAgentDispatcher(mock_adapters)


def test_dispatcher_rejects_non_professional_registry():
    """Case 1: 注册表禁止注册闲聊与 OOS"""
    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不能包含非专业 Agent"):
        SingleAgentDispatcher({IntentType.CHITCHAT: AsyncMock()})


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_ready_calls_target(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """Case 2: 准备妥当 -> 精准调用 1 次目标，其他不唤起"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,))
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "Dummy"})

    await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "测八字", context)

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 0
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.KNOWLEDGE].invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_needs_input_bypasses_agents(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """Case 3: 缺少资料 (NEEDS_INPUT) -> 所有 Agent 调次数均为 0"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,), reason="测试")
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(
        intent=IntentType.DREAM,
        status=RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
        missing_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
    )

    res = await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "解梦", RequirementContext())

    assert res is req_result  # 返回 orchestration 信息以供前端处理追问
    for adapter in mock_adapters.values():
        assert adapter.invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_chitchat_bypass(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """Case 4: BYPASS (如寒暄) -> 所有 Agent 调用次数均为 0"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT, confidence=1.0, reason="hi")
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT, status=RequirementStatus.BYPASS)

    res = await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "你好", RequirementContext())

    assert res is req_result
    for adapter in mock_adapters.values():
        assert adapter.invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_mismatch_intent_fails(dispatcher):
    """Case 5: 防御脏数据：如果路由结果与核对结果 Intent 发生漂移，立即阻断"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    # 模拟外部意外传入了不匹配的 DREAM 的 CheckResult
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.DREAM, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,))

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="与 RequirementCheckResult intent 不一致"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "问题", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_dual_domain_fails(dispatcher):
    """Case 6: 双领域试图挤名单领域 Dispatcher -> 立刻拒绝"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="测试",
    )
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,))

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不接受双领域"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "问题", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_mismatch_context_fails(dispatcher):
    """验证防伪防线：当 RequirementCheckResult 合法，但它与传来的 Context 的重新计算不符合时报错"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    # 这个 Requirement 是合法的，表明准备就绪
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,))
    # 但实际投递的上下文是空的！
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={})

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="RequirementCheckResult 与当前 RoutingResult / Context 不一致"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "八字", context)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_knowledge_dispatch_never_requires_bazi(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """端到端拦截测试 1：知识查询就算上下文里什么都没有，也只会触发 Knowledge Adapter 而不会牵连八字检查"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(),
        reason="知识问答",
    )
    # 这里也可以故意在 Context 里丢一个假出生数据看看会不会出错，但根据契约，不要求就不会检查
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "干扰数据"})

    # 手动算一遍期待的 Requirement
    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import check_requirements

    req = check_requirements(routing, context)
    assert req.status == RequirementStatus.READY

    await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req, "什么是正官", context)

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.KNOWLEDGE].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_dispatch_never_triggers_others(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """端到端拦截测试 2：解梦请求哪怕满足条件了，也绝不能连累别的 Agent"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,), reason="测试梦")
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "昨晚梦见蛇"})

    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import check_requirements

    req = check_requirements(routing, context)
    assert req.status == RequirementStatus.READY

    await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req, "解梦蛇", context)

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 0
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.LIUYAO].invoke.call_count == 0
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.KNOWLEDGE].invoke.call_count == 0

FILE: tests\router\test_dual_dispatch.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import AgentExecutionResult, DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    def create_adapter(return_val: str):
        adapter = AsyncMock()
        adapter.invoke.return_value = return_val
        return adapter

    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: create_adapter("bazi_result"),
        IntentType.DREAM: create_adapter("dream_result"),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: create_adapter("liuyao_result"),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def dispatcher(mock_adapters):
    return DualDomainDispatcher(mock_adapters)


def test_dual_dispatcher_rejects_non_professional_registry():
    """验证注册表合法性：禁止闲聊进入双领域配置"""
    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不能包含非专业 Agent"):
        DualDomainDispatcher({IntentType.CHITCHAT: AsyncMock()})


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_dispatcher_rejects_single_domain(dispatcher):
    """验证路由契约：坚决拒绝单领域请求挤进双领域 Dispatcher"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="单领域")

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="仅接受双领域请求"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, "测试", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_dispatcher_rejects_missing_adapters(mock_adapters):
    """验证防御机制：如果要求的 Agent 没注册，必须 Fail-fast"""
    # 故意剔除 DREAM adapter
    partial_registry = {IntentType.BAZI: mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI]}
    bad_dispatcher = DualDomainDispatcher(partial_registry)

    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="双领域",
    )

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="缺少处理 dream 的 AgentAdapter"):
        await bad_dispatcher.dispatch(routing, "测试", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_dispatcher_executes_sequentially(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """验证 Happy Path：串行调用，结果被正确包装进 AgentExecutionResult"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="结合八字和梦境",
    )
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile", RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "content"})

    results = await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, "联合分析", context)

    # 验证底层被精确调用
    mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.assert_awaited_once_with("联合分析", context)
    mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.assert_awaited_once_with("联合分析", context)
    # 不相干的 Agent 绝对不会被唤起
    mock_adapters[IntentType.LIUYAO].invoke.assert_not_called()

    # 验证返回契约
    assert len(results) == 2

    assert isinstance(results[0], AgentExecutionResult)
    assert results[0].intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert results[0].output == "bazi_result"

    assert isinstance(results[1], AgentExecutionResult)
    assert results[1].intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert results[1].output == "dream_result"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_requirements.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import (
    RequirementCheckResult,
    RequirementContext,
    RequirementStatus,
    check_requirements,
)


def test_check_requirements_bazi_ready():
    """Case 1: BAZI + BirthProfile available -> READY"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "DummyProfile"})

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


def test_check_requirements_bazi_needs_input():
    """Case 2: BAZI missing BirthProfile -> NEEDS_INPUT"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext()  # 空上下文

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT
    assert result.missing_data == (RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)


def test_check_requirements_knowledge_ready():
    """Case 3: KNOWLEDGE + empty context -> READY (因为知识域不要上下文)"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE, confidence=1.0, required_data=(), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext()

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


def test_check_requirements_bypass():
    """Case 4: CHITCHAT / OOS -> BYPASS"""
    routing1 = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT, confidence=1.0, reason="hi")
    result1 = check_requirements(routing1, RequirementContext())
    assert result1.status == RequirementStatus.BYPASS

    routing2 = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE, confidence=1.0, reason="code")
    result2 = check_requirements(routing2, RequirementContext())
    assert result2.status == RequirementStatus.BYPASS


def test_check_requirements_rejects_dual_domain():
    """Case 5: 双领域直接 Fail-fast 拒绝进入单领域 Checker"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="测试",
    )

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不接受双领域"):
        check_requirements(routing, RequirementContext())


def test_requirement_result_rejects_missing_data_on_ready():
    """验证非法状态构造：READY 不能带有 missing_data"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="READY 状态不能包含 missing_data"):
        RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            status=RequirementStatus.READY,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            missing_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),  # 非法！
        )


def test_requirement_result_rejects_empty_missing_on_needs_input():
    """验证非法状态构造：NEEDS_INPUT 不能没有 missing_data"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="NEEDS_INPUT 必须包含 missing_data"):
        RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.DREAM,
            status=RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
            missing_data=(),  # 非法！
        )


def test_requirement_result_rejects_bypass_for_professional_intent():
    """验证非法状态构造：专业能力不能乱挂 BYPASS"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="专业 Intent 不能使用 BYPASS 状态"):
        RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, status=RequirementStatus.BYPASS, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION,), missing_data=()
        )


def test_liuyao_question_seed_is_enough_to_start():
    """验证：六爻意图只要有最基本的用户问题输入 (Seed) 就可以过 READY 进入它的 workflow，不需要提前确认好"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION,), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "我今年能脱单吗"})

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)
    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


def test_requirement_context_rejects_none_value():
    """验证非法状态构造：available_data 中声称拥有的数据不能是虚假的 None"""
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="不能为 None",
    ):
        RequirementContext(
            available_data={
                RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: None,
            }
        )


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_stage7_router_acceptance.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoInvocationResult
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import HighPrecisionRuleRouter, RouterDecision, TwoLayerIntentRouter
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import AgentExecutionResult, DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_requirements import check_dual_requirements
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext, RequirementStatus
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesisInput
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesizer import AgentSynthesizer


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    def create_adapter(return_val):
        adapter = AsyncMock()
        adapter.invoke.return_value = return_val
        return adapter

    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: create_adapter({"bazi": "done"}),
        IntentType.DREAM: create_adapter({"dream": "done"}),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: create_adapter(LiuyaoInvocationResult(thread_id="test_tid", output="paused")),
        IntentType.KNOWLEDGE: create_adapter("knowledge done"),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    return AsyncMock()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case1_explicit_dual_domain_happy_path(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 1: 真实双领域，成功流转直到合成器"""
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)
    dual_dispatch = DualDomainDispatcher(mock_adapters)
    synthesizer = AgentSynthesizer(mock_llm)

    # 1. 路由
    query = "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看吧"
    routing_result = await router.route(query)
    assert routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert routing_result.secondary_intents == (IntentType.DREAM,)

    # 2. 校验
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile", RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "content"})
    req_result = check_dual_requirements(routing_result, context)
    assert req_result.status == RequirementStatus.READY

    # 3. 分发
    exec_results = await dual_dispatch.dispatch(routing_result, query, context)
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1

    # 4. 汇总
    synthesis_input = SynthesisInput(query=query, results=exec_results)
    from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesizedResponse

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = SynthesizedResponse(content="合并结果", used_intents=())
    await synthesizer.synthesize(synthesis_input)

    # Synthesizer 发起的 LLM 调用合并结论
    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case2_missing_data_rejects_dispatch(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 2: 资料缺失导致中断，绝不盲目派遣 Agent"""
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)

    routing_result = await router.route("结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看吧")

    # Context 中故意漏掉 DreamContent
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile"})
    req_result = check_dual_requirements(routing_result, context)

    # 精准阻断
    assert req_result.status == RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT
    assert req_result.missing_data == (RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,)

    # 确保没有任何 Agent 被实际调用
    for adapter in mock_adapters.values():
        adapter.invoke.assert_not_called()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case3_pseudo_dual_domain_isolated(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 3: 防止伪双领域越权（提及算命，但属于单梦境诉求）"""
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=0.9, reason="仅仅提及八字")
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)
    single_dispatch = SingleAgentDispatcher(mock_adapters)

    query = "昨天睡觉的时候，梦里有个道士帮我排八字，这是啥意思？"
    routing_result = await router.route(query)

    # 坚决不产生双领域
    assert routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert routing_result.secondary_intents == ()

    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "梦见道士排八字"})

    # 因为是单领域，所以可以直接使用 Single Dispatch
    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import check_requirements

    req = check_requirements(routing_result, context)
    await single_dispatch.dispatch(routing_result, req, query, context)

    # 确保没有发生蔓延，Bazi Adapter 完全不知道
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case4_liuyao_synthesizer_preserves_thread_id(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 4: Liuyao 返回结构必须能无损送达，且严格防止 thread_id 泄露给大模型"""
    synthesizer = AgentSynthesizer(mock_llm)

    exec_results = (
        mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke("...", None),
        mock_adapters[IntentType.LIUYAO].invoke("...", None),
    )
    resolved = await __import__("asyncio").gather(*exec_results)

    synthesis_input = SynthesisInput(
        query="结合八字看看要不要换工作",
        results=(
            AgentExecutionResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, output=resolved[0]),
            AgentExecutionResult(intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, output=resolved[1]),
        ),
    )

    from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesizedResponse

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = SynthesizedResponse(content="合并结果", used_intents=())
    await synthesizer.synthesize(synthesis_input)

    synthesizer_call_args = mock_llm.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs
    prompt_sent_to_llm = synthesizer_call_args["user_prompt"]

    # 核心断言：大模型看到的必须是纯业务载荷，绝对不包含内部状态句柄(test_tid)
    assert "[Liuyao Result]" in prompt_sent_to_llm
    assert "test_tid" not in prompt_sent_to_llm  # 防止内存越权泄漏
    assert "paused" in prompt_sent_to_llm


================================================================================
FILE: tests\agents\bazi\test_requirements.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    MissingField,
    NeedUserInput,
    ReadyForBazi,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.requirements import check_bazi_requirements
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender


def test_complete_input_ready_preserves_question():
    """Case 1: 资料齐全，不仅得到 BirthProfile，原问题也一字不落地保留并清洗。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="  测事业  ", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, ReadyForBazi)
    assert result.question == "测事业"
    assert result.profile.year == 1998


def test_missing_time_needs_input_preserves_question():
    """Case 2: 缺时辰时分，触发 NeedUserInput，同时记住用户想问什么。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="运势如何？", year=1998, month=7, day=21, gender=Gender.MALE)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.original_question == "运势如何？"
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.HOUR, MissingField.MINUTE]


def test_missing_gender_needs_input():
    """Case 3: 缺少性别，触发 NeedUserInput。绝对不调用 LLM 去猜。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="测财运", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.GENDER]


def test_multiple_missing_fields_stable_order():
    """Case 4: 大面积缺失资料，确保系统返回的 missing_fields 顺序是稳定的。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="我适合做什么？", year=2000)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [
        MissingField.MONTH,
        MissingField.DAY,
        MissingField.HOUR,
        MissingField.MINUTE,
        MissingField.GENDER,
    ]


def test_invalid_calendar_date_preserves_question():
    """Case 5: 非法的跨字段日期组合，拦截器立刻阻断并报 Invalid。已知错误优先于缺失信息。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="算一卦", year=2023, month=2, day=29)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, InvalidBaziInput)
    assert result.original_question == "算一卦"
    assert "非法的日期组合" in result.reason


def test_empty_question_rejected():
    """Case 6: 最基础的输入 Schema 校验，拦截空白问题。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="question 不能为空或纯空格"):
        BaziAgentInput(question="   ", year=1998, month=1, day=1)


def test_known_single_field_invalid_rejected():
    """Case 7: 单字段自身的硬伤（如 year=0）直接在模型层被拒收，不会流入 NeedUserInput。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BaziAgentInput(question="测事业", year=0, month=1, day=1)
