from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest
from langchain_core.messages import ToolMessage

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.exceptions import (
    BaziAgentCitationError,
    BaziAgentInvariantError,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziInterpretation,
    ReadyForBazi,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import (
    BaziAgentRuntime,
)
from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import (
    LunarPythonBaziAdapter,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BirthProfile,
    Gender,
)
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi_retrieval import (
    BaziKnowledgeArtifact,
)


@pytest.fixture
def ready_input():
    return ReadyForBazi(
        question="事业",
        profile=BirthProfile(
            year=1998,
            month=7,
            day=21,
            hour=14,
            minute=30,
            gender=Gender.MALE,
        ),
    )


def _patch_runtime_dependencies(
    monkeypatch,
    mock_agent,
) -> None:
    import guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "create_agent",
        lambda **kwargs: mock_agent,
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "build_chat_model",
        Mock(),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "build_calculate_bazi_tool",
        Mock(),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool",
        Mock(),
    )


def _create_chart(
    ready_input: ReadyForBazi,
):
    adapter = LunarPythonBaziAdapter()
    return adapter.calculate(ready_input.profile)


def _create_retrieval_result(
    chunk_id: str = "c1",
) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="d1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="T",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=1,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_success_with_calculation_and_retrieval(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    retrieval_result = _create_retrieval_result("c1")

    retrieval_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="txt",
        name="retrieve_bazi_knowledge",
        tool_call_id="2",
        artifact=BaziKnowledgeArtifact(results=[retrieval_result]),
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [
                bazi_tool_message,
                retrieval_tool_message,
            ],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="成功获取解释",
                    used_chunk_ids=["c1"],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    result = await runtime.run(ready_input)

    assert result.answer == "成功获取解释"
    assert result.chart is real_chart

    assert len(result.citations) == 1

    assert result.citations[0].chunk_id == "c1"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_success_with_calculation_only(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    用户只询问确定性排盘事实时，
    Retrieval Tool 可以合法地完全不调用。
    """
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [bazi_tool_message],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer=("你的日主为当前排盘计算结果所示日主。"),
                    used_chunk_ids=[],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    result = await runtime.run(ready_input)

    assert result.chart is real_chart

    assert result.answer == "你的日主为当前排盘计算结果所示日主。"

    assert result.citations == ()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_calculation_only_cannot_fabricate_citation(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    未调用 Retrieval 时，
    structured response 不能凭空声明知识证据。
    """
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [bazi_tool_message],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="带有伪造出处的解释。",
                    used_chunk_ids=["fabricated-chunk"],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        BaziAgentCitationError,
        match="未授权的证据编号",
    ):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_fails_on_zero_calculation_calls(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="没有经过排盘的回答。",
                    used_chunk_ids=[],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        BaziAgentInvariantError,
        match="必须只调用 1 次",
    ):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_fails_when_retrieval_has_no_valid_artifact(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    malformed_retrieval_message = ToolMessage(
        content="txt",
        name="retrieve_bazi_knowledge",
        tool_call_id="2",
        artifact=None,
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [
                bazi_tool_message,
                malformed_retrieval_message,
            ],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="解释。",
                    used_chunk_ids=[],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        BaziAgentInvariantError,
        match=("未返回合法的 BaziKnowledgeArtifact"),
    ):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziBirthDataPatch,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    NeedUserInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.session import BaziTaskSession
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


@pytest.fixture
def mock_runtime():
    runtime = Mock()
    runtime.run = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BaziAgentResult))
    return runtime


async def test_one_turn_completion(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.evaluate(), NeedUserInput)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 0

    res = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(res, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1


async def test_multi_turn_completion_and_question_preservation(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="感情怎样", year=1998), mock_runtime)
    res2 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=7, day=21))
    assert isinstance(res2, NeedUserInput)

    res3 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(res3, BaziAgentResult)

    ready = mock_runtime.run.call_args.args[0]
    assert ready.question == "感情怎样"
    assert ready.profile.year == 1998


async def test_field_override_correction(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=13), mock_runtime)
    await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_args.args[0].profile.hour == 14


async def test_invalid_patch_protects_state(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7), mock_runtime)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=17))
    assert session._current_input.month == 7


async def test_invalid_date_combination_recovery(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="算卦", year=2023, month=2), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(day=29)), InvalidBaziInput)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(year=2024)), NeedUserInput)


async def test_completed_session_does_not_run_runtime_again(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21), mock_runtime)
    result1 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))

    result2 = await session.evaluate()
    assert result2 is result1
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="已完成"):
        await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=15))

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziBirthDataPatch,
    MissingField,
    NeedUserInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.session import BaziTaskSession
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


@pytest.fixture
def mock_runtime():
    runtime = Mock()
    runtime.run = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BaziAgentResult))
    return runtime


async def test_A01_complete_data_ready_for_bazi(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(question="分析事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)
    result = await session.evaluate()
    assert isinstance(result, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1


async def test_A02_missing_time(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(question="看看事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, gender=Gender.MALE)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)
    result = await session.evaluate()
    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.HOUR, MissingField.MINUTE]


async def test_A03_missing_gender(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)
    result = await session.evaluate()
    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)


async def test_A04_multi_turn_repeated_supplement(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="感情", year=1998), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=7, day=21)), NeedUserInput)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14)), NeedUserInput)
    r4 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(r4, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1

FILE: tests\agents\knowledge\test_agent_orchestration.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.models import KnowledgeAnswerDraft
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import (
    KnowledgeAgentError,
    KnowledgeRetrievalError,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import SparseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient


def _create_result(
    chunk_id: str,
    rank: int,
) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=rank,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="TXT",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=rank,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=2,
    )


def _create_llm_mock() -> MagicMock:
    mock_llm = MagicMock(spec_set=LLMClient)
    mock_llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock()
    return mock_llm


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_propagates_filters():
    mock_dense = MagicMock(spec=DenseRetriever)
    mock_sparse = MagicMock(spec=SparseRetriever)

    mock_dense.search = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    mock_sparse.search = MagicMock(return_value=[])

    mock_rrf = MagicMock()
    mock_reranker = MagicMock()
    mock_assembler = MagicMock()

    mock_rrf.fuse.return_value = []

    service = RetrievalService(
        mock_dense,
        mock_sparse,
        mock_rrf,
        mock_reranker,
        mock_assembler,
        candidate_limit=5,
        result_limit=2,
    )

    filters = RetrievalFilter(
        domains=frozenset(
            {
                KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
            }
        )
    )

    await service.retrieve(
        "Query",
        filters=filters,
    )

    mock_dense.search.assert_called_once_with(
        "Query",
        limit=5,
        filters=filters,
    )

    mock_sparse.search.assert_called_once_with(
        "Query",
        limit=5,
        filters=filters,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_limits_safeguards():
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError):
        RetrievalService(
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            candidate_limit=2,
            result_limit=5,
        )

    service = RetrievalService(
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        5,
        2,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        KnowledgeRetrievalError,
        match="不能为空",
    ):
        await service.retrieve("   ")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_empty_evidence_bypasses_llm_and_single_call():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    agent = KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    )

    response = await agent.answer("什么是八字？")

    assert "无法据此作答" in response.answer
    assert response.evidence_sufficient is False
    assert response.citations == ()

    mock_service.retrieve.assert_awaited_once_with(
        query="什么是八字？",
        filters=None,
    )

    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_not_awaited()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_uses_canonical_structured_llm_contract():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法_1",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="基于证据作答。",
        evidence_sufficient=True,
        used_chunk_ids=["合法_1"],
    )

    agent = KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    )

    await agent.answer("查询")

    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()

    kwargs = mock_llm.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs

    assert set(kwargs) == {
        "system_prompt",
        "user_prompt",
        "schema",
    }

    assert kwargs["schema"] is KnowledgeAnswerDraft

    assert "GuanChen Knowledge Agent" in kwargs["system_prompt"]

    assert "=== EVIDENCE START ===" in kwargs["user_prompt"]

    assert "问题：查询" in kwargs["user_prompt"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_zero_citations_discards_llm_answer():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="没有引用证据的回答。",
        evidence_sufficient=False,
        used_chunk_ids=[],
    )

    response = await KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    ).answer("查")

    assert response.answer == "当前知识库中没有检索到足够依据，无法据此作答。"

    assert response.evidence_sufficient is False
    assert response.citations == ()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_insufficient_evidence_enforces_stamp():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法_1",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer=("这里包含部分证据支持的内容。"),
        evidence_sufficient=False,
        used_chunk_ids=["合法_1"],
    )

    response = await KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    ).answer("查")

    assert response.answer.startswith("现有检索证据不足以完整回答该问题。\n\n")

    assert "这里包含部分证据支持的内容。" in (response.answer)

    assert len(response.citations) == 1
    assert response.citations[0].chunk_id == "合法_1"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_llm_fabrication_fails_fast():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法_1",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="存在伪造证据编号。",
        evidence_sufficient=True,
        used_chunk_ids=[
            "合法_1",
            "虚构的_999",
        ],
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        KnowledgeAgentError,
        match="未授权的证据编号",
    ):
        await KnowledgeAgent(
            mock_service,
            mock_llm,
        ).answer("查询")


def test_knowledge_answer_draft_pydantic_invariants():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="必须至少提供一个实际引用的 chunk_id",
    ):
        KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
            answer="回答",
            evidence_sufficient=True,
            used_chunk_ids=[],
        )

    draft = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="证据不足。",
        evidence_sufficient=False,
        used_chunk_ids=[],
    )

    assert draft.evidence_sufficient is False


================================================================================
FILE: tests\core\session\test_session.py

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.core.session.enums import SessionStatus
from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import (
    AgentStateContainer,
    ConversationState,
    MessageState,
    PendingInterrupt,
    SessionMetadata,
)
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementCheckResult, RequirementStatus


def test_1_create_session():
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-xxx")
    assert state.session_id == "session-xxx"
    assert state.status == SessionStatus.ACTIVE
    assert state.metadata.created_at.tzinfo == timezone.utc

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ConversationState(session_id=None)

def test_2_serialization_round_trip():
    state = ConversationState(
        session_id="session-123",
        status=SessionStatus.WAITING_INPUT,
        messages=[
            MessageState(
                role="user", 
                content="帮我分析八字", 
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            )
        ],
        current_query="帮我分析八字",
        metadata=SessionMetadata(
            trace_id="trace-001",
            extra={"runtime_flag": True}
        )
    )

    json_data = state.model_dump_json()
    restored_state = ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_data)

    assert restored_state.session_id == "session-123"
    assert restored_state.status == SessionStatus.WAITING_INPUT
    assert len(restored_state.messages) == 1
    assert restored_state.messages[0].timestamp.tzinfo == timezone.utc
    assert restored_state.metadata.extra["runtime_flag"] is True

def test_3_agent_namespace_isolation():
    """
    Test 3: 验证结构级的 Namespace 隔离。
    ADR 记录：Application层不再审查 Agent 内部字典的键值对。
    """
    state = ConversationState(
        session_id="test",
        agent_states=AgentStateContainer(
            dream={"record": {"id": 1}},
            liuyao={"step": "cast"}
        )
    )
    assert state.agent_states.dream["record"]["id"] == 1
    
    # 验证 Pydantic ConfigDict 拦截野生高层级命名空间
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ConversationState(
            session_id="test",
            agent_states={"unknown_agent": {}}
        )

def test_4_reject_runtime_object():
    class ChatModel:
        pass

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="禁止包含 Runtime Object"):
        ConversationState(
            session_id="test",
            agent_states=AgentStateContainer(
                bazi={"client": ChatModel()}
            )
        )

def test_5_pending_interrupt_recovery():
    interrupt = PendingInterrupt(
        agent="liuyao",
        interrupt_type="confirmation_required",
        payload={"question": "是否确认起卦"},
        created_at=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    state = ConversationState(session_id="test", pending_interrupt=interrupt)
    
    json_str = state.model_dump_json()
    restored = ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_str)
    
    assert restored.pending_interrupt.interrupt_type == "confirmation_required"
    assert restored.pending_interrupt.payload["question"] == "是否确认起卦"
    assert restored.pending_interrupt.created_at.tzinfo == timezone.utc

def test_routing_result_round_trip():
    """Test 8: RoutingResult / RequirementResult serialization"""
    state = ConversationState(
        session_id="test-routing",
        routing_result=RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(),
            confidence=0.95,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            reason="显式请求八字排盘"
        ),
        requirement_result=RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            status=RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            missing_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)
        )
    )

    json_str = state.model_dump_json()
    restored = ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_str)

    assert restored.routing_result is not None
    assert restored.routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert restored.routing_result.required_data == (RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)

    assert restored.requirement_result is not None
    assert restored.requirement_result.status == RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT
    assert restored.requirement_result.missing_data == (RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)



================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_chunker.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeChunkingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


def _create_doc(title: str, text: str) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    return KnowledgeDocument.from_parser(
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title=title,
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="测试章节",
        text=text,
    )


def test_short_document_remains_one_chunk():
    """C1: 长度未越界的段落，必须 Bypass 保持绝对完整"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "100个字符的正文。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    assert len(chunks) == 1
    assert chunks[0].text == "100个字符的正文。"
    # Metadata 全继承
    assert chunks[0].document_id == doc.document_id
    assert chunks[0].source_title == doc.source_title


def test_long_document_splits_with_metadata_inheritance():
    """C2: 长文档切分，且 Metadata 血缘完整继承"""
    text = "A" * 900
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    assert len(chunks) > 1
    for chunk in chunks:
        assert chunk.document_id == doc.document_id
        assert chunk.domain == doc.domain
        assert chunk.section == doc.section


def test_no_cross_document_contamination():
    """C3: 不同 Document 的切片绝不产生交叉感染"""
    doc_a = _create_doc("《书》", "A" * 900)
    doc_b = _create_doc("《测试》", "B" * 900)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    chunks = chunker.chunk_documents([doc_a, doc_b])
    a_chunks = [c for c in chunks if c.source_title == "《书》"]
    b_chunks = [c for c in chunks if c.source_title == "《测试》"]

    for c in a_chunks:
        assert "A" in c.text and "B" not in c.text
    for c in b_chunks:
        assert "B" in c.text and "A" not in c.text


def test_paragraph_aware_preference():
    """C4: 切片偏好验证，必须优先在段落处（双换行）下刀"""
    # 构造两个段落，单段均不到 100，总长超过 100
    text = "第一大段。" + ("A" * 60) + "\n\n第二大段。" + ("B" * 60)
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=100, chunk_overlap=10)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    # 因为在 \n\n 处切了，所以第一个块里绝对不该混入第二大段的内容
    assert "第二大段" not in chunks[0].text
    assert chunks[0].text.startswith("第一大段")


def test_deterministic_chunk_ids():
    """C5: 多次重试，生成的 ID 序列必须是恒定的指纹"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "A" * 900)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    chunks_1 = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    chunks_2 = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    ids_1 = [c.chunk_id for c in chunks_1]
    ids_2 = [c.chunk_id for c in chunks_2]
    assert ids_1 == ids_2


def test_whitespace_preservation_in_chunk_text():
    """C6: Chunk 储存的内容绝不被 Canonicalization 破坏段落和多余空格"""
    text = "第一段。   \n\n  第二段。"
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunk = chunker.chunk_document(doc)[0]

    assert "\n\n" in chunk.text
    assert "第一段。   \n\n  第二段。" == chunk.text


def test_ditiansui_multi_anchor_grouping():
    """C7: 滴天髓多锚点截断组装。不允许混杂！"""
    text = "【经文】A\n【原注】A解\n【经文】B\n【任氏曰】B解"
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    # 长度充裕，应当划分为两块，每块守着自己的锚
    assert len(chunks) == 2
    assert "【经文】A" in chunks[0].text and "【经文】B" not in chunks[0].text
    assert "【经文】B" in chunks[1].text and "【经文】A" not in chunks[1].text


def test_ditiansui_anchor_retained_after_fallback():
    """C8: 极其核心 - 当注解超长时，切断的残肢必须自带经文头部 context"""
    text = "【经文】何知其人富\n【任氏曰】" + ("释义" * 50)
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    # 强行切断
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=50, chunk_overlap=5)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    assert len(chunks) > 1
    # 每一个被迫拆分的碎块，开头都必须带回原经文
    for chunk in chunks:
        assert chunk.text.startswith("【经文】何知其人富")
        assert len(chunk.text) > len("【经文】何知其人富")  # 不会产生 verse-only 孤立块


def test_empty_result_impossible():
    """C9: 输入有内容，绝对不能返回空"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "哪怕只有一个字")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    assert len(chunker.chunk_document(doc)) > 0


def test_invalid_config():
    """C10: 防止程序员乱填切分参数"""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="必须大于 0"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=-1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="不能为负数"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(chunk_overlap=-1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="严格小于"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=100, chunk_overlap=100)


def test_ditiansui_anchor_without_commentary_fails():
    """C11: 发现孤立经文锚点时 Fail Fast，决不静默产生 verse-only chunk"""
    # 只有经文锚点，没有任何后续说明正文
    text = "【经文】此句之后没有任何注解"
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeChunkingError, match="缺失关联释义"):
        chunker.chunk_document(doc)


def test_repeated_split_content_is_stably_deduplicated():
    text = "全新的颠覆性文本二号，旧版全部作废！" * 15

    doc = _create_doc(
        "《书》",
        text,
    )

    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(
        max_chars=50,
        chunk_overlap=5,
    )

    chunks_first = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    chunks_second = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    first_ids = [chunk.chunk_id for chunk in chunks_first]

    second_ids = [chunk.chunk_id for chunk in chunks_second]

    assert len(chunks_first) > 1

    assert len(first_ids) == len(set(first_ids))

    assert first_ids == second_ids


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_ingestion_service.py

from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeTransactionError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeChunkingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.service import KnowledgeIngestionService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    get_engine,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import (
    Base,
    KnowledgeChunkRow,
    KnowledgeDocumentRow,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def setup_database():
    await init_db()

    try:
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            async with get_engine().begin() as conn:
                await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
        finally:
            await dispose_db()


def _create_mock_adapter():
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)

    adapter.model_name = BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME

    adapter.embed_documents.side_effect = lambda chunks: [[0.5] * 512 for _ in chunks]

    return adapter


def _create_test_doc(text: str) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    return KnowledgeDocument.from_parser(
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="卷一 / 测试",
        text=text,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_chunks_fails_fast():
    """S0 异常防线：不能悄无声息地跳过未产生 Chunk 的残破文档。"""
    chunker = MagicMock()
    chunker.chunk_document.return_value = []
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    doc = _create_test_doc("将被 Mock 掉的文本")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeChunkingError, match="未产生任何 Chunk"):
        await service.ingest_document(doc)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_success_happy_path():
    """S1 首次完美摄入验证。确认 Document 和衍生 Chunk 被无损保存。"""
    doc = _create_test_doc("一段非常普通的初版测试文字，用于基础查验。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    await service.ingest_document(doc)

    async with async_session() as session:
        res_doc = await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == doc.document_id))
        assert res_doc.scalar_one().text == "一段非常普通的初版测试文字，用于基础查验。"

        res_chunks = await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id == doc.document_id))
        saved_chunks = res_chunks.scalars().all()
        assert len(saved_chunks) == 1
        assert len(saved_chunks[0].embedding) == 512
        assert saved_chunks[0].embedding_model == BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_identical_repeat_no_extra_rows():
    """S2 完全重复输入测试。必须维持稳定的一行记录和原样 Chunk，严禁行数膨胀！"""
    doc = _create_test_doc("不会变化的千古真理。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    await service.ingest_document(doc)
    await service.ingest_document(doc)

    async with async_session() as session:
        docs = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(docs) == 1
        chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(chunks) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_text_update_replaces_chunks():
    """S3 高级幂等：旧历史必须全被物理除名，绝不留痕。"""
    # 故意设积极短的切割长度，迫使长文本产生大量的碎片 (Chunk)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=50, chunk_overlap=5)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    # 模拟初版入库产生了多个切片
    doc_v1 = _create_test_doc("旧版文本一。" * 20)
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v1)

    async with async_session() as session:
        chunks_v1 = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        old_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in chunks_v1}
        assert len(old_chunk_ids) > 1

    # 同一个 document_id，但文案全部更新
    doc_v2 = _create_test_doc("全新的颠覆性文本二号，旧版全部作废！" * 15)
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v2)

    async with async_session() as session:
        docs = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(docs) == 1
        assert "全新的颠覆性文本二号" in docs[0].text

        chunks_v2 = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        new_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in chunks_v2}

        # 绝大部分旧 ID 必须全部被物理删除，绝不能出现新老交织的混合态
        assert len(new_chunk_ids) > 1
        assert not old_chunk_ids.intersection(new_chunk_ids)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_rollback_on_partial_failure():
    """S4 终极战役：基于 PostgreSQL 底层报错触发的物理级大回滚。"""
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)
    adapter.model_name = BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME

    # 设局：只要碰到含有特定词汇的 Chunk，就向数据库返回 PostgreSQL pgvector 无法忍受的 768 维瞎编向量！
    def fake_embed(chunks):
        if "造反" in chunks[0].text:
            return [[0.5] * 768 for _ in chunks]
        return [[0.5] * 512 for _ in chunks]

    adapter.embed_documents.side_effect = fake_embed
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, adapter)

    # Phase A：初版入库
    doc_v1 = _create_test_doc("绝不容篡改的正确初版。")
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v1)

    async with async_session() as session:
        old_chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        old_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in old_chunks}

    # Phase B：模拟灾难现场
    # 此时：1. UPSERT 已完成; 2. DELETE 旧 Chunk 已完成; 3. INSERT 768维新 Chunk 惨遭 PostgreSQL 拒绝！
    doc_v2 = _create_test_doc("这版文本很正常，但我要在数据写入环节造反。")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeTransactionError, match="知识文档幂等替换失败"):
        await service.ingest_document(doc_v2)

    # Phase C：探查战后现场（验证数据库原子性保障）
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Document 回退如初
        saved_doc = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == doc_v1.document_id))).scalar_one()
        assert saved_doc.text == "绝不容篡改的正确初版。"

        # 那些在事务中曾被 DELETE 的 Chunk 又满血复活了！
        saved_chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id == doc_v1.document_id))).scalars().all()
        saved_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in saved_chunks}
        assert saved_chunk_ids == old_chunk_ids

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeSourceLoadError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.loader import KnowledgeSourceLoader
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


@pytest.fixture
def temp_raw_root(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "raw_verified"
    root.mkdir()
    return root


def test_loader_happy_path(temp_raw_root):
    bazi_dir = temp_raw_root / "bazi"
    bazi_dir.mkdir()

    sample = bazi_dir / "sample.md"

    original_text = "# 《测试经典》\n\n## 第一章\n正文  \t \n\n"

    sample.write_text(
        original_text,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)
    sources = loader.load_all()

    assert len(sources) == 1

    source = sources[0]

    assert source.domain == KnowledgeDomain.BAZI
    assert source.source_title == "《测试经典》"
    assert source.source_type == SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC
    assert source.source_path == "bazi/sample.md"
    assert source.text == original_text

        exc_info.value.__cause__,

    )

        "# B",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)

    assert sources[0].source_path == "bazi/a.md"

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.models import LoadedKnowledgeSource
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.normalizer import KnowledgeSourceNormalizer
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


def test_nfkc_normalization():
    """N1: 测试 NFKC 转换"""
    source = LoadedKnowledgeSource(
        source_path="test.md",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《測試》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        text="正文：ＡＢＣ１２３\n",
    )
    normalizer = KnowledgeSourceNormalizer()
    res = normalizer.normalize(source)
    assert "ABC123" in res.text
    assert "《測試》" in res.source_title


def test_paragraph_preservation():
    """N2: 绝不破坏段落边界"""
    text = "第一段。\n\n第二段。"
    source = LoadedKnowledgeSource(
        source_path="test.md",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《测试》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        text=text,
    )
    normalizer = KnowledgeSourceNormalizer()
    res = normalizer.normalize(source)
    assert res.text == text


def test_metadata_preservation():
    """N3: 保持元数据不变，仅 normalize title 和 text"""
    source = LoadedKnowledgeSource(
        source_path="a/b.md",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.YIJING,
        source_title="《测试》",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        text="内容",
    )
    normalizer = KnowledgeSourceNormalizer()
    res = normalizer.normalize(source)
    assert res.source_path == "a/b.md"
    assert res.domain == KnowledgeDomain.YIJING
    assert res.source_type == SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE

FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_parser.py

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeStructureParseError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.models import NormalizedKnowledgeSource
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.parser import MarkdownKnowledgeParser
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


@pytest.fixture
def markdown_parser():
    return MarkdownKnowledgeParser()


def _create_source(text: str, source_title: str) -> NormalizedKnowledgeSource:
    return NormalizedKnowledgeSource(
        source_path="test.md",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title=source_title,
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        text=text,
    )


def test_h2_only_structure(markdown_parser):
    """P1: 纯 H2 结构完美剥离"""
    text = "# 《测试》\n\n## 第一卦\n乾正文\n\n## 第二卦\n坤正文"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "《测试》"))
    assert len(docs) == 2
    assert docs[0].section == "第一卦"
    assert docs[0].text == "乾正文"
    assert docs[1].section == "第二卦"
    assert docs[1].text == "坤正文"


def test_nested_h2_h3(markdown_parser):
    """P2: 嵌套层级生成正确的 section path，不生成空父级"""
    text = "# 《书》\n\n## 卷一\n\n### 第一章\nA\n\n### 第二章\nB"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "《书》"))
    assert len(docs) == 2
    assert docs[0].section == "卷一 / 第一章"
    assert docs[0].text == "A"
    assert docs[1].section == "卷一 / 第二章"
    assert docs[1].text == "B"


def test_parent_has_own_content(markdown_parser):
    """P3: 父层级有自己的正文时，绝不能丢失它"""
    text = "# 《书》\n\n## 卷一\n\n卷一说明。\n\n### 第一章\n正文。"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "《书》"))
    assert len(docs) == 2
    assert docs[0].section == "卷一"
    assert docs[0].text == "卷一说明。"
    assert docs[1].section == "卷一 / 第一章"
    assert docs[1].text == "正文。"


def test_root_body(markdown_parser):
    """P4: H1 根节点直属正文。必须被保留并挂载到书名下"""
    text = "# 《书》\n\n总说明。\n\n## 第一章\n正文。"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "《书》"))
    assert len(docs) == 2
    assert docs[0].section == "《书》"
    assert docs[0].text == "总说明。"
    assert docs[1].section == "第一章"
    assert docs[1].text == "正文。"


def test_no_cross_section_contamination(markdown_parser):
    """P5: 跨区无污染，下一章不会沾染上一章"""
    text = "# 《书》\n\n## A\n文本A\n## B\n文本B"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "《书》"))
    assert docs[0].text == "文本A"
    assert docs[1].text == "文本B"


def test_heading_jump_fails(markdown_parser):
    """P6: 破坏物理逻辑的结构跳级"""
    text = "# Book\n\n## A\n\n#### B\n内容"
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeStructureParseError, match="结构跳级"):
        markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "Book"))


def test_duplicate_path_fails(markdown_parser):
    """P7: 重复路径被拦截防撞"""
    text = "# Book\n\n## 卷一\n\n### 第一章\nA\n\n### 第一章\nB"
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeStructureParseError, match="重复的 section 路径"):
        markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "Book"))


def test_fenced_heading_ignored(markdown_parser):
    """P8: Fenced 代码块内即使存在标题符号也被无情无视"""
    text = "# Book\n\n## 代码\n```\n# 不是标题\n## 也不算\n```\n结束"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "Book"))
    assert len(docs) == 1
    assert docs[0].section == "代码"
    assert "## 也不算" in docs[0].text


def test_tilde_fenced_heading_ignored(markdown_parser):
    """新增: ~~~ 代码块包裹防御"""
    text = "# Book\n\n## 代码\n~~~\n# 不是标题\n## 也不算\n~~~\n结束"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "Book"))
    assert len(docs) == 1
    assert docs[0].section == "代码"
    assert "## 也不算" in docs[0].text


def test_parser_paragraph_preservation(markdown_parser):
    """新增: Parser 后的 Document 必须完美保留正文段落（空行）边界"""
    text = "# 《测试》\n\n## 第一章\n\n第一段。\n\n第二段。"
    docs = markdown_parser.parse(_create_source(text, "《测试》"))
    assert docs[0].text == "第一段。\n\n第二段。"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_stage4_corpus_smoke.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.loader import KnowledgeSourceLoader
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.normalizer import KnowledgeSourceNormalizer
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.parser import MarkdownKnowledgeParser


def test_corpus_v1_smoke_pipeline():
    """真实语料 Smoke Test: Loader -> Normalizer -> Parser -> Chunker 全链路连通性测试"""
    raw_root = Path("data/knowledge/raw_verified").resolve()

    if not raw_root.exists():
        pytest.skip("本地未发现真实语料库，跳过 Smoke Test。")

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(raw_root)
    normalizer = KnowledgeSourceNormalizer()
    parser = MarkdownKnowledgeParser()
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    sources = loader.load_all()
    assert len(sources) > 0, "未能从 raw_verified 加载到任何 Markdown 知识源"

    total_documents = []
    seen_doc_ids = set()

    for raw_source in sources:
        norm_source = normalizer.normalize(raw_source)
        docs = parser.parse(norm_source)
        assert docs, f"{norm_source.source_path} 未产生任何 KnowledgeDocument"

        # 【此处断言放开】：实际周易不仅有 64 卦，可能还有系辞等结构
        if norm_source.source_path.endswith("zhouyi.md"):
            assert len(docs) >= 64, "《周易》至少应产生 64 个基础 Document"

        for doc in docs:
            assert doc.document_id not in seen_doc_ids, f"发生 document_id 碰撞: {doc.document_id}"
            seen_doc_ids.add(doc.document_id)
            total_documents.append(doc)

    assert len(total_documents) > 0

    # ==============================
    # 终极一跃：将全量 Document 进行切分
    # ==============================
    all_chunks = chunker.chunk_documents(total_documents)

    # 基本数学逻辑：切出来的碎片数量绝对不可能少于原文篇数
    assert len(all_chunks) >= len(total_documents)

    seen_chunk_ids = set()

    # 验证血缘图谱：每一个碎片的 document_id 都一定有源头对应
    for chunk in all_chunks:
        assert chunk.chunk_id, "chunk_id 不能为空"
        assert chunk.document_id, "document_id 不能为空"
        assert chunk.section, "section 不能为空"
        assert chunk.text, "text 不能为空"

        assert chunk.document_id in seen_doc_ids, f"血缘断裂！找到未知来源的 chunk，关联 document_id: {chunk.document_id}"

        assert chunk.chunk_id not in seen_chunk_ids, f"发生 chunk_id 全局碰撞 (重复数据块): {chunk.chunk_id}"
        seen_chunk_ids.add(chunk.chunk_id)

    # 作为本地观察用途打印统计，但不阻断流程
    print(f"\n[Smoke Test Success] 统计: 产生 {len(total_documents)} 份 Documents, 拆分为 {len(all_chunks)} 份 Chunks.")


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\persistence\test_database.py
================================================================================

import os
import subprocess
import sys
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

import guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database as database
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import (
    DatabaseSettings,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import (
    KnowledgePersistenceError,
)


def test_database_settings_are_independent_from_llm():
    settings = DatabaseSettings(
        _env_file=None,
        database_url=("postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/test"),
    )

    assert settings.database_url.endswith("/test")


def test_database_module_import_has_no_llm_config_dependency(
    tmp_path,
):
    """
    在一个没有 DeepSeek credentials 的独立 Python
    进程中 import database 模块必须成功。

    该测试直接锁住 CODE-10 的原始故障。
    """
    env = os.environ.copy()

    env.pop(
        "GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_API_KEY",
        None,
    )

    env.pop(
        "GUANCHEN_DEEPSEEK_MODEL",
        None,
    )

    command = "import guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database as db; assert db._default_runtime is None"

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-c",
            command,
        ],
        cwd=tmp_path,
        env=env,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr or result.stdout


def test_database_runtime_is_created_lazily(
    monkeypatch,
):
    fake_runtime = MagicMock()

    fake_settings = DatabaseSettings(
        _env_file=None,
        database_url=("postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/lazy_test"),
    )

    create_mock = MagicMock(return_value=fake_runtime)

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        database,
        "_default_runtime",
        None,
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        database,
        "create_database_runtime",
        create_mock,
    )

    runtime = database.get_database_runtime(settings=fake_settings)

    assert runtime is fake_runtime

    create_mock.assert_called_once_with(database_url=(fake_settings.database_url))


def test_database_runtime_is_reused(
    monkeypatch,
):
    fake_runtime = MagicMock()

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        database,
        "_default_runtime",
        fake_runtime,
    )

    create_mock = MagicMock()

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        database,
        "create_database_runtime",
        create_mock,
    )

    first = database.get_database_runtime()

    second = database.get_database_runtime()

    assert first is fake_runtime
    assert second is fake_runtime

    create_mock.assert_not_called()


def test_blank_database_url_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(
        KnowledgePersistenceError,
        match="数据库连接地址不能为空",
    ):
        database.DatabaseRuntime(database_url="   ")


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\persistence\test_db_integration.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import (
    DBAPIError,
    IntegrityError,
)

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    get_engine,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import (
    Base,
    KnowledgeChunkRow,
    KnowledgeDocumentRow,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


EMBEDDING_MODEL = "BAAI/bge-small-zh-v1.5"


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def setup_database():
    """
    每个 integration test 使用独立 DB runtime。

    pytest-asyncio 默认给每个 test function 创建独立 event loop，
    因此必须在当前 loop 关闭前 dispose AsyncEngine，
    避免 asyncpg pool connection 被带入下一个 event loop。
    """
    await init_db()

    try:
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            async with get_engine().begin() as conn:
                await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
        finally:
            await dispose_db()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_document_round_trip():
    doc_row = KnowledgeDocumentRow(
        document_id="doc_test_1",
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type="public_domain_classic",
        section="卷一",
        text="天干地支...",
    )

    async with async_session() as session:
        session.add(doc_row)
        await session.commit()

    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == "doc_test_1"))

        fetched = result.scalar_one_or_none()

    assert fetched is not None
    assert fetched.source_title == "《渊海子平》"
    assert fetched.text == "天干地支..."


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chunk_embedding_round_trip_and_fk_lineage():
    """
    验证：
    - Chunk FK lineage
    - vector(512)
    - embedding_model persistence
    """

    isolated_chunk = KnowledgeChunkRow(
        chunk_id="chunk_test_1",
        document_id="doc_test_1",
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type="public_domain_classic",
        section="卷一",
        text="甲乙丙丁",
        embedding=[0.1] * 512,
        embedding_model=EMBEDDING_MODEL,
    )

    async with async_session() as session:
        session.add(isolated_chunk)

        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            await session.commit()

    doc_row = KnowledgeDocumentRow(
        document_id="doc_test_1",
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type="public_domain_classic",
        section="卷一",
        text="天干地支...",
    )

    valid_chunk = KnowledgeChunkRow(
        chunk_id="chunk_test_1",
        document_id="doc_test_1",
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type="public_domain_classic",
        section="卷一",
        text="甲乙丙丁",
        embedding=[0.1] * 512,
        embedding_model=EMBEDDING_MODEL,
    )

    async with async_session() as session:
        session.add(doc_row)
        await session.flush()
        session.add(valid_chunk)
        await session.commit()

    async with async_session() as session:
        result = await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.chunk_id == "chunk_test_1"))

        fetched_chunk = result.scalar_one()

    assert len(fetched_chunk.embedding) == 512

    assert fetched_chunk.embedding_model == EMBEDDING_MODEL


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_database_rejects_wrong_dimension():
    """
    PostgreSQL pgvector 必须拒绝非 512 维向量。
    """

    doc_row = KnowledgeDocumentRow(
        document_id="doc_test_2",
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="《测试》",
        source_type="project_note",
        section="章一",
        text="正文",
    )

    bad_chunk = KnowledgeChunkRow(
        chunk_id="chunk_test_bad_dim",
        document_id="doc_test_2",
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="《测试》",
        source_type="project_note",
        section="章一",
        text="正文",
        embedding=[0.1] * 768,
        embedding_model=EMBEDDING_MODEL,
    )

    async with async_session() as session:
        session.add(doc_row)
        session.add(bad_chunk)

        with pytest.raises(DBAPIError):
            await session.commit()


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\retrieval\test_assembler.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeRetrievalError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    Citation,
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RerankedSearchHit,
    RrfSearchHit,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.assembler import RetrievalResultAssembler


def _create_rrf(chunk_id: str, score: float) -> RrfSearchHit:
    return RrfSearchHit(
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="TXT",
        score=score,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=2,
    )


def _create_reranked(chunk_id: str, reranker_score: float, rrf_rank: int) -> RerankedSearchHit:
    return RerankedSearchHit(
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="TXT",
        score=reranker_score,
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=rrf_rank,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=2,
    )


def test_assembler_from_rrf_preserves_order_and_sets_none():
    """证明 Assembler 不会再自作主张对分数进行排序。"""
    # 故意打乱分数，证明我们只信任位置 (Position)
    hits = [
        _create_rrf("A", 0.1),
        _create_rrf("B", 0.9),
    ]

    results = RetrievalResultAssembler().from_rrf(hits)

    assert len(results) == 2

    assert results[0].chunk_id == "A"
    assert results[0].rank == 1
    assert results[0].rrf_rank == 1
    assert results[0].reranker_score is None  # 完美证明没有开重排

    assert results[1].chunk_id == "B"
    assert results[1].rank == 2


def test_assembler_from_reranked_preserves_old_ranks():
    """证明重排后，旧有的 RRF 名次作为重要探针被原样刻录保留。"""
    hits = [
        _create_reranked("A", reranker_score=9.9, rrf_rank=17),  # 从底层杀上来的黑马
        _create_reranked("B", reranker_score=1.1, rrf_rank=1),  # 被挤下来的前冠军
    ]

    results = RetrievalResultAssembler().from_reranked(hits)

    assert results[0].chunk_id == "A"
    assert results[0].rank == 1  # 当前最终第一名
    assert results[0].rrf_rank == 17  # 探针完美保留

    assert results[1].chunk_id == "B"
    assert results[1].rank == 2
    assert results[1].rrf_rank == 1


def test_assembler_fails_fast_on_duplicates():
    """防伪测试：决不允许两个相同的 chunk_id 出现在同一个最终大名单里。"""
    hits = [_create_rrf("A", 0.9), _create_rrf("A", 0.8)]

    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError, match="发现了重复的 chunk_id"):
        RetrievalResultAssembler().from_rrf(hits)


def test_citation_mapping_is_clean():
    """证明 Citation 不带正文、不带算法分，只带血缘。"""
    hit = _create_rrf("C123", 0.9)
    res = RetrievalResultAssembler().from_rrf([hit])[0]

    citation = Citation.from_retrieval_result(res)

    assert citation.chunk_id == "C123"
    assert citation.document_id == "doc1"
    assert citation.source_title == "T"
    assert citation.section == "S"

    # 彻底杜绝越权获取正文或算法分数
    assert not hasattr(citation, "text")
    assert not hasattr(citation, "score")
    assert not hasattr(citation, "rank")


def test_assembler_empty_lists():
    assert RetrievalResultAssembler().from_rrf([]) == []
    assert RetrievalResultAssembler().from_reranked([]) == []

FILE: tests\knowledge\retrieval\test_dense_integration.py

import math
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.exc import DBAPIError

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeEmbeddingError, KnowledgeRetrievalError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    get_engine,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import (
    Base,
    KnowledgeChunkRow,
    KnowledgeDocumentRow,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def setup_database():
    await init_db()

    try:
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            async with get_engine().begin() as conn:
                await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
        finally:
            await dispose_db()


async def _seed_data(
    session,
    chunk_id: str,
    vector: list[float],
    *,
    embedding_model: str = (BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME),
):
    doc_id = f"doc_{chunk_id}"

    doc = KnowledgeDocumentRow(
        document_id=doc_id,
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="T",
        source_type="project_note",
        section="S",
        text="T",
    )

    chunk = KnowledgeChunkRow(
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id=doc_id,
        domain="bazi",
        source_title="T",
        source_type="project_note",
        section="S",
        text="T",
        embedding=vector,
        embedding_model=embedding_model,
    )

    session.add(doc)
    await session.flush()

    session.add(chunk)


def _mock_adapter_with_query_vector(
    q_vector: list[float],
):
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)

    adapter.model_name = BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME

    adapter.embed_query.return_value = q_vector

    return adapter


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dense_retrieval_cosine_mathematics():
    """
    【绝对核心测试】真实 pgvector 库的余弦相似度运算验证！
    Q = [1, 0, ...],
    A = [1, 0, ...], 理论相似度: 1.0
    B = [√0.5, √0.5, 0...], 理论相似度: ~0.707
    C = [0, 1, ...], 理论相似度: 0.0
    """
    # 构造完美数学几何关系的向量
    q_vec = [0.0] * 512
    q_vec[0] = 1.0

    vec_a = [0.0] * 512
    vec_a[0] = 1.0
    vec_b = [0.0] * 512
    vec_b[0] = math.sqrt(0.5)
    vec_b[1] = math.sqrt(0.5)
    vec_c = [0.0] * 512
    vec_c[1] = 1.0

    async with async_session() as session:
        await _seed_data(session, "chunk_c", vec_c)
        await _seed_data(session, "chunk_a", vec_a)
        await _seed_data(session, "chunk_b", vec_b)
        await session.commit()

    retriever = DenseRetriever(_mock_adapter_with_query_vector(q_vec))
    hits = await retriever.search("哪怕传入天书，Mock 也会返回强行指定的 q_vec", limit=3)

    # 因为 Limit 没超，必须找齐 3 个
    assert len(hits) == 3

    # 排序与相关性必须按余弦几何铁律倒置
    assert hits[0].chunk_id == "chunk_a"
    assert math.isclose(hits[0].score, 1.0, rel_tol=1e-5)

    assert hits[1].chunk_id == "chunk_b"
    assert math.isclose(hits[1].score, 0.707106, rel_tol=1e-5)

    assert hits[2].chunk_id == "chunk_c"
    assert math.isclose(hits[2].score, 0.0, rel_tol=1e-5)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dense_retrieval_limit_contract():
    """验证 Limit 参数的边界拦截与正确截断效果"""
    vec = [0.0] * 512
    vec[0] = 1.0

    async with async_session() as session:
        await _seed_data(session, "c1", vec)
        await _seed_data(session, "c2", vec)
        await _seed_data(session, "c3", vec)
        await session.commit()

    retriever = DenseRetriever(_mock_adapter_with_query_vector(vec))

    # 限制 2 条，哪怕有 3 条完全一样的，也绝不逾越
    hits = await retriever.search("query", limit=2)
    assert len(hits) == 2

    # 输入保护
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError, match="必须大于 0"):
        await retriever.search("query", limit=0)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError, match="必须大于 0"):
        await retriever.search("query", limit=-1)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dense_retrieval_tie_breaking_determinism():
    """验证平局打破机制：当多条内容距离完全相同时，必须由 chunk_id 强制排定次序"""
    vec = [0.0] * 512
    vec[0] = 1.0

    async with async_session() as session:
        # 为了打破偶然因素，我们乱序打入数据库
        await _seed_data(session, "chunk_z", vec)
        await _seed_data(session, "chunk_a", vec)
        await _seed_data(session, "chunk_x", vec)
        await session.commit()

    retriever = DenseRetriever(_mock_adapter_with_query_vector(vec))
    hits = await retriever.search("query", limit=3)

    # 余弦分数理论上必定是完全相等的
    assert hits[0].score == hits[1].score == hits[2].score

    # 但结果绝不允许任何随机漂移，必须严格按 chunk_id 的字典序升序！
    assert hits[0].chunk_id == "chunk_a"
    assert hits[1].chunk_id == "chunk_x"
    assert hits[2].chunk_id == "chunk_z"


# ---------------------------------------------------------
# Mock 隔离测试区 (验证 Error Boundary 原样穿透与底线转换)
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_error_boundary_passes_embedding_errors_raw():
    """确保 Embedding 层炸毁时，原样向上抛出，保留正确的业务领域故障属性"""
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)
    adapter.embed_query.side_effect = KnowledgeEmbeddingError("来自嵌入模型的错误")

    retriever = DenseRetriever(adapter)
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeEmbeddingError, match="来自嵌入模型的错误"):
        await retriever.search("query", limit=5)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_error_boundary_translates_database_crashes():
    """确保数据库炸毁时，彻底拦截并转译为业务侧的 KnowledgeRetrievalError"""
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)
    adapter.embed_query.return_value = [0.1] * 512
    retriever = DenseRetriever(adapter)

    # 我们拦截最高层的 SQLAlchemy execute 语句让它抛一个底层内部异常
    with patch("guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense.async_session") as mock_session_maker:

        class FakeSession:
            async def __aenter__(self):
                return self

            async def __aexit__(self, *args):
                pass

            async def execute(self, stmt):
                raise DBAPIError("SELECT", "params", Exception("DB down"))

        mock_session_maker.return_value = FakeSession()

        with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError, match="知识向量检索失败") as exc_info:
            await retriever.search("query", limit=5)

        assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, DBAPIError)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dense_retrieval_excludes_incompatible_embedding_model():
    vector = [0.0] * 512
    vector[0] = 1.0

    async with async_session() as session:
        await _seed_data(
            session,
            "compatible",
            vector,
            embedding_model=(BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME),
        )

        await _seed_data(
            session,
            "incompatible",
            vector,
            embedding_model=("other/model-with-512-dim"),
        )

        await session.commit()

    retriever = DenseRetriever(_mock_adapter_with_query_vector(vector))

    hits = await retriever.search(
        "query",
        limit=10,
    )

    assert [hit.chunk_id for hit in hits] == ["compatible"]


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\retrieval\test_filters_integration.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, RetrievalFilter, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    get_engine,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import (
    Base,
    KnowledgeChunkRow,
    KnowledgeDocumentRow,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import SparseRetriever

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def setup_database():
    await init_db()

    try:
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            async with get_engine().begin() as conn:
                await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
        finally:
            await dispose_db()


async def _seed_data(session, chunk_id: str, domain: str, source_type: str, text: str, score_tier: float):
    doc_id = f"doc_{chunk_id}"
    doc = KnowledgeDocumentRow(
        document_id=doc_id,
        domain=domain,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=source_type,
        section="S",
        text=text,
    )
    # 为 Dense 控制打分：Query 为 [1, 0...]。
    # score_tier = 1 -> vec=[1, 0...] 距离0，最高分
    # score_tier = 0.5 -> vec=[√0.5, √0.5...] 中等分
    # score_tier = 0 -> vec=[0, 1...] 距离1，最低分
    import math

    if score_tier == 1.0:
        vec = [0.0] * 512
        vec[0] = 1.0
    elif score_tier == 0.5:
        vec = [0.0] * 512
        vec[0] = math.sqrt(0.5)
        vec[1] = math.sqrt(0.5)
    else:
        vec = [0.0] * 512
        vec[1] = 1.0

    chunk = KnowledgeChunkRow(
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id=doc_id,
        domain=domain,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=source_type,
        section="S",
        text=text,
        embedding=vec,
        embedding_model=BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME,
    )
    session.add(doc)
    await session.flush()

    session.add(chunk)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_filter_must_happen_before_limit():
    """【绝对核心】证明过滤器没有把本该排上号的合法文档挤出局。"""
    async with async_session() as session:
        # 六爻的文档靠得极近（极其相关）
        await _seed_data(session, "L1", "liuyao", "project_note", "相关词汇极高 相关词汇", score_tier=1.0)
        await _seed_data(session, "L2", "liuyao", "project_note", "相关词汇极高 相关词汇", score_tier=1.0)
        await _seed_data(session, "L3", "liuyao", "project_note", "相关词汇极高 相关词汇", score_tier=1.0)
        # 八字的文档相较之下距离较远（或命中词较少）
        await _seed_data(session, "B1", "bazi", "project_note", "相关词汇", score_tier=0.5)
        await _seed_data(session, "B2", "bazi", "project_note", "相关词汇", score_tier=0.5)
        await session.commit()

    # 查询：强制只要 八字 (bazi)，且只要 2 条。
    filters = RetrievalFilter(domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.BAZI}))

    # 1. 验证 Dense
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)
    adapter.model_name = BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME
    q_vec = [0.0] * 512
    q_vec[0] = 1.0
    adapter.embed_query.return_value = q_vec
    dense = DenseRetriever(adapter)

    d_hits = await dense.search("...", limit=2, filters=filters)
    # 绝不能是空列表（如果 Limit 在过滤之前，那前三名全是六爻被吃掉，剩0条就是巨大Bug）
    assert len(d_hits) == 2
    assert all(h.domain == KnowledgeDomain.BAZI for h in d_hits)

    # 2. 验证 Sparse
    sparse = SparseRetriever()
    await sparse.rebuild_index()
    s_hits = sparse.search("相关词汇", limit=2, filters=filters)

    assert len(s_hits) == 2
    assert all(h.domain == KnowledgeDomain.BAZI for h in s_hits)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_filter_cross_field_and_same_field_or_semantics():
    """证明跨字段 AND，同字段 OR 的共享契约。"""
    async with async_session() as session:
        # 满足条件：八字 + 经典
        await _seed_data(session, "A", "bazi", "public_domain_classic", "特定文本", 1.0)
        # 满足条件：易经 + 经典
        await _seed_data(session, "B", "yijing", "public_domain_classic", "特定文本", 1.0)
        # 不满足条件：八字 + 个人笔记 (跨字段 AND 失败)
        await _seed_data(session, "C", "bazi", "project_note", "特定文本", 1.0)
        await session.commit()

    # 条件： (bazi OR yijing) AND (classic)
    filters = RetrievalFilter(
        domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.BAZI, KnowledgeDomain.YIJING}),
        source_types=frozenset({SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC}),
    )

    # 因为 Dense/Sparse 都共用这一契约，抽一个查验即可 (以 Sparse 为例)
    sparse = SparseRetriever()
    await sparse.rebuild_index()
    s_hits = sparse.search("特定文本", limit=10, filters=filters)

    assert len(s_hits) == 2
    returned_ids = {h.chunk_id for h in s_hits}
    assert "A" in returned_ids
    assert "B" in returned_ids
    assert "C" not in returned_ids  # C 被铁面无私地拦截了


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_filter_no_match_returns_empty_safely():
    """证明空匹配不会引发崩溃，只是返回空数组。"""
    async with async_session() as session:
        await _seed_data(session, "C1", "bazi", "project_note", "绝密行动", 1.0)
        await session.commit()

    # 故意刁难：找一本根本没入库的书
    filters = RetrievalFilter(source_titles=frozenset({"《神鬼传奇》"}))

    sparse = SparseRetriever()
    await sparse.rebuild_index()
    s_hits = sparse.search("绝密行动", limit=5, filters=filters)
    assert s_hits == []


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\retrieval\test_reranker.py
